# v0.25.0 — The Clockwork Dark overhaul — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The flagship holds together: every one of its eight endings is locked by name through an authored door a player can walk without the narrator (ten doors, fourteen decks, 34 cards), the caravan, tinker and press roll per in-game day on new streams instead of per wall-clock tick, every overlay action that moves, spends or risks anything is an intent the server checks under the turn lock, the cast and map match the lore, the six bugs found while reading are fixed, the UI debts are paid, and the 47-plate art pack is generated and promoted.

**Architecture:**
- **Seven generic engine seams** (spec §6), each paying nothing for a story that does not use it: E1 probabilistic declared world events on the day roll (`engine/world/schedules.py`, `engine/game/clock.py`, `engine/world/world_sim.py`); E2 the overlay intent path (`engine/game/intents.py`, `engine/agents/tool_dispatcher.py`, `engine/scenes/default_state.py`, `engine/scenes/default_scene.py`); E3 the legacy terminal death locking an ending (`engine/game/encounter.py`); E4 two read-only routes (`engine/scenes/default_api.py`); E5 a beat-level `when:` on menu cards (`engine/content/deck.py`, `engine/content/director.py`); E6 the `npc_here` predicate (`engine/world/npc_sim.py`); E7 the `gallery` art kind (`engine/media/art.py`, `engine/media/providers/shipped.py`).
- **Everything else is the flagship's data** under `games/clockwork-dark/`: decks (`data/scenes/`), one clock (`data/rules/clocks.yaml`), one thread (`data/rules/threads.yaml`), two secret places, four declared people, five encounter bands, rewritten ending gates and teases, quest edits.
- **The client** (`ui/src/stories/clockwork-dark/`, a little of `ui/src/core/`) presses overlay actions as `{action, target}` intents read from `/api/intents/overlay`, and draws the encounter, the sheet's faction words, "The ways it ends" and the ending plate.
- **Balance is measured before it moves** (rule 10): T1 measures the legacy event rates before E1 converts them; T10 builds `scripts/simulate_doors.py` and measures every door over 40 seeds before any constant moves.

**Tech Stack:**
- Server: Python 3.11, Flask 3.1 with Flask-SocketIO 5.6, PyYAML (libyaml).
- Python tests: pytest with pytest-xdist and pytest-timeout, run through `scripts/run_tests.py`.
- Client: React 18, Vite 5, vitest 2 with jsdom, playwright-core for the layout check.
- Client tests follow the repo's convention: `// @vitest-environment jsdom` on line 1, `createRoot` plus `React.act`, the helpers in `ui/tests/helpers/dom.js` (`mount`, `click`, `key`, `button`, `byTestId`, `byText`). **No `@testing-library/react`.**
- Art: `engine/media/providers/xai.py` (`grok-imagine-image-2.0`) through `scripts/generate_art.py`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-27-v0.25.0-clockwork-dark-overhaul-design.md` (written, reviewed and revised 2026-10-27; its "Revision 1" section maps the review's 29 findings). The review: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-27-v0.25.0-clockwork-dark-overhaul-design.REVIEW.md`. Section numbers (§N) and task numbers (T0-T17, C1) below are the spec's. Where this plan and the spec disagree, the spec wins unless a **Plan decision** below says why not; every Plan decision is a gap or a disagreement with the code that the spec did not rule on.

---

## Global Constraints

Each line is copied from the spec ("spec"), from the owner's rulings for this release ("owner"), or is the controller's standing rule ("standing"). Every task's requirements include this whole section.

**Rule 12 (spec, preamble):**
- (spec) "Nothing here rates content. No intensity, no ceiling, no fade, no `safety:` block, no register line in any prompt. The register of `games/clockwork-dark/prompts/storyteller.md` is the owner's and the loaded model's, and this design edits that prompt only to name the cast and the places (§3)."
- (standing) AGENTS.md rule 12 in full. Commit bodies describe engine SCOPE, never a content-policy decision.

**Canon (spec, preamble):**
- (spec) "Nothing in AGENTS.md's canon list is renamed or removed ... The eight ending ids, the 25 quest ids, the five canon NPC ids and every item id stay too (saves and the canon dictionary name them). Everything below is additive or an edit inside an existing id."
- (spec §11.1) "No state moves and `CURRENT_SAVE_VERSION` stays 2". Quest and stage ids and every narrative flag's id are unchanged.

**What the release may not move (spec):**
- (spec, Non-goals) "**No change to any other story's turns.** Byte-identity is asserted (§11.3)." `WorldSim.on_tick`'s eager draws on the three legacy streams and `ScheduleRoll.check_*` are untouched.
- (spec, Non-goals) "**No change to the narrator's grammar for any story.** The new overlay-only verbs (§7) never enter `legal_intents`, and the overlay's uncapped lists are built beside the capped catalogue, not into it".
- (spec §11.2) "`tests/test_llm_golden_lmstudio.py` replays unchanged ... **A moved recording is a design failure to fix by gating, never a reason to re-record**". "`tests/test_local_mode_golden.py` ... each recording the overhaul moves ... carries its own named `json_sanction`".
- (spec, Non-goals) "**The doom clock is not redesigned.** `evil_progress`, the four phases, the base rate (0.028), `doom_resistance`, the engagement factor and the five doom beats stay as they are."
- (spec, Non-goals) "**The companion is unchanged.**" "**No new archetypes, skills, factions or recipes**, no New Game+ ... and no sound cue beyond what v0.24.0 shipped."
- (spec §6) Each seam is "generic (no flagship noun in engine code, `tests/test_no_flagship_nouns_in_engine.py`), each with a production caller (`tests/test_reachability.py`, no new allowlist row)".

**Rule 1 and the overlay (spec §7):**
- (spec §7.1) "A refusal before the turn ... answers `turn_error {message, busy: false, stale_choice: true}`: nothing ran, the save is unchanged". "A scene owns the turn ... refused with the engine's words, "That will have to wait: what is in front of you comes first." The refusal is the turn's receipt, so it reaches the prose (rule 1)". "Typed text still carries no intent. `resolve_player_intent` is unchanged."
- (spec §7.2) `OVERLAY_ACTIONS = {buy, sell, eat, use, equip, unequip, craft, work}`.

**Doors (spec §4.1):**
- (spec) "No id-less lock remains in the story (asserted, §12.2)." "Every deck in this story is `draw: 1`, all its cards in the pool." "Every beat stays inside four effects."
- (spec) "Hours are half-open everywhere in this file: `[a, b]` is `hour_between: [a, b]`, a through b−1 (`quests.py:566`)." (Plan decision 2 rules on the cast table, which cannot be read that way.)

**Balance (spec §10, rule 10; owner):**
- (spec §10) "**Nothing below is a decision; every number is a first value, unmeasured, until T10 measures it.** The order is fixed: build, measure, write the table into the CHANGELOG and the files' headers, and only then move a constant, naming the lever and re-measuring."
- (owner) T1 measures the background-tick event rates BEFORE the conversion. (spec §6.1) "The per-day probabilities are then set to the measured mean across the five policies".
- (owner) The run-length bands stay as the spec has them (§10.2).

**Art (owner, 2026-10-27):**
- (owner) Approved: 47 plates through the `xai` provider, run with the owner's key at `xai_api_key.txt` (gitignored). **Never print, log, echo, `cat` or commit that key**; the provider reads it itself.

**Live play (owner):**
- (owner) T16 is owner-gated. The controller asks the owner for the model when it gets there; if the owner declines, T16 is recorded as deferred (CLAUDE.md's deferred list) and T17 proceeds.

**Test cost (owner):**
- (owner) Each task runs only its affected files through `scripts/run_tests.py files`.
- (owner) The full suite runs ONCE, at T17, as `scripts/run_tests.py full --limit-minutes 240`.
- (owner) `tests/test_social_save_size.py`, `tests/test_simulate_social.py` and `tests/test_social_commit_scope.py` are not touched by this release and run only in T17's full run. No task names them in a `files` run.

**Standing rules (the controller's, every task):**
- **No live model in a test.** `_no_live_model_calls` stays default-deny. Only T16 runs live, under `@pytest.mark.live`, and never loads or unloads a model.
- **Agents never run engine code by hand**, except the simulator scripts a balance or measurement task exists to run (T1's `scripts/simulate.py --event-rates`, T10's `scripts/simulate_doors.py` and `scripts/simulate.py`) and T15's `scripts/generate_art.py`. Everything else runs as a test through `scripts/run_tests.py`.
- **Background servers** start with `run_in_background` (or `--exit-with-parent` under it), never a shell `&`, and are stopped with `TaskStop`.
- **Temp directories are deleted only by a literal, printed path** (never `rm -rf "$(...)"`, never a variable). A git worktree is removed with `git worktree remove <literal path>`.
- **Never touch the owner's files or services:** not `config/local.yaml`, the real `data/`, LM Studio's `mcp.json`. Tests use `tmp_path`, `CLOCKWORK_DATA_DIR`, `tests/hosted_app.py` and a patched `engine.config._CONFIG_DIR`.
- **AGENTS.md's twelve rules bind every task**, in particular rule 2 (`clock.advance_time` is the only writer of world time), rule 3 (`effects.apply_effect` the only writer of state), rule 4 (named streams, never bare `random`), rule 5 (config, nothing hardcoded), rule 9 (NOT WIRED rows with files), rule 11 (`pathlib`, no libm value reaching state: `engine/game/detmath.py`) and rule 12.
- **How tests run.** A task runs only what it touches, plus the guards:
  - PowerShell: `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\run_tests.py files <paths> <GUARDS>`.
  - **`<GUARDS>`, the stories-wide guards, ALWAYS this exact list:** `tests/test_story_content_integrity.py tests/test_presence_every_story.py tests/test_story_surface.py tests/test_new_story.py tests/test_cache_warming.py tests/test_cache_reset_race.py tests/test_module_state_inventory.py tests/test_reachability.py tests/test_social_seam.py tests/test_release_hygiene.py tests/test_finales.py tests/test_no_story_literals_in_core_ui.py tests/test_ui_contract.py tests/test_no_flagship_nouns_in_engine.py`.
  - **`<GOLDENS>`** is `tests/test_local_mode_golden.py tests/test_llm_golden_lmstudio.py tests/test_thread_safety.py`, added by every task that touches a shared engine file, a route, a payload or the flagship's content.
  - **`<ISOLATION>`** is `tests/test_flagship_overhaul_isolation.py` (T0), added by every task from T1 on that touches engine code: it holds the other six stories to their v0.24.0 recordings.
  - **A task that moves a payload** also runs `tests/test_admin_no_play_text.py`.
  - **Content validity** is `tests/test_story_content_integrity.py` (in `<GUARDS>`), which runs `validate_story` over every story: no task runs `scripts/validate_content.py` or `scripts/doctor.py` by hand. `doctor.py` runs by hand only at T13 and T17, on the controller's word.
  - **A layout check** starts the server as `scripts/screenshot_runs.py --serve clockwork-dark --root <a fresh directory under the scratchpad> --exit-with-parent` with `CLOCKWORK_DATA_DIR` pointing into that directory, as a background task, and stops it with `TaskStop`.
  - `npm test --prefix ui` whenever `ui/src` or `ui/tests` changes.
- **The client build is committed.** Any `ui/src` change runs `npm run build --prefix ui` and commits `content/scenes/clockwork/static/dist` IN THE SAME COMMIT (`dist/build-source.txt` hashes every `ui/src` file).
- **Docs move with the change** (AGENTS.md), in the same commit, each one reviewed, not appended to: `games/clockwork-dark/CHANGELOG.md` `## [Unreleased]` and `README.md`; the root `CHANGELOG.md` `## [Unreleased]` and `README.md` for engine changes; `docs/AUTHORING.md`; `docs/GOVERNANCE.md` (NOT WIRED rows); `docs/DESIGN.md`; `CLAUDE.md` when the status, roadmap or deferred list moves.
- **Module state is classified** in the same task: `tests/fixtures/module_state.yaml`, `engine/games/caches.py` `WARMERS` and `LRU_CACHES`. A new module lock joins `engine/locks.py`'s order.
- **Git.** The branch is `release/v0.25.0`. Write each commit message with the editor tool to `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-27-v0.25.0-clockwork-dark/commit-t<N>-msg.txt` (gitignored) and commit with `git commit -F <file>`; heredocs lose backticks here. Every message ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. **Nothing is pushed, tagged or merged to `main`** by a task: T17 stops on the release branch.
- **Tests, the discipline.** A fix ships with a test that FAILED before it (each fix step below says how it fails). A guard is canary-checked by reintroducing what it guards and watching it fail, then restoring. Mark a test `process`, `loopback` or `slow` as AGENTS.md says. Force every draw a test depends on.

## Review Focus

The five input classes the spec implies but no spec'd test exercises, most likely first. Each has its test in the owning task (named here; the test code is in that task).

1. **A door's person leaves while its card is open** (Maris goes up to bed at 10 with `H1` still in hand). A player expects the card in front of them to stay answerable and to lock what it says. T6: `test_a_dealt_door_stays_answerable_after_its_person_leaves`.
2. **An overlay pressed from a list one turn old** (a turn arrived, the overlay's refetch has not landed, the coin is already spent). A player expects a plain refusal in words, the save untouched, and the overlay to redraw. T2 (server: `test_an_overlay_pair_stale_before_the_turn_is_refused_with_nothing_run`) and T11 (client: `refetches its list when a turn arrives and after a stale refusal`).
3. **Two overlay presses back to back** (a double-click on "Buy"). A player expects one purchase. T2: `test_a_second_overlay_press_while_a_turn_runs_is_busy`.
4. **The world's last hour arrives while a hand or an encounter is open.** A player expects to finish what is in front of them and then be dealt the last morning. T7: `test_the_last_morning_waits_for_an_open_hand_then_is_dealt`.
5. **A v0.24.0 save holding a legacy-shaped `caravan_arrival`** (its payload carries `goods` and `npc_id`, no `declared`). A player expects Odran still in the square, no second caravan until it expires, and nothing to crash. T9: `test_save_2_keeps_its_caravan_and_odran`.

---

## Plan decisions

Places where the code disagrees with the spec or the spec is silent. Each is binding unless the spec is revised.

1. **The doom beats' `npc_moves` move nobody today, and the spec's cast relies on them** (spec §3.3, §4.2, §6.6). `engine/game/clocks.py::_apply_npc_moves` writes the PROCGEN row's `location_id`, but `npc_sim.resolve_npc` places a person by their routine slot first, and every canon NPC has a 24-hour routine, so Ilya never reaches the square at 0.50 nor Sera at 0.88; and an NPC not in `state.procgen.npcs` (Edrin, Nessa: declared only in `npc_schedules.yaml`, §3.3) is skipped with a warning. **Ruling (T4):** `_apply_npc_moves` also writes the soft override flag `npc_at_<id>` = destination (npc_sim's existing "state override" rank: above a routine, below an event and a pin), and accepts an NPC known to the procgen list OR the schedule file. Only the flagship declares `npc_moves` anywhere (`games/*/data` searched), so no other story moves. A v0.24.0 save already past 0.50 or 0.88 fired those beats before the override existed and keeps its routines: recorded as a deferred row in T13, no migration (§11.1).
2. **The cast table's hour ranges cannot all be half-open** (spec §3.3 says they are). Read half-open, Hallam leaves 05, 18 and 20 uncovered, Nessa 05, 08, 16 and 20, Wenna 07 and 21-ish; read inclusive, Edrin overlaps at 05 and 20. **Ruling:** each person covers exactly 24 hours with no overlap: Hallam, Nessa and Wenna are read INCLUSIVE and Edrin half-open, as written. The exact hour lists are T4's YAML. The door decks then read Wenna at the shrine in hours 6, 7, 18, 19, 20 and 21.
3. **A deck whose `when:` holds while none of its cards can be dealt is spent with a failed receipt** (`director.begin`: "no cards were eligible", the deck marked played until its `when:` is seen false). The spec writes each deck's `when:` as its place, hour and person only, so the bakery at 05:00 with Maris and no quest open would spend `the_morning_firing` and hand the narrator a failure line. **Ruling:** every flagship deck that has a `when:` is written `all: [<the spec's place/hour/person clauses>, {any: [<each card's own when:, verbatim, in card order>]}]`, so it is due only when a card can be dealt. `tests/test_clockwork_dark.py::test_every_deck_is_due_only_when_a_card_can_be_dealt` holds the shape (T8).
4. **`the_press` would schedule itself** (`director.due`: "A deck's `when:` ALSO SCHEDULES IT ON ITS OWN"), so with the spec's `when:` it would be dealt on any day the player stands in the square from 8 to 18. **Ruling:** its `when:` gains `{forced_scene: the_press}` (the clock grammar's existing predicate), so it is dealt only while the levy's beat owes it.
5. **A menu card resolved with no choice takes its FIRST beat** (`deck.chosen_beats`), which E5 can filter out (`H1`'s `stay` while the Hearth is shut). **Ruling (T3):** `director.resolve` with an empty `chosen` on a menu card that has at least one beat carrying `when:` substitutes the first OFFERED beat's id. A card with no beat-level `when:` (every other story's) takes exactly the path it takes today.
6. **`the_price_asked`'s three gate clauses cannot be unmet at `S2`** (the quest's own preconditions give `named`; the stage gives `chose`; the beat writes `paid`). **Ruling (T9):** its unearned rows walk the door where the DECK or the CARD refuses: one row with no sacrifice open (the deck's `when:` fails; clauses `named`, `chose`, `paid`), one at `choose_what` with nothing offered (`S2` is not eligible; clauses `chose`, `paid`). Every clause is covered; no `unfailable` entry is added for the flagship.
7. **The flagship's card door for The Stopped Wheel is a sequence card**, so its row is `Door.card("the_stopped_wheel", "Z1_the_last_morning")` with no beat (spec §4.2's "resolve" is the director's option id for a sequence card, not a beat id). `Z1` carries `when: {ending: {eligible: the_stopped_wheel}}` like every door.
8. **The flagship's stub-run catalogue cannot stay pinned after T4**: the four new people stand in the square, and the turn schema's `npc_id` enum lists who is present. **Ruling:** the permanent isolation test holds the flagship's `legal_intents` and turn schema at its OPENINGS (seeds 5 and 7, the LM Studio golden's, and 1234) only; T2 alone also compares the flagship's 20-turn stub run against T0 (the builder split moved no catalogue), before T4 lands.
9. **T0 records by test, not by hand.** The recorders are test functions that write fixtures only under `CLOCKWORK_FLAGSHIP_RECORD=1`, run through `scripts/run_tests.py files` inside a worktree at `3c6882f`.
10. **The overlay's sentence.** The turn an overlay press runs is handed to the narrator exactly as a chip is: `f"The player chooses: {describe_overlay_intent(...)}"`.
11. **A press while a turn holds the session lock** is answered `turn_error {message: "A turn is already in progress.", busy: true}` before the pre-check (which reads state off the lock and would race the running turn), the words `run_guarded` already uses.
12. **The two routes join `engine/scenes/default_api.py`'s blueprint** but NOT `tests/local_golden.py::STORY_ROUTES` (that tuple feeds a recording; adding to it would move `story_routes` for no reason). `tests/test_hosting_ownership.py` names them explicitly.
13. **`scripts/generate_art.py --game clockwork-dark` is the flagship's legacy plan** (cache only, items promoted), pinned by `tests/test_generate_art_cli.py`. **Ruling (T15):** a new `--story-plan` flag routes the flagship through `story_main` (the generic plan every other story uses), whose `SECTIONS` gain `enemies` and `gallery`, and whose location subjects gain an opt-in `phases: [consuming]` key that plans one `corrupted` plate. Without the flag every line printed is v0.24.0's.
14. **The doors harness lives beside the policies it reuses.** `scripts/simulate_doors.py` imports `scripts/simulate.py`'s `Simulation`, `route` and upkeep, and `simulate.py --policy doors` dispatches to its `main`.

### Task 0: Recordings from v0.24.0 (§11.3, §12.4, §12.5) — M

Every later byte-identity and save test compares against what THIS task records from the v0.24.0 tree. The recorders are tests (Plan decision 9), run once inside a worktree at `3c6882f`; on this branch the same tests then compare, and pass, because no engine code has moved yet.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/flagship_overhaul_recorder.py` (the readers, shared by the recorder and the comparisons)
- Create: `tests/test_flagship_overhaul_isolation.py` (recorder + comparisons; `<ISOLATION>` from T1 on)
- Create: `tests/test_clockwork_dark_saves.py` (the nine-save recorder + a load smoke test; T9 adds the assertions)
- Create (recorded): `tests/fixtures/flagship_overhaul/v0240.json`, `tests/fixtures/clockwork_dark_v0240/<nine>.json`
- Docs: `CHANGELOG.md` `[Unreleased]` (a "Tests" line: the v0.24.0 recordings and how they were made)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `tests.flagship_overhaul_recorder` — `FIXTURE: Path`, `FLAGSHIP`, `OTHER_SIX: tuple[str, ...]`, `SEVEN`, `FLAGSHIP_GOLDEN_SEEDS = (5, 7)`, `OPENING_SEED = 1234`, `LEGACY_SLOT_STORIES = ("wicked-garden", "dev-story")`, `sha(value) -> str`, `active(slug)` (context manager), `catalogue(state) -> dict[str, str]`, `openings(slug, seeds) -> dict[str, dict[str, str]]`, `bound_beats(slug) -> dict[str, str]`, `stub_run(slug) -> dict`, `legacy_rolls(slug) -> dict`, `hue_endings() -> str`, `record_all() -> dict`.
- Produces: `tests.test_clockwork_dark_saves` — `SAVES: Path`, `NAMES: tuple[str, ...]` (the nine fixture names, §12.4 order), `load_fixture(name) -> GameState`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the readers.** Create `tests/flagship_overhaul_recorder.py`:

```python
"""
What v0.24.0 looked like, for v0.25.0's byte-identity and save tests
(spec §11.3, §12.4, §12.5; plan T0).

Recorded ONCE, by plan T0, from a git worktree at 3c6882f (the v0.24.0
commit): ``tests/test_flagship_overhaul_isolation.py`` writes ``FIXTURE``
when ``CLOCKWORK_FLAGSHIP_RECORD=1``. Every reader here is a pure function
of a fresh state, so the SAME code reads the v0.25.0 tree and a comparison
is exact. Digests, not payloads: a sha256 of canonical JSON.

Version: v0.1.0 [2026-10-27]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterator

import pytest

FIXTURE = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "flagship_overhaul" / "v0240.json"
FLAGSHIP = "clockwork-dark"
OTHER_SIX = ("hue-and-cry", "neon-city", "wicked-garden", "the-long-con", "dev-story", "read-receipts")
SEVEN = (FLAGSHIP, *OTHER_SIX)
#: The flagship's LM Studio golden builds its states at these seeds (tests/llm_golden.py).
FLAGSHIP_GOLDEN_SEEDS = (5, 7)
OPENING_SEED = 1234
RUN_SEED = 7
RUN_TURNS = 20
#: The stories whose 20-turn stub run is recorded. READ RECEIPTS keeps its own
#: turn-cost and digest pins (spec §11.3).
RUN_STORIES = (FLAGSHIP, "hue-and-cry", "neon-city", "wicked-garden", "the-long-con", "dev-story")
#: The two stories that declare the three legacy wall-clock slots (spec §6.1).
LEGACY_SLOT_STORIES = ("wicked-garden", "dev-story")
LEGACY_STREAMS = ("schedule.caravan", "schedule.tinker", "schedule.militia")
LEGACY_SLOT_IDS = ("caravan_arrival", "tinker_camp", "militia_press")
LEGACY_TICKS = 120
#: Save keys that differ between two runs of one seed (wall clock, minted ids).
#: Step 4 records twice and adds any key that differed, with a comment saying why.
VOLATILE = frozenset({"session_id", "last_sim_tick_at"})

#: Long enough for the turn schema's narration minimum, and story-neutral
#: (the same words tests/test_social_seam.py stubs every story with).
NARRATION = (
    "The moment holds. Nothing in the room moves that you did not move yourself, and the air "
    "carries the small sounds of a place going on without you: a door somewhere, a voice "
    "further off, the light changing by a degree. You take it in and decide what comes next."
)


def sha(value: Any) -> str:
    """sha256 of canonical JSON (sorted keys; anything else by ``str``)."""
    blob = json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, default=str, ensure_ascii=False)
    return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


@contextmanager
def active(slug: str) -> Iterator[None]:
    """Activate a story for the duration; READ RECEIPTS quietened as its own tests do."""
    from engine.games import registry

    registry.activate(slug)
    try:
        if slug == "read-receipts":
            from engine import social
            from tests.social_fixtures import _quieted, _stop_dirty_saver

            with _quieted():
                social.boot()
                try:
                    yield
                finally:
                    _stop_dirty_saver()
        else:
            yield
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()


def catalogue(state: Any) -> dict[str, str]:
    """``legal_intents`` and the rendered turn schema, digested."""
    from engine.game import intents
    from engine.llm.schemas import storyteller_turn_schema

    verbs = intents.legal_intents(state)
    return {"intents": sha(repr(verbs)), "schema": sha(storyteller_turn_schema(intents=verbs))}


def openings(slug: str, seeds: tuple[int, ...]) -> dict[str, dict[str, str]]:
    """The catalogue at a new game's opening, per seed."""
    from engine.game import procgen

    with active(slug):
        return {
            str(seed): catalogue(procgen.new_game_state(player_name="Ash", seed=seed))
            for seed in seeds
        }


def bound_beats(slug: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Every shipped card's bound beats (``deck.bound_beats`` via ``load_deck``), digested."""
    from engine.content import deck as deck_module

    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    with active(slug):
        for deck_id in deck_module.deck_ids():
            deck = deck_module.load_deck(deck_id)
            for card in (deck.cards if deck is not None else []):
                out[f"{deck_id}/{card.id}"] = sha(card.beats)
    return out


def _stub() -> Any:
    """An ``llm_fn`` for any story's storyteller; records each storyteller request's digest."""
    from engine.agents.prompts import storyteller_persona

    prompts: list[str] = []

    def fake(messages: list[dict[str, Any]], **_: Any) -> str:
        persona = storyteller_persona().strip().splitlines()[0]
        if not any(persona in str(m.get("content", "")) for m in messages):
            return "Nothing is said, at length."
        prompts.append(sha(messages))
        return json.dumps({"narration": NARRATION, "choices": [
            {"id": "a", "text": "Go on"}, {"id": "b", "text": "Stand a while and look"}]})

    fake.prompts = prompts  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    return fake


def stub_run(slug: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Twenty narrated turns through ``/api/game/choice`` with a stub narrator,
    the background tick held at zero (it is wall-clock, R-03): each turn's
    catalogue, each storyteller prompt's digest, and the final save's digest.
    """
    from engine.scenes import default_scene, default_state

    fake = _stub()
    catalogues: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    with pytest.MonkeyPatch.context() as patch, active(slug):
        patch.setattr(default_state, "_background_tick", lambda state: 0.0)
        scene, app = default_scene.create_app(testing=True, llm_fn=fake)
        http = app.test_client()
        body = http.post("/api/game/new", json={"seed": RUN_SEED}).get_json()
        session_id = body["session_id"]
        session = scene.store.require(session_id)
        choice = body["opening"]["choices"][0]["id"]
        for _ in range(RUN_TURNS):
            catalogues.append(catalogue(session.engine.state))
            turn = http.post("/api/game/choice",
                             json={"session_id": session_id, "choice_id": choice}).get_json()
            choice = ((turn or {}).get("choices") or [{"id": "a"}])[0]["id"]
        save = {k: v for k, v in session.engine.state.to_save_dict().items() if k not in VOLATILE}
    return {"catalogues": catalogues, "prompts": list(fake.prompts), "save": sha(save)}


def legacy_rolls(slug: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The three legacy slots, unforced over 120 two-hour ticks and forced once."""
    from engine.game.procgen import new_game_state
    from engine.world.world_sim import WorldSim

    with active(slug):
        state = new_game_state(seed=3)
        emitted: list[list[Any]] = []
        for _ in range(LEGACY_TICKS):
            emitted += [[e.event_id, e.day, e.expires_day] for e in WorldSim.on_tick(state, hours=2.0)]
        counters = {s: int(state.rng_counters.get(s, 0)) for s in LEGACY_STREAMS}
        forced_state = new_game_state(seed=3)
        forced = [[e.event_id, e.day, e.expires_day]
                  for e in WorldSim.on_tick(forced_state, hours=2.0, force=list(LEGACY_SLOT_IDS))]
    return {"emitted": emitted, "counters": counters, "forced": forced}


def hue_endings() -> str:
    """HUE & CRY's endings payload block at a new game (spec §11.3, its {when, ending} terminal)."""
    from engine.game import endings
    from engine.game.procgen import new_game_state

    with active("hue-and-cry"):
        return sha(endings.to_client(new_game_state(seed=11, location_id="tallow_docks")))


def record_all() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Everything above, for every story it applies to."""
    return {
        "openings": {slug: openings(slug, (OPENING_SEED,)) for slug in SEVEN},
        "flagship_golden": openings(FLAGSHIP, FLAGSHIP_GOLDEN_SEEDS),
        "beats": {slug: bound_beats(slug) for slug in OTHER_SIX},
        "runs": {slug: stub_run(slug) for slug in RUN_STORIES},
        "legacy": {slug: legacy_rolls(slug) for slug in LEGACY_SLOT_STORIES},
        "hue_endings": hue_endings(),
    }
```

import os

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the save recorder.** Create `tests/test_clockwork_dark_saves.py` (T9 appends the v0.25.0 assertions to this same file):

```python
"""
v0.24.0 flagship saves load and play under v0.25.0 (spec §11.1, §12.4).

The nine fixtures are recorded by plan T0 from the v0.24.0 tree, under
``CLOCKWORK_FLAGSHIP_RECORD=1``. Each is ``GameState.to_save_dict()`` as
v0.24.0 wrote it. A fixture is CONSTRUCTED, not played: the builders below
write quest records and a doom reading directly, which no gameplay path
does -- the point is the shape of a save v0.24.0 could hold, not the road
to it. Read back through the save migrator, as ``SaveStore.load`` does.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

import pytest

SAVES = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "clockwork_dark_v0240"
RECORD = os.environ.get("CLOCKWORK_FLAGSHIP_RECORD") == "1"
NAMES = (
    "1_new_run", "2_caravan_active", "3_seam_solved", "4_seam_unsolved", "5_at_the_question",
    "6_at_choose_what", "7_at_pay_it_by_narrator", "8_old_press", "9_locked_hearth",
)


def _new(seed: int = 7) -> Any:
    from engine.game.procgen import new_game_state

    return new_game_state(seed=seed)


def _effect(state: Any, **row: Any) -> None:
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect

    apply_effect(state, dict(row))


def _flag(state: Any, flag: str) -> None:
    _effect(state, type="flag", flag=flag, value=True)


def _to_day(state: Any, day: int) -> None:
    from engine.game.clock import advance_time

    while state.world_day < day:
        advance_time(state, 24.0)


def _quest(state: Any, quest_id: str, stage: int, *, status: str = "active") -> None:
    from engine.game.quests import QuestProgress

    state.quests[quest_id] = QuestProgress(
        quest_id=quest_id, stage_index=stage, status=status,
        started_day=max(1, state.world_day - 1), stage_started_day=state.world_day,
    ).to_dict()


def _doom(state: Any, progress: float) -> None:
    """A world this far gone, its doom beats fired the way advance_time fires them."""
    from engine.world import world_effects

    state.evil_progress = float(progress)
    world_effects.apply_pending_beats(state)


def _item(state: Any, item_id: str) -> None:
    _effect(state, type="item", item_id=item_id, qty=1)


def _force(state: Any, event_id: str) -> None:
    """An event forced through the world tick, then seen as a turn sees it (`QuestEngine.observe`)."""
    from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine
    from engine.world.world_sim import WorldSim

    WorldSim.on_tick(state, hours=1.0, force=[event_id])
    QuestEngine.evaluate(state)


def _b1(s: Any) -> None:
    s.turn_number = 1


def _b2(s: Any) -> None:
    _to_day(s, 6)
    _force(s, "caravan_arrival")


def _b3(s: Any) -> None:
    _doom(s, 0.72)
    _flag(s, "seam_opened")


def _b4(s: Any) -> None:
    _doom(s, 0.72)


def _b5(s: Any) -> None:
    _to_day(s, 20)
    _quest(s, "bakery_apprentice", 3, status="completed")
    _quest(s, "banking_the_fire", 1)
    _flag(s, "nf_hearth_taking_stock")


def _sacrifice_open(s: Any) -> None:
    _doom(s, 0.55)
    _effect(s, type="awareness", delta=60)
    _quest(s, "naming_the_wound", 3, status="completed")
    _item(s, "the_written_name")
    _item(s, "brass_shard")


def _b6(s: Any) -> None:
    _sacrifice_open(s)
    _quest(s, "the_sacrifice", 1)
    _flag(s, "nf_sacrifice_understood")


def _b7(s: Any) -> None:
    _sacrifice_open(s)
    _flag(s, "nf_sacrifice_understood")
    _flag(s, "nf_sacrifice_chosen")
    _quest(s, "the_sacrifice", 2)


def _b8(s: Any) -> None:
    _effect(s, type="awareness", delta=25)
    _to_day(s, 4)
    _force(s, "militia_press")
    _to_day(s, 20)


def _b9(s: Any) -> None:
    _quest(s, "bakery_apprentice", 3, status="completed")
    _flag(s, "nf_hearth_book_closed")
    _effect(s, type="ending_lock", ending="the_hearth_holds")
    _effect(s, type="ending_module")


BUILDERS: dict[str, Callable[[Any], None]] = dict(zip(NAMES, (_b1, _b2, _b3, _b4, _b5, _b6, _b7, _b8, _b9)))


def load_fixture(name: str) -> Any:
    """A recorded v0.24.0 state, migrated as SaveStore.load migrates one."""
    from engine.game.state import GameState
    from engine.persistence.migrations import migrate

    raw = json.loads((SAVES / f"{name}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return GameState.from_dict(migrate(raw, slug="clockwork-dark"))


@pytest.fixture
def flagship():
    from engine.games import registry

    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()


def test_the_v0240_saves_exist_or_are_made_now(flagship) -> None:
    """Under CLOCKWORK_FLAGSHIP_RECORD=1 (plan T0, in the v0.24.0 worktree) this WRITES the nine."""
    if RECORD:
        SAVES.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        for name, build in BUILDERS.items():
            state = _new()
            build(state)
            (SAVES / f"{name}.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(state.to_save_dict(), indent=1, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    assert sorted(p.stem for p in SAVES.glob("*.json")) == sorted(NAMES)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", NAMES)
def test_each_v0240_save_loads(flagship, name: str) -> None:
    state = load_fixture(name)
    assert state.save_version == 2 and state.world_day >= 1
```

### Task 1: Measure the legacy event rates, then E1 probabilistic declared events (§6.1, §10.1, rule 10) — L

**Files:**
- Modify: `scripts/simulate.py` (`RunResult.event_instances`, `RunResult.day_awareness`, `Simulation._observe_events`, `event_rates`, `rate_gates`, the `--event-rates` flag)
- Create: `tests/test_simulate_event_rates.py`
- Modify: `engine/world/schedules.py` (`LEGACY_STREAMS`, `EVENT_STREAM_PREFIX`, `event_stream`, `declared_event_problem`, `instances`, `force_declared`, `declared_events_due(..., active_before=...)`, `_declared_event(..., rng=, state=)`, `load_schedules` refusing a bad probabilistic event)
- Modify: `engine/game/clock.py` (the day roll's pre-expiry snapshot)
- Modify: `engine/world/world_sim.py` (`on_tick`'s force hook for a declared event)
- Modify: `engine/games/validation.py` (`check_declared_events`, called from `_run`)
- Modify: `games/clockwork-dark/data/world/schedules.yaml` (the three legacy blocks become three declared `events:`; the header carries the measured tables)
- Test: `tests/test_declared_events.py` (extended)
- Docs: `docs/GOVERNANCE.md` (the "Probabilistic declared world events" row rewritten), `docs/AUTHORING.md` §3.7 (the probabilistic form), `docs/DESIGN.md` (the story bible's schedule table), root and story `CHANGELOG.md` (the pre-change and post-change tables)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T0's `<ISOLATION>`.
- Produces: `schedules.declared_events_due(state, from_day, to_day, *, active_before: list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None = None) -> list[SimEvent]`; `schedules.instances(state) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]`; `schedules.event_stream(event_id, spec) -> str`; `schedules.declared_event_problem(event_id, spec) -> str | None`; `schedules.force_declared(state, event_id, spec) -> SimEvent`; `schedules.LEGACY_STREAMS: frozenset[str]`. A declared event with `probability_per_day` repeats, sets no `event_fired_` flag, and draws on `event.<id>`. `simulate.py --event-rates [--seeds N] [--json]`. T7 adds `the_last_hour` to the flagship's `events:` (Plan decision 15 below).

**Plan decision 15 (this task):** `the_last_hour` forces `the_stopped_wheel`, a deck that exists only from T7, and the validator refuses a `forces_scene:` naming no deck (`check_declared_event_scenes`). It therefore lands in T7 with its deck, not here.

- [ ] **Step 1: The measuring instrument (a failing test first).** Create `tests/test_simulate_event_rates.py`:

```python
"""
`simulate.py --event-rates` (v0.25.0 T1, spec §6.1 "Rates", rule 10): starts per
eligible in-game day of the caravan, tinker and press, per policy.

A day d is ELIGIBLE for an event when no instance of it that started BEFORE d
is still active on d, and the event's own gate holds on d (the caravan from its
`min_day`, the press while awareness was at its threshold at d's first sample).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def _simulate():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("simulate_for_rates", ROOT / "scripts" / "simulate.py")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_event_rates_counts_starts_per_eligible_day() -> None:
    sim = _simulate()
    instances = {("caravan_arrival", 6, 8), ("caravan_arrival", 10, 12), ("militia_press", 7, 8)}
    awareness = {d: (10.0 if d < 7 else 25.0) for d in range(1, 13)}
    gates = {
        "caravan_arrival": lambda d, aw: d >= 5 and d in aw,
        "tinker_camp": lambda d, aw: d in aw,
        "militia_press": lambda d, aw: d in aw and aw[d] >= 20,
    }
    rates = sim.event_rates(instances, awareness, first_day=1, last_day=12, gates=gates)
    # Caravan: days 5..12 = 8 days, day 7 (carried from 6) and day 11 (carried from 10) not eligible.
    assert rates["caravan_arrival"] == {"starts": 2, "eligible_days": 6, "per_day": pytest.approx(2 / 6)}
    # Press: eligible from day 7 (awareness 25) to 12 = 6 days, one start.
    assert rates["militia_press"]["starts"] == 1 and rates["militia_press"]["eligible_days"] == 6
    assert rates["tinker_camp"] == {"starts": 0, "eligible_days": 12, "per_day": 0.0}


def test_the_flag_reports_every_policy_and_the_mean(capsys) -> None:
    from engine.games import registry

    sim = _simulate()
    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        assert sim.main(["--event-rates", "--policy", "cautious", "--turns", "6", "--seeds", "1", "--json"]) == 0
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
    report = json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)
    assert set(report["policies"]) == {"cautious"}
    assert set(report["mean"]) == {"caravan_arrival", "tinker_camp", "militia_press"}
    assert "first_caravan_day" in report["policies"]["cautious"]


def test_the_flag_is_the_flagships_alone() -> None:
    sim = _simulate()
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        sim.main(["--event-rates", "--game", "hue-and-cry"])
```

```python
#: The three events whose rates v0.25.0 T1 measures (spec §6.1 "Rates").
RATE_EVENTS = ("caravan_arrival", "tinker_camp", "militia_press")


def rate_gates() -> dict[str, Callable[[int, dict[int, float]], bool]]:
    """Each event's own gate, read from the ACTIVE story's schedules (legacy block or declared event)."""
    sched = load_schedules()

    def spec(event_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return dict((sched.get("events") or {}).get(event_id) or sched.get(event_id) or {})

    caravan_min = int(spec("caravan_arrival").get("min_day", 5) or 5)
    press = spec("militia_press")
    press_min = float(press.get("min_awareness", 20) or 20)
    when = press.get("when")
    if isinstance(when, dict) and "min_awareness" in when:
        press_min = float(when["min_awareness"])
    return {
        "caravan_arrival": lambda d, aw: d >= caravan_min and d in aw,
        "tinker_camp": lambda d, aw: d in aw,
        "militia_press": lambda d, aw: d in aw and aw[d] >= press_min,
    }


def event_rates(
    instances: Iterable[tuple[str, int, int]],
    day_awareness: dict[int, float],
    *,
    first_day: int,
    last_day: int,
    gates: dict[str, Callable[[int, dict[int, float]], bool]],
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Starts per ELIGIBLE day, per event: a day is eligible when no instance that
    started before it is active on it and the event's gate holds. A day with no
    sample (a long sleep crossed it) fails every gate, so it is never counted.
    """
    rows = sorted(set(instances))
    out: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for event_id in RATE_EVENTS:
        mine = [(start, expires) for eid, start, expires in rows if eid == event_id]
        starts = eligible = 0
        for day in range(int(first_day), int(last_day) + 1):
            if any(start < day < expires for start, expires in mine):
                continue
            if not gates[event_id](day, day_awareness):
                continue
            eligible += 1
            starts += sum(1 for start, _ in mine if start == day)
        out[event_id] = {"starts": starts, "eligible_days": eligible,
                         "per_day": (starts / eligible) if eligible else 0.0}
    return out
```

(`Iterable` joins the module's `typing` import; `load_schedules` is imported from `engine.world.schedules` beside the module's other engine imports.)

```python
def measure_event_rates(
    policies: list[str], seeds: list[int], turns: int, tick_hours: float
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Per policy: summed starts and eligible days over the seeds, and the first caravan's median day."""
    gates = rate_gates()
    report: dict[str, Any] = {"seeds": [seeds[0], seeds[-1]], "turns": turns, "policies": {}}
    for name in policies:
        totals = {e: {"starts": 0, "eligible_days": 0} for e in RATE_EVENTS}
        firsts: list[int] = []
        for seed in seeds:
            result = Simulation(policy=name, seed=seed, turns=turns, tick_hours=tick_hours).run()
            last_day = max(result.day_awareness) if result.day_awareness else 1
            rates = event_rates(result.event_instances, result.day_awareness,
                                first_day=1, last_day=last_day, gates=gates)
            for event_id, row in rates.items():
                totals[event_id]["starts"] += row["starts"]
                totals[event_id]["eligible_days"] += row["eligible_days"]
            caravans = sorted(s for e, s, _ in result.event_instances if e == "caravan_arrival")
            if caravans:
                firsts.append(caravans[0])
        row = {e: {**t, "per_day": round(t["starts"] / t["eligible_days"], 4) if t["eligible_days"] else 0.0}
               for e, t in totals.items()}
        row["first_caravan_day"] = statistics.median(firsts) if firsts else None
        report["policies"][name] = row
    report["mean"] = {
        e: round(statistics.fmean(report["policies"][p][e]["per_day"] for p in policies), 4)
        for e in RATE_EVENTS
    }
    return report
```

(`statistics` is already imported there. `statistics.median`/`fmean` read only the harness's own counts and nothing they return reaches a `GameState`, so rule 11's libm ban does not apply.)

def _recorder(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> list[tuple]:
    """Every event applied, as (id, start day, expiry, rumour), in order."""
    seen: list[tuple] = []
    real = WorldSim.apply_events

    def record(state, events):
        seen.extend((e.event_id, e.day, e.expires_day, e.payload.get("rumor", "")) for e in events)
        return real(state, events)

    monkeypatch.setattr(WorldSim, "apply_events", staticmethod(record))
    return seen


def _cut(monkeypatch, cuts: list[float], *, seed: int = 5, tick: bool = False) -> tuple[list, list]:
    state = GameState(rng_seed=seed)
    seen = _recorder(monkeypatch)
    for hours in cuts:
        if tick:
            WorldSim.on_tick(state, hours=hours)
        else:
            clock_module.advance_time(state, hours)
    monkeypatch.undo()
    return seen, sorted((str(e["event_id"]), int(e["day"])) for e in state.world_events)

Imports gain `from engine.game.rng import SCHEDULE_CARAVAN, SCHEDULE_MILITIA, SCHEDULE_TINKER, world_rng`. Add after `EVENT_FIRED_PREFIX`:

```python
#: The three legacy slots' streams. ``WorldSim.on_tick`` draws each of them on
#: EVERY background tick in every story, before it asks whether the story
#: declares the slot, so their counters count real-time ticks. A declared
#: event that drew on one would roll on wall-clock time again (v0.25.0 §6.1).
LEGACY_STREAMS = frozenset({SCHEDULE_CARAVAN, SCHEDULE_TINKER, SCHEDULE_MILITIA})

#: A probabilistic declared event's default stream: ``event.<event_id>``.
EVENT_STREAM_PREFIX = "event."

#: Keys read only on the probabilistic form.
_PROBABILISTIC_ONLY = ("stream", "rumor_from")


def event_stream(event_id: str, spec: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """The stream a probabilistic event draws on: its own ``stream:``, else ``event.<id>``."""
    return str((spec or {}).get("stream") or f"{EVENT_STREAM_PREFIX}{event_id}")


def declared_event_problem(event_id: str, spec: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    What is wrong with one declared event's PROBABILISTIC shape, or None.

    Shared by ``load_schedules`` (a ValueError naming the file) and the content
    validator (an Issue), so the two cannot disagree. An event with no
    ``probability_per_day`` and none of its keys is the v0.13 shape and is not
    checked here.
    """
    if not isinstance(spec, dict):
        return None
    if "probability_per_day" not in spec:
        extra = [k for k in _PROBABILISTIC_ONLY if k in spec]
        return f"`{extra[0]}` is read only with probability_per_day" if extra else None
    try:
        chance = float(spec["probability_per_day"])
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return "probability_per_day must be a number"
    if not 0.0 <= chance <= 1.0:
        return "probability_per_day must be a number from 0 to 1"
    if "on_day" in spec or "every_days" in spec:
        return "probability_per_day cannot be combined with on_day or every_days"
    stream = event_stream(event_id, spec)
    if stream in LEGACY_STREAMS:
        return (f"stream `{stream}` is a legacy slot's stream, drawn by the background tick "
                f"in every story; use `{EVENT_STREAM_PREFIX}{event_id}`")
    return None


def instances(state: GameState) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
    """``(event_id, start_day, expires_day)`` for every event on state; a malformed row is skipped."""
    out: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
    for raw in state.world_events:
        try:
            out.append((str(raw.get("event_id") or ""), int(raw.get("day", 0) or 0),
                        int(raw.get("expires_day"))))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
    return out


def _active_on(rows: list[tuple[str, int, int]], event_id: str, day: int) -> bool:
    return any(eid == event_id and start <= day < expires for eid, start, expires in rows)
```

Replace `declared_events_due` with:

```python
def declared_events_due(
    state: GameState,
    from_day: int,
    to_day: int,
    *,
    active_before: Optional[list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = None,
) -> list[SimEvent]:
    """
    Story-declared world events that start on a day in ``(from_day, to_day]``.

    Called from ``advance_time``'s day roll, so an event starts from the seed,
    the choices and the days crossed -- never from how long the menu sat open.

    THREE FORMS. A fixed day (``on_day``) or a cadence (``every_days``); a
    ``when:`` that fires ONCE, the first rolled day it holds; and (v0.25.0 E1)
    ``probability_per_day``: one draw per eligible day crossed, on the event's
    OWN stream (``event_stream``), never a legacy slot's. A probabilistic event
    REPEATS and its ``when:`` is a gate, so it sets no ``event_fired_`` flag.

    ``active_before``: the ``(event_id, start, expires)`` rows state held
    BEFORE this advance's expiry (``advance_time`` takes it). A probabilistic
    event's activity is read PER DAY from those rows plus this call's own
    draws, so an instance expiring inside a long step still blocks its last
    days, and one drawn inside it blocks the days after. The other two forms
    read ``state.world_events`` as they always did.

    Also fires the flagship's procgen festival on its ``day_offset``.
    """
    loaded = load_schedules()
    declared = (loaded.get("events") or {}) if isinstance(loaded, dict) else {}
    rows = list(active_before) if active_before is not None else instances(state)
    out: list[SimEvent] = []
    for day in range(int(from_day) + 1, int(to_day) + 1):
        for event_id, spec in sorted(declared.items()):
            if not isinstance(spec, dict):
                continue
            if "probability_per_day" in spec:
                event = _probabilistic_due(state, str(event_id), spec, day, rows)
                if event is not None:
                    out.append(event)
                    rows.append((event.event_id, event.day, event.expires_day))
                continue
            if _event_active(state, str(event_id)):
                continue
            if "when" in spec:
                if state.flags.get(f"{EVENT_FIRED_PREFIX}{event_id}"):
                    continue
                from engine.game.quests import evaluate_condition

                if not evaluate_condition(state, spec.get("when")):
                    continue
            elif not _declared_fire_day(spec, day):
                continue
            out.append(_declared_event(str(event_id), spec, day))

        festival = getattr(state.procgen, "festival", None) or {}
        if (
            festival.get("name")
            and int(festival.get("day_offset") or 0) == day
            and not _event_active(state, "festival")
        ):
            out.append(
                _declared_event(
                    "festival",
                    {
                        "duration_days": 1,
                        "text": f"It is {festival['name']}, and the whole village knows it.",
                    },
                    day,
                )
            )
    return out


def _probabilistic_due(
    state: GameState,
    event_id: str,
    spec: dict[str, Any],
    day: int,
    rows: list[tuple[str, int, int]],
) -> Optional[SimEvent]:
    """One eligible day's draw, or None. An ineligible day draws nothing."""
    if day < int(spec.get("min_day") or 0) or _active_on(rows, event_id, day):
        return None
    if "when" in spec:
        from engine.game.quests import evaluate_condition

        # Read against the state as the step ends: inside one multi-day step
        # nothing time-driven writes what a gate here may read (spec §6.1).
        if not evaluate_condition(state, spec.get("when")):
            return None
    rng = world_rng(state, event_stream(event_id, spec))
    if rng.random() >= float(spec.get("probability_per_day") or 0.0):
        return None
    return _declared_event(event_id, spec, day, rng=rng, state=state)


def force_declared(state: GameState, event_id: str, spec: dict[str, Any]) -> SimEvent:
    """
    Start a declared event TODAY with no probability draw (the force hook:
    ``WorldSim.on_tick(force=...)``). Its rumour, if it names a speaker, is
    still drawn on its own stream.
    """
    rng = world_rng(state, event_stream(event_id, spec)) if spec.get("rumor_from") else None
    return _declared_event(event_id, spec, state.world_day, rng=rng, state=state)
```

Change `_declared_event`'s signature and two lines:

```python
def _declared_event(
    event_id: str,
    spec: dict[str, Any],
    day: int,
    *,
    rng: Optional[random.Random] = None,
    state: Optional[GameState] = None,
) -> SimEvent:
    duration = max(1, int(spec.get("duration_days") or 1))
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {"text": str(spec.get("text") or ""), "declared": True}
    if spec.get("rumor"):
        payload["rumor"] = str(spec["rumor"])
    if spec.get("rumor_from") and rng is not None and state is not None:
        # After the probability draw, on the same stream; the tier is read as
        # the step ends (spec §6.1).
        payload["rumor"] = pick_rumor(state, rng, source_npc=str(spec["rumor_from"]))
    if "when" in spec and "probability_per_day" not in spec:
        payload["fired_flag"] = f"{EVENT_FIRED_PREFIX}{event_id}"

    def check_declared_events(self) -> None:
        """A probabilistic declared event's shape (v0.25.0 E1): a probability, never a legacy stream."""
        path = self._file("world_schedules")
        doc = _read_yaml(path) if path is not None and path.is_file() else None
        if not isinstance(doc, dict):
            return
        from engine.world.schedules import declared_event_problem

        source = self._rel(path)
        for event_id, spec in (doc.get("events") or {}).items():
            problem = declared_event_problem(str(event_id), spec)
            if problem:
                self._add(source, str(event_id), problem)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T0's recordings.
- Produces (T11 reads these): `intents.OVERLAY_ACTIONS: frozenset[str]` = `{"buy","sell","eat","use","equip","unequip","craft","work"}`; `intents.overlay_targets(state) -> dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]]` (every action a key, uncapped, `(target, label)`); `intents.describe_overlay_intent(state, intent) -> str` (`"Buy: Loaf from Maris, 2c"`, `"Use: Poultice bandage"`; `""` when not on offer); `intents.overlay_refusal(state, intent) -> str`; `intents.OVERLAY_SCENE_REFUSAL`, `intents.OVERLAY_STALE`; `execute_intent(intent, engine, *, agent=..., overlay: bool = False)`; `run_turn(session, action, *, intent, authored, emit_callback, overlay: bool = False)`; `run_guarded(..., overlay: bool = False)`; `default_state.resolve_overlay_intent(session, raw) -> tuple[dict, str, str]` (`(intent, sentence, refusal)`); socket event `overlay_action {session_id, intent: {action, target}, label}`; REST `POST /api/game/choice {session_id, overlay_intent: {action, target}}`; `GET /api/intents/overlay?session_id=` → `{scene_owns_turn: bool, actions: {action: [{target, label, slot?}]}}` (`slot` on `equip`/`unequip` rows only: Plan decision 16).

**Plan decision 16 (this task):** the route's `equip` and `unequip` rows also carry `slot`, read from the item's `equip:` block, so the pack (T11) can say "Wear" or "Ready" without reading tags (§7.3). Every other row is exactly `{target, label}`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `tests/test_overlay_intents.py`:

```python
"""
The overlay intent path (v0.25.0 E2, spec §6.2, §7.1-§7.2; rule 1).

An overlay hands the server `{action, target}`. The server checks it against
an UNCAPPED legality list for a closed set of verbs (`overlay_targets`), under
the turn lock, runs the turn with it -- and refuses in words otherwise: before
the turn (`turn_error`, nothing ran) for a pair not on offer, in the PROSE for
a scene that owns the turn. The narrator's capped catalogue does not move.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Iterator

import pytest

from engine.games import registry
from tests import flagship_overhaul_recorder as rec

PROMPTS: list[str] = []


@pytest.fixture
def flagship() -> Iterator[None]:
    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()


def _state(seed: int = 7) -> Any:
    from engine.game.procgen import new_game_state

    return new_game_state(seed=seed)


def _at(state: Any, place: str, hour: int) -> None:
    """Stand somewhere at an hour (the clock moved by its one writer)."""
    from engine.game.clock import advance_time, hours_until

    state.location_id = place
    if state.world_hour != hour:
        advance_time(state, hours_until(state, hour))


def _grant(state: Any, item_id: str, qty: int = 1) -> None:
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect

    apply_effect(state, {"type": "item", "item_id": item_id, "qty": qty})


def _run(state: Any, intent: dict, *, overlay: bool) -> dict:
    from engine.agents.tool_dispatcher import execute_intent
    from engine.game.engine import GameEngine, active_engine

    engine = GameEngine(state)
    with active_engine(engine):
        (receipt,) = execute_intent(intent, engine, overlay=overlay)
    return receipt


def _edible(n: int) -> list[str]:
    from engine.game import inventory

    return sorted(i for i in inventory.load_items() if inventory.is_edible(i))[:n]


def test_the_capped_catalogue_is_the_first_eight_uncapped_rows(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.game import intents

    state = _state()
    for item_id in _edible(10):
        _grant(state, item_id)
    rows = intents.overlay_targets(state)["eat"]
    assert len(rows) >= 10
    verb = intents.find_verb(intents.legal_intents(state), "eat")
    assert verb is not None and list(verb.options) == rows[:8]


def test_a_ninth_sellable_thing_is_legal_from_the_overlay_with_its_own_label(flagship, monkeypatch) -> None:
    from engine.game import intents, trade

    state = _state()
    _at(state, "edgewood_bakery", 9)
    monkeypatch.setattr(trade, "deals_in", lambda npc_id, item_id: True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(trade, "refusal_to_buy", lambda st, npc_id: "")
    monkeypatch.setattr(trade, "quote", lambda *a, **k: {"unit_price": 1, "unit_kind": "clean"})
    for item_id in _edible(10):
        _grant(state, item_id)
    rows = intents.overlay_targets(state)["sell"]
    verb = intents.find_verb(intents.legal_intents(state), "sell")
    assert verb is not None and len(verb.targets) == 8 and len(rows) >= 10
    assert list(verb.options) == rows[:8]
    ninth, label = rows[8]
    intent = {"action": "sell", "target": ninth}
    assert intents.describe_intent(state, intent) == ""
    assert intents.describe_overlay_intent(state, intent) == f"Sell: {label}"
    assert _run(state, intent, overlay=True)["skill"] == "trade_sell"
    assert _run(state, intent, overlay=False)["skill"] == "sell"          # a legality refusal


def _first(items: dict, key: str, state: Any) -> str:
    from engine.game import inventory

    for item_id, row in sorted(items.items()):
        if isinstance(row.get(key), dict) and (key != "equip" or inventory.equip_spec(item_id)):
            _grant(state, item_id)
            if key != "use" or not inventory.use_refusal(state, item_id):
                return item_id
    raise AssertionError(f"no flagship item with a usable {key}: block")


def test_use_equip_and_unequip_pass_only_with_the_overlay_mark(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.game import intents, inventory

    state = _state()
    items = inventory.load_items()
    wear = _first(items, "equip", state)
    use = _first(items, "use", state)
    refused = _run(state, {"action": "equip", "target": wear}, overlay=False)
    assert refused["refused"] and refused["skill"] == "equip"
    worn = _run(state, {"action": "equip", "target": wear}, overlay=True)
    assert worn["skill"] == "equip_item" and worn["success"]
    assert wear in [t for t, _ in intents.overlay_targets(state)["unequip"]]
    off = _run(state, {"action": "unequip", "target": wear}, overlay=True)
    assert off["skill"] == "unequip_item" and off["success"]
    used = _run(state, {"action": "use", "target": use}, overlay=True)
    assert used["skill"] == "use_item"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("slug", rec.SEVEN)
def test_no_storys_grammar_offers_an_overlay_only_verb(slug: str) -> None:
    from engine.game import intents, procgen

    with rec.active(slug):
        state = procgen.new_game_state(player_name="Ash", seed=rec.OPENING_SEED)
        actions = {v.action for v in intents.legal_intents(state)}
    assert not actions & intents.OVERLAY_ONLY_ACTIONS


def test_a_press_while_an_encounter_is_open_is_refused_and_the_encounter_is_untouched(flagship) -> None:
    import copy

    from engine.game import encounter, intents

    state = _state()
    _grant(state, _edible(1)[0])
    encounter.begin(state, "lost_child_at_the_treeline")
    before = copy.deepcopy(state.encounter)
    target = intents.overlay_targets(state)["eat"][0][0]
    receipt = _run(state, {"action": "eat", "target": target}, overlay=True)
    assert receipt["refused"] and receipt["result"]["error"] == intents.OVERLAY_SCENE_REFUSAL
    assert state.encounter == before


def test_a_press_while_a_card_is_dealt_is_refused_and_the_card_is_untouched() -> None:
    import copy

    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game import intents

    with rec.active("dev-story"):
        from engine.content import deck as deck_module

        state = _state()
        deck_id = deck_module.deck_ids()[0]
        assert director.begin(state, deck_id, forced_card=deck_module.load_deck(deck_id).cards[0].id)["ok"]
        before = copy.deepcopy(state.scene)
        receipt = _run(state, {"action": "eat", "target": "anything"}, overlay=True)
    assert receipt["refused"] and receipt["result"]["error"] == intents.OVERLAY_SCENE_REFUSAL
    assert state.scene == before


# -- through the doors ----------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _stub_llm():
    from engine.agents.prompts import storyteller_persona

    def fake(messages, **_):
        persona = storyteller_persona().strip().splitlines()[0]
        if not any(persona in str(m.get("content", "")) for m in messages):
            return "Nothing is said, at length."
        PROMPTS.append(json.dumps(messages))
        return json.dumps({"narration": rec.NARRATION, "choices": [{"id": "a", "text": "Go on"},
                                                                   {"id": "b", "text": "Look about"}]})

    return fake


@pytest.fixture
def game(flagship, monkeypatch):
    from engine.scenes import default_scene, default_state

    PROMPTS.clear()
    monkeypatch.setattr(default_state, "_background_tick", lambda state: 0.0)
    scene, app = default_scene.create_app(testing=True, llm_fn=_stub_llm())
    http = app.test_client()
    body = http.post("/api/game/new", json={"seed": 7}).get_json()
    session = scene.store.require(body["session_id"])
    return scene, app, http, session


def test_the_route_lists_every_verb_and_whether_a_scene_owns_the_turn(game) -> None:
    from engine.game import intents

    _scene, _app, http, session = game
    answer = http.get("/api/intents/overlay", query_string={"session_id": session.session_id}).get_json()
    assert answer["scene_owns_turn"] is False
    assert set(answer["actions"]) == set(intents.OVERLAY_ACTIONS)
    assert http.get("/api/intents/overlay", query_string={"session_id": "000000000000"}).status_code == 404


def test_an_unknown_action_is_refused_before_any_turn(game) -> None:
    scene, app, _http, session = game
    sio = scene.socketio.test_client(app)
    turn = session.engine.state.turn_number
    sio.get_received()
    sio.emit("overlay_action", {"session_id": session.session_id, "intent": {"action": "dance", "target": "x"}})
    (event,) = [e for e in sio.get_received() if e["name"] == "turn_error"]
    assert event["args"][0]["busy"] is False and event["args"][0]["stale_choice"] is True
    assert session.engine.state.turn_number == turn and not PROMPTS


def test_an_overlay_pair_stale_before_the_turn_is_refused_with_nothing_run(game) -> None:
    _scene, _app, http, session = game
    gold = session.engine.state.stats.gold
    answer = http.post("/api/game/choice", json={
        "session_id": session.session_id, "overlay_intent": {"action": "buy", "target": "npc_maris/no_such_loaf"}})
    assert answer.status_code == 409 and answer.get_json()["stale_choice"] is True
    assert session.engine.state.stats.gold == gold and not PROMPTS


def test_a_pair_made_stale_under_the_lock_reaches_the_prose(game) -> None:
    from engine.game import intents
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.scenes.default_state import run_turn

    _scene, _app, _http, session = game
    state = session.engine.state
    _at(state, "edgewood_bakery", 9)
    rows = intents.overlay_targets(state)["buy"]
    assert rows, "Maris sells at the bakery at nine"
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "gold", "delta": -state.stats.gold})     # the coin spent elsewhere
    run_turn(session, "The player chooses: buy", intent={"action": "buy", "target": rows[0][0]}, overlay=True)
    assert PROMPTS and intents.OVERLAY_STALE in PROMPTS[-1]      # the refusal, in the narrator's input


def test_a_press_while_an_encounter_is_open_reaches_the_prose_and_leaves_it_open(game) -> None:
    import copy

    from engine.game import encounter, intents
    from engine.scenes.default_state import run_turn

    _scene, _app, _http, session = game
    state = session.engine.state
    _grant(state, _edible(1)[0])
    encounter.begin(state, "lost_child_at_the_treeline")
    before = copy.deepcopy(state.encounter)
    target = intents.overlay_targets(state)["eat"][0][0]
    run_turn(session, "The player chooses: eat", intent={"action": "eat", "target": target}, overlay=True)
    assert intents.OVERLAY_SCENE_REFUSAL in PROMPTS[-1]
    assert encounter.active(state) and state.encounter["id"] == before["id"]


def test_a_legal_press_runs_its_skill_and_the_turn_carries_it(game) -> None:
    from engine.game import intents

    scene, app, _http, session = game
    state = session.engine.state
    _grant(state, _edible(1)[0])
    target = intents.overlay_targets(state)["eat"][0][0]
    sio = scene.socketio.test_client(app)
    sio.emit("join_session", {"session_id": session.session_id})
    sio.get_received()
    sio.emit("overlay_action", {"session_id": session.session_id,
                                "intent": {"action": "eat", "target": target}, "label": "ignored"})
    names = [e["name"] for e in sio.get_received()]
    assert "turn_update" in names and "turn_error" not in names
    assert PROMPTS and "The player chooses: Eat:" in PROMPTS[-1]


def test_a_second_overlay_press_while_a_turn_runs_is_busy(game) -> None:
    scene, app, _http, session = game
    sio = scene.socketio.test_client(app)
    sio.get_received()
    assert session.lock.acquire(blocking=False)
    try:
        sio.emit("overlay_action", {"session_id": session.session_id, "intent": {"action": "eat", "target": "x"}})
    finally:
        session.lock.release()
    (event,) = [e for e in sio.get_received() if e["name"] == "turn_error"]
    assert event["args"][0]["busy"] is True and not PROMPTS


def test_typed_text_still_carries_no_intent(game) -> None:
    from engine.scenes.default_state import resolve_player_intent

    _scene, _app, _http, session = game
    assert resolve_player_intent(session, "custom", "I eat the loaf") == {}

def _eat_rows(state: GameState) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Whatever in the pack the survival rules count as food, uncapped."""
    from engine.game import survival

    rules = survival.load_rules()
    options: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for entry in state.inventory:
        if entry.qty <= 0:
            continue
        if survival.food_value(entry.id, list(entry.tags), rules):
            options.append((entry.id, str(getattr(entry, "name", "") or entry.id)))
    return options


def _eat(state: GameState) -> Optional[IntentVerb]:
    """The narrator's ``eat``: the first eight of ``_eat_rows``."""
    options = _eat_rows(state)
    return IntentVerb("eat", tuple(options[:_MAX_OPTIONS])) if options else None
```

Then add, after `scene_owns_turn`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# the overlay's list (v0.25.0 E2, spec §7)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#: The verbs an overlay may press. Closed: anything else is refused before a turn runs.
OVERLAY_ACTIONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {"buy", "sell", "eat", "use", "equip", "unequip", "craft", "work"}
)
#: Overlay-only: never in ``legal_intents``, so no story's turn schema moves
#: (docs/GOVERNANCE.md, NOT WIRED: the narrator cannot offer them).
OVERLAY_ONLY_ACTIONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"use", "equip", "unequip"})
#: The engine's verb for each overlay action, in the sentence the narrator is handed.
OVERLAY_WORDS: dict[str, str] = {
    "buy": "Buy", "sell": "Sell", "eat": "Eat", "use": "Use", "equip": "Put on",
    "unequip": "Take off", "craft": "Make", "work": "Work",
}
#: A press while a dealt card, an encounter, a set-piece or a job owns the turn.
OVERLAY_SCENE_REFUSAL = "That will have to wait: what is in front of you comes first."
#: A pair not on offer, with no skill reason of its own.
OVERLAY_STALE = "That is not on offer here and now."


def _use_rows(state: GameState) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Carried things with a ``use:`` block that ``inventory.use`` would not refuse now."""
    from engine.game import inventory

    out: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for entry in state.inventory:
        if entry.qty > 0 and inventory.use_spec(entry.id) and not inventory.use_refusal(state, entry.id):
            out.append((entry.id, inventory.name_of(entry.id)))
    return out


def _equip_rows(state: GameState) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Carried things with an ``equip:`` block, not already worn."""
    from engine.game import inventory

    worn = set(inventory.equipped(state).values())
    return [
        (entry.id, inventory.name_of(entry.id))
        for entry in state.inventory
        if entry.qty > 0 and entry.id not in worn and inventory.equip_spec(entry.id)
    ]


def _unequip_rows(state: GameState) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """What is worn, slot by slot."""
    from engine.game import inventory

    return [(item_id, inventory.name_of(item_id)) for _slot, item_id in sorted(inventory.equipped(state).items()) if item_id]


_OVERLAY_ROWS = {
    "buy": _buy_rows, "sell": _sell_rows, "eat": _eat_rows, "craft": _craft_rows, "work": _work_rows,
    "use": _use_rows, "equip": _equip_rows, "unequip": _unequip_rows,
}


def overlay_targets(state: GameState) -> dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]]:
    """
    What an overlay may press now: every ``OVERLAY_ACTIONS`` verb, its legal
    ``(target, label)`` rows UNCAPPED. Built BESIDE ``legal_intents``, never into
    it: the narrator's catalogue keeps its eight. Read only. A verb whose rows
    cannot be built (a story with no economy) is an empty list, never an error.
    """
    out: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]] = {}
    for action in sorted(OVERLAY_ACTIONS):
        try:
            out[action] = list(_OVERLAY_ROWS[action](state))
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 -- one verb must not cost the list
            logger.debug("[intents] No overlay rows for %s: %s", action, exc)
            out[action] = []
    return out


def describe_overlay_intent(state: GameState, intent: Any) -> str:
    """
    The engine's sentence for an overlay press -- ``"Use: Poultice bandage"`` --
    from ``overlay_targets``' label, never a raw id. ``describe_intent`` cannot
    serve: it reads ``legal_intents``, which never holds the overlay-only verbs
    or a target past the eighth. "" when the pair is not on offer now.
    """
    row = normalise(intent)
    action, target = str(row.get("action") or ""), str(row.get("target") or "")
    if action not in OVERLAY_ACTIONS or not target:
        return ""
    for candidate, label in overlay_targets(state).get(action, []):
        if candidate == target:
            return f"{OVERLAY_WORDS[action]}: {label}"
    return ""


def overlay_refusal(state: GameState, intent: Any) -> str:
    """Why an overlay pair is not on offer: the skill's own reason where it has one, else ``OVERLAY_STALE``."""
    row = normalise(intent)
    action, target = str(row.get("action") or ""), str(row.get("target") or "")
    try:
        if action == "sell":
            from engine.game import trade

            reason = trade.refusal_to_buy(state, target.partition("/")[0])
            if reason:
                return str(reason)
        if action == "craft":
            from engine.skills.builtin import mechanics

            reason = mechanics._craft_refusal(state, target, mechanics._load_recipes().get(target))
            if reason:
                return str(reason)
        if action == "use":
            from engine.game import inventory

            reason = inventory.use_refusal(state, target)
            if reason:
                return reason
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 -- a reason must never cost the refusal
        logger.debug("[intents] No overlay reason for %s/%s: %s", action, target, exc)
    return OVERLAY_STALE
```

In `engine/game/inventory.py`, add and use:

```python
def use_refusal(state: GameState, item_id: str) -> str:
    """
    Why ``use`` would refuse this item now, or "". ONE RULE, TWO READERS:
    ``use`` refuses on it, and the overlay's ``use`` rows (engine/game/intents.py)
    are built from it, so an offered use is one the skill will make.
    """
    item_id = str(item_id)
    spec = use_spec(item_id)
    if spec is None:
        return f"There is nothing to do with {name_of(item_id).lower()}."
    if not holds(state, item_id):
        return "You are not carrying that."
    marker = _use_marker(item_id)
    if bool(spec.get("once_per_day")) and any(e.id == marker for e in state.active_effects):
        return f"You have already done that with the {name_of(item_id).lower()} today."
    also = [r for r in (spec.get("also_consumes") or []) if isinstance(r, dict)]
    short = [r for r in also if not holds(state, str(r.get("id")), int(r.get("qty", 1) or 1))]
    if short:
        return "You are short of " + ", ".join(name_of(str(r.get("id"))).lower() for r in short)
    return ""
```

def _sum_use_item(result: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """What was used and what it did -- the item by name, the item's own line."""
    name = str(result.get("name") or "it").lower()
    text = str(result.get("text") or "").strip()
    return f"used the {name}: {text}" if text else f"used the {name}."


def _sum_equip_item(result: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return str(result.get("text") or f"put on the {str(result.get('name') or 'it').lower()}.")


def _sum_unequip_item(result: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return str(result.get("text") or "took it off.")

False for every other caller."), and its `execute_intent(intent, session.engine)` becomes `execute_intent(intent, session.engine, overlay=overlay)`.
- Add after `resolve_authored_choice`:

```python
def resolve_overlay_intent(session: GameSession, raw: Any) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str, str]:
    """
    An overlay's press, checked before any turn runs (v0.25.0 E2, spec §7.1).

    Returns ``(intent, sentence, refusal)``. A refusal here means NOTHING RAN:
    an action outside ``OVERLAY_ACTIONS``, a story whose world turn is read
    under a narration turnstile (READ RECEIPTS' phone owns its own path), or a
    pair not in ``overlay_targets`` now. It does NOT refuse on a scene owning
    the turn: that refusal belongs in the prose, so it is left to the lock
    (``execute_intent(..., overlay=True)``). Read off the lock, cheap: the
    lock re-checks.
    """
    from engine.game import intents

    row = intents.normalise(raw)
    action = str(row.get("action") or "")
    if action not in intents.OVERLAY_ACTIONS or getattr(session, "narration_turnstile", None) is not None:
        return {}, "", intents.OVERLAY_STALE
    state = session.engine.state
    sentence = intents.describe_overlay_intent(state, row)
    if not sentence:
        return {}, "", intents.overlay_refusal(state, row)
    return {"action": action, "target": str(row.get("target") or "")}, sentence, ""
```

        @self.on("overlay_action")
        def on_overlay_action(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            """An overlay's press (v0.25.0 E2): `{session_id, intent: {action, target}, label}`."""
            data = data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
            session_id = str(data.get("session_id", ""))
            try:
                session = self.store.require(session_id)
            except KeyError:
                emit("turn_error", {"message": "session not found", "fatal": True})
                return
            if session.lock.locked():
                # Plan decision 11: the pre-check reads state off the lock.
                emit("turn_error", {"message": "A turn is already in progress.", "busy": True})
                return
            intent, sentence, refusal = resolve_overlay_intent(session, data.get("intent"))
            if refusal:
                emit("turn_error", {"message": refusal, "busy": False, "stale_choice": True})
                return
            _emit, present, cancel, waiting = _turn_hooks(session_id)
            try:
                _, error, busy = run_guarded(session, f"The player chooses: {sentence}", intent,
                                             emit_callback=_emit, present=present, cancel=cancel, overlay=True)
            finally:
                if waiting is not None:
                    self._wait_ends(waiting)
            if error:
                emit("turn_error", {"message": error, "busy": busy})
