In `engine/scenes/default_api.py`, add a view-model builder and the route (in the module docstring's route list too):

```python
def overlay_offer(state: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    What an overlay may press now (v0.25.0 E4/E2): ``{scene_owns_turn, actions:
    {action: [{target, label, slot?}]}}``, uncapped. ``slot`` rides the
    ``equip``/``unequip`` rows only (from the item's ``equip:`` block).
    """
    from engine.game import intents, inventory

    actions: dict[str, list[dict[str, str]]] = {}
    for action, rows in intents.overlay_targets(state).items():
        out = []
        for target, label in rows:
            row = {"target": target, "label": label}
            if action in ("equip", "unequip"):
                row["slot"] = str((inventory.equip_spec(target) or {}).get("slot") or "")
            out.append(row)
        actions[action] = out
    return {"scene_owns_turn": bool(intents.scene_owns_turn(state)), "actions": actions}
```

```python
def with_overlay_door(value: Any) -> None:
    """
    S14 (v0.25.0 T2, spec §6.4, §7.1): `url_map` gains exactly `/api/intents/overlay`,
    shaped as the story blueprint's other session routes, and the default namespace's
    socket handlers gain exactly `overlay_action`.
    """
    rows = value["url_map"]
    clues = next(row for row in rows if row[0] == "/api/clues")
    assert not any(row[0] == "/api/intents/overlay" for row in rows)
    rows.append(["/api/intents/overlay", list(clues[1]), clues[2].replace("api_clues", "api_overlay_intents")])
    rows.sort()
    handlers = value["socketio_handlers"]["/"]
    assert "overlay_action" not in handlers
    handlers.append("overlay_action")
    handlers.sort()
```

- Produces: a legacy terminal `{phases, flag, ending}` locks `ending` on its second death (T7 declares it); `GET /api/factions?session_id=` → `[{id, name, band: {id, label}, score}]` (T12 reads it); a menu beat's `when:` (T6-T8 author them) and `director.options`/`player_options` honouring it; `deck.beat_when_problems(card) -> list[str]`; the predicate `{npc_here: <npc_id>}` (T6-T9); the `gallery` art kind: `shipped_art_url(gallery_key, "gallery")` and `/api/art?id=<key>&kind=gallery` (T12, T15).

Append to `tests/test_terminal_death_ending.py`:

```python
# -- the legacy terminal may lock an ending (v0.25.0 E3, spec §6.3) ---------------------------------


@pytest.fixture
def legacy_with_ending(monkeypatch):
    """The flagship, its `phases`/`flag` terminal naming the fail-forward."""
    from engine.game import encounter
    from engine.games import registry

    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        rules = dict(encounter.load_death_rules())
        rules["terminal"] = {**rules["terminal"], "ending": "the_dark_keeps_its_hours"}
        monkeypatch.setattr(encounter, "load_death_rules", lambda: rules)
        yield rules
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()


def _consuming_state():
    from engine.game.procgen import new_game_state

    state = new_game_state(seed=7)
    state.evil_progress = 0.85
    return state


def _die(state):
    from engine.game import encounter
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect

    apply_effect(state, {"type": "hp", "delta": -int(state.stats.hp)})
    return encounter.check_death(state)


def test_the_first_death_in_a_listed_phase_only_marks_and_respawns(legacy_with_ending) -> None:
    from engine.game import endings

    state = _consuming_state()
    record = _die(state)
    assert record and not record.get("terminal")
    assert state.flags.get("saints_marked_you") and not state.ended
    assert endings.locked(state) == endings.NONE_ID


def test_the_second_locks_its_ending_and_shows_its_epilogue(legacy_with_ending) -> None:
    from engine.game import endings, epilogue

    state = _consuming_state()
    _die(state)
    record = _die(state)
    assert record["terminal"] and state.ended and record["ending"] == "the_dark_keeps_its_hours"
    assert endings.locked(state) == "the_dark_keeps_its_hours"
    assert epilogue.for_state(state).ending_id == "the_dark_keeps_its_hours"


def test_without_an_ending_the_legacy_terminal_is_unchanged() -> None:
    from engine.game import endings
    from engine.games import registry

    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        state = _consuming_state()
        _die(state)
        record = _die(state)
        assert record == {"died": True, "terminal": True, "ended": True, "text": record["text"]}
        assert endings.locked(state) == endings.NONE_ID
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("terminal, words", [
    ({"phases": ["consuming"], "flag": "m", "ending": "the_dark_keeps_its_hours"}, None),
    ({"phases": ["consuming"], "flag": "m", "ending": "no_such_ending"}, "is not a declared ending"),
    ({"ending": "the_dark_keeps_its_hours"}, "needs a `terminal.when`"),
    ({"when": {"flag": "x"}, "ending": "the_dark_keeps_its_hours", "phases": ["consuming"]}, "mixes"),
])
def test_the_terminal_shapes_are_told_apart(terminal, words) -> None:
    from engine.game.encounter import death_terminal_problem

    problem = death_terminal_problem(terminal, {"the_dark_keeps_its_hours"})
    assert (problem is None) if words is None else (words in (problem or ""))
```

```python
"""GET /api/factions (v0.25.0 E4, spec §6.4): names and band words for the sheet, read-only."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from engine.games import registry


@pytest.fixture
def client():
    from engine.scenes import default_scene

    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        scene, app = default_scene.create_app(testing=True, llm_fn=lambda m, **k: "{}")
        http = app.test_client()
        body = http.post("/api/game/new", json={"seed": 7}).get_json()
        yield scene, http, body["session_id"]
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()


def test_every_declared_faction_in_order_with_its_band(client) -> None:
    from engine.game import reputation

    scene, http, session_id = client
    state = scene.store.require(session_id).engine.state
    rows = http.get("/api/factions", query_string={"session_id": session_id}).get_json()
    assert [r["id"] for r in rows] == reputation.faction_ids()
    saints = next(r for r in rows if r["id"] == "unnamed_saints")
    assert saints["name"] == reputation.faction_name("unnamed_saints")
    assert saints["band"] == {"id": reputation.standing_id(state, "unnamed_saints"),
                              "label": reputation.standing(state, "unnamed_saints")}
    assert saints["score"] == reputation.get(state, "unnamed_saints")


def test_it_writes_nothing_and_needs_a_session(client) -> None:
    scene, http, session_id = client
    state = scene.store.require(session_id).engine.state
    before = state.to_save_dict()
    http.get("/api/factions", query_string={"session_id": session_id})
    assert state.to_save_dict() == before
    assert http.get("/api/factions").status_code == 404
```

Create `tests/test_deck_beat_when.py`:

```python
"""
A menu beat's own `when:` (v0.25.0 E5, spec §6.5).

Kept on the bound beat ONLY when authored, so every other story's beats bind to
the dicts they always did; `options` and `player_options` drop a beat whose
`when:` is false, and `resolve` (whose legality reads `options`) refuses it.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Iterator

import pytest
import yaml

from engine.config import set_overlay
from tests import flagship_overhaul_recorder as rec

DECK = {
    "id": "the_door",
    "draw": 1,
    "when": {"min_day": 1},
    "cards": [{
        "id": "C1", "title": "A door", "tags": ["menu"],
        "beats": [
            {"id": "open_it", "text": "Open it.", "when": {"flag": "has_key"},
             "gate": {"on_pass": {"effects": [{"type": "flag", "flag": "opened", "value": True}]}}},
            {"id": "leave_it", "text": "Leave it."},
        ],
    }],
}


@pytest.fixture
def door(tmp_path: Path) -> Iterator[None]:
    """A one-deck story directory (the deck reader is memoized on path and mtime, so no reset)."""
    scenes = tmp_path / "scenes"
    scenes.mkdir()
    (scenes / "the_door.yaml").write_text(yaml.safe_dump(DECK), encoding="utf-8")
    set_overlay({"paths": {"decks": str(scenes)}})
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        set_overlay(None)


def test_a_beat_keeps_its_when_only_when_authored() -> None:
    from engine.content.deck import bound_beats

    bound = bound_beats(DECK["cards"][0]["beats"], "the_door", "C1")
    assert bound[0]["when"] == {"flag": "has_key"}
    assert "when" not in bound[1]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("slug", rec.OTHER_SIX)
def test_no_shipped_beat_outside_the_flagship_carries_one(slug: str) -> None:
    from engine.content import deck as deck_module

    with rec.active(slug):
        for deck_id in deck_module.deck_ids():
            for card in deck_module.load_deck(deck_id).cards:
                assert not any("when" in beat for beat in card.beats), f"{deck_id}/{card.id}"


def test_options_and_labels_drop_a_beat_whose_when_is_false(door) -> None:
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.game.state import GameState

    state = GameState(rng_seed=1)
    assert director.begin(state, "the_door")["ok"]
    assert [o["id"] for o in director.options(state)] == ["leave_it"]
    assert list(director.player_options(state)) == ["leave_it"]
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "flag", "flag": "has_key", "value": True})
    assert [o["id"] for o in director.options(state)] == ["open_it", "leave_it"]


def test_a_stale_pick_is_refused_at_resolve(door) -> None:
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game.state import GameState

    state = GameState(rng_seed=1)
    director.begin(state, "the_door")
    out = director.resolve(state, chosen="open_it")
    assert out["ok"] is False and "is not on this card" in out["error"]
    assert not state.flags.get("opened")


def test_an_empty_choice_takes_the_first_offered_beat(door) -> None:
    """Plan decision 5: never the filtered-out first beat."""
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game.state import GameState

    state = GameState(rng_seed=1)
    director.begin(state, "the_door")
    assert director.resolve(state, chosen="")["ok"] and not state.flags.get("opened")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("card, words", [
    ({"id": "S", "tags": [], "beats": [{"id": "a", "when": {"flag": "x"}}, {"id": "b"}]}, "not a menu"),
    ({"id": "M", "tags": ["menu"], "beats": [{"id": "a", "when": {"flag": "x"}}]}, "keep one without"),
    ({"id": "M", "tags": ["menu"], "beats": [{"id": "a", "when": {"no_such": 1}}, {"id": "b"}]}, "unknown predicate"),
])
def test_the_validator_names_a_misused_beat_when(card, words) -> None:
    from engine.content.deck import beat_when_problems

    assert any(words in p for p in beat_when_problems(card))


def test_a_card_with_no_beat_when_has_no_problem() -> None:
    from engine.content.deck import beat_when_problems

    assert beat_when_problems({"id": "M", "tags": ["menu"], "beats": [{"id": "a"}, {"id": "b"}]}) == []
```

Create `tests/test_npc_here.py`:

```python
"""`{npc_here: <npc_id>}` (v0.25.0 E6, spec §6.6): the person is where the player is, and can be spoken to."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

import pytest

from engine.games import registry


@pytest.fixture
def state():
    from engine.game.procgen import new_game_state

    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        yield new_game_state(seed=7)
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()


def _here(state: Any, npc_id: str, place: str, hour: int) -> bool:
    from engine.game.clock import set_clock
    from engine.game.quests import evaluate_condition

    set_clock(state, day=state.world_day, hour=hour)
    state.location_id = place
    return bool(evaluate_condition(state, {"npc_here": npc_id}))


def test_it_is_in_the_grammar() -> None:
    from engine.game.quests import predicate_names

    assert "npc_here" in predicate_names()


def test_by_routine(state) -> None:
    assert _here(state, "npc_maris", "edgewood_bakery", 5)
    assert not _here(state, "npc_maris", "edgewood_square", 5)


def test_asleep_is_not_here(state) -> None:
    assert not _here(state, "npc_maris", "edgewood_bakery", 22)


def test_by_override(state) -> None:
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect

    apply_effect(state, {"type": "flag", "flag": "npc_at_npc_maris", "value": "edgewood_square"})
    assert _here(state, "npc_maris", "edgewood_square", 8)


def test_by_event(state) -> None:
    from engine.world.world_sim import WorldSim

    WorldSim.on_tick(state, hours=0.0, force=["caravan_arrival"])
    assert _here(state, "npc_odran", "edgewood_square", 2)   # asleep by routine, here by the event


def test_by_pin(state) -> None:
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect

    apply_effect(state, {"type": "flag", "flag": "npc_pin_npc_ilya", "value": True})
    assert _here(state, "npc_ilya", "tinker_caravan", 16)     # the square by routine


def test_nobody_is_not_here(state) -> None:
    assert not _here(state, "npc_nobody", "edgewood_square", 12)
```

(`set_clock` is a tests-and-migrations writer, used here only to stand on an hour; T1's force hook places Odran.)

```python
"""The `gallery` art kind (v0.25.0 E7, spec §6.7): an epilogue's gallery_key resolves to a plate."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest
import yaml

from engine.config import set_overlay


@pytest.fixture
def pack(tmp_path: Path):
    from engine.media.providers import shipped

    root = tmp_path / "art"
    (root / "gallery").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "gallery" / "banked-oven.jpg").write_bytes(b"\xff\xd8\xff")
    manifest = tmp_path / "manifest.yaml"
    manifest.write_text(yaml.safe_dump({"root": "/static/art", "gallery": {"banked_oven": "gallery/banked-oven.jpg"}}),
                        encoding="utf-8")
    subjects = tmp_path / "subjects.yaml"
    subjects.write_text(yaml.safe_dump({"gallery": {"banked_oven": {"subject": "A banked oven at night"}}}),
                        encoding="utf-8")
    set_overlay({"paths": {"art_root": str(root), "art_manifest": str(manifest), "art_subjects": str(subjects)}})
    shipped.reset_manifest_cache()
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        set_overlay(None)
        shipped.reset_manifest_cache()


def test_a_gallery_row_resolves(pack) -> None:
    from engine.api.art import shipped_art_url

    assert shipped_art_url("banked_oven", "gallery").endswith("gallery/banked-oven.jpg")
    assert shipped_art_url("no_such_key", "gallery") == ""


def test_its_prompt_reads_the_subjects_gallery_block(pack) -> None:
    from engine.media import art

    art.reset_subjects_cache()
    assert art.render_prose("banked_oven", kind="gallery").startswith("A banked oven at night.")


def test_a_story_without_the_section_resolves_nothing_new(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    from engine.api.art import shipped_art_url
    from engine.media.providers import shipped

    manifest = tmp_path / "manifest.yaml"
    manifest.write_text(yaml.safe_dump({"root": "/static/art"}), encoding="utf-8")
    set_overlay({"paths": {"art_root": str(tmp_path), "art_manifest": str(manifest)}})
    shipped.reset_manifest_cache()
    try:
        assert shipped_art_url("banked_oven", "gallery") == ""
    finally:
        set_overlay(None)
        shipped.reset_manifest_cache()
```

```python
def faction_rows(state: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Every faction the story declares, in declared order: its name, its band
    (the faction's own ``bands``, "kept in the count" included) and its score
    (v0.25.0 E4). Read-only. A route, not a payload key, so every story's turn
    payload stays byte-identical.
    """
    from engine.game import reputation

    return [
        {
            "id": faction,
            "name": reputation.faction_name(faction),
            "band": {"id": reputation.standing_id(state, faction), "label": reputation.standing(state, faction)},
            "score": int(reputation.get(state, faction)),
        }
        for faction in reputation.faction_ids()
    ]
```

Add after `chosen_beats`:

```python
def beat_when_problems(card: Any) -> list[str]:
    """
    What is wrong with a card's beat-level ``when:`` (v0.25.0 E5), for the
    validator. Allowed only on a ``menu`` card (a sequence's beats are steps,
    not choices); a menu card using one keeps at least one beat without one, so
    it can never be dealt with nothing to answer; each is a condition the
    shared grammar reads.
    """
    beats = [b for b in ((card or {}).get("beats") or []) if isinstance(b, dict)]
    with_when = [b for b in beats if "when" in b]
    if not with_when:
        return []
    if MENU_TAG not in ((card or {}).get("tags") or []):
        return [f"beat `{with_when[0].get('id') or '-'}` has a `when:` on a card that is not a menu; "
                "a sequence's beats are steps, not choices"]
    from engine.game.quests import condition_problem

    problems: list[str] = []
    if len(with_when) == len(beats):
        problems.append("every beat of this menu card has a `when:`; keep one without, or the card "
                        "can be dealt with nothing to answer")
    for beat in with_when:
        problem = condition_problem(beat.get("when"), where=f"beat `{beat.get('id') or '-'}` when")
        if problem:
            problems.append(problem)
    return problems
```

```python
def _beat_offered(state: GameState, beat: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """A menu beat's own ``when:`` (v0.25.0 E5). A beat without one is always offered."""
    if "when" not in beat:
        return True
    from engine.game.quests import evaluate_condition

    return bool(evaluate_condition(state, beat.get("when")))
```

- [ ] **Step 5: E6.** In `engine/world/npc_sim.py`, after `resolve_npc`:

```python
def _p_npc_here(state: GameState, value: Any, ctx: Any) -> bool:
    """
    ``{npc_here: npc_maris}`` (v0.25.0 E6): that person is at the player's
    place and can be spoken to -- awake, or there because of an event or a pin,
    as ``resolve_npc`` already reports. It asks the same function the people
    strip and the narrator's presence block ask, so a door and the person the
    player sees cannot disagree. A list asks for all of them. Read-only.
    """
    wanted = value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]
    if not wanted:
        return False
    for npc_id in wanted:
        presence = resolve_npc(state, str(npc_id))
        if presence is None or presence.location_id != state.location_id or not presence.available:
            return False
    return True


def _register() -> None:
    """Extend the shared condition grammar (engine/game/quests.py)."""
    from engine.game.quests import register_predicate

    register_predicate("npc_here", _p_npc_here)


_register()
```

```python
def _gallery_fields(gallery_key: str) -> Optional[dict[str, str]]:
    """Prompt fields for an ending's plate, from the subjects file's ``gallery:`` block (v0.25.0 E7)."""

    return {
        "subject": entry.get("subject", gallery_key.replace("_", " ")),
        "details": entry.get("details", ""),
        "setting": entry.get("setting", ""),
        "light": entry.get("light", ""),
        "style": entry.get("style", ""),
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `tests/test_clockwork_dark.py`:

```python
"""
The Clockwork Dark, overhauled (v0.25.0; spec docs/superpowers/specs/
2026-10-27-v0.25.0-clockwork-dark-overhaul-design.md).

One file for the story's own claims, grown task by task: the cast (T4), the
map (T5), the doors (T6-T7), the critical path and the levy (T8), and the
card-only walks and the presence sweep (T9).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterator

import pytest
import yaml

from engine.games import registry

STORY = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "games" / "clockwork-dark"
NEW_PEOPLE = ("npc_hallam", "npc_nessa", "npc_wenna", "npc_edrin")

#: Plan decision 2: each person's hours, each covering the day exactly once.
WHERE = {
    "npc_hallam": {"the_forge": range(6, 19), "edgewood_square": (19, 20),
                   "well_row": (21, 22, 23, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5)},
    "npc_nessa": {"fallow_farm": (6, 7, 8, 17, 18, 19, 20, 21, 22, 23, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5),
                  "edgewood_square": range(9, 17)},
    "npc_wenna": {"edgewood_shrine": (6, 7, 18, 19, 20, 21), "well_row": (*range(8, 18), 22, 23, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5)},
    "npc_edrin": {"fallow_farm": range(24)},
}
ASLEEP = {
    "npc_hallam": {21, 22, 23, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5}, "npc_nessa": {21, 22, 23, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5},
    "npc_wenna": {22, 23, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5}, "npc_edrin": {20, 21, 22, 23, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4},
}


@pytest.fixture
def flagship() -> Iterator[None]:
    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()


def new_state(seed: int = 7) -> Any:
    from engine.game.procgen import new_game_state

    return new_game_state(seed=seed)


def at_hour(state: Any, hour: int) -> None:
    """Stand on an hour of the current day (tests only; `set_clock` writes no world)."""
    from engine.game.clock import set_clock

    set_clock(state, day=state.world_day, hour=hour)


def _schedules() -> dict:
    return yaml.safe_load((STORY / "data" / "world" / "npc_schedules.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


# -- the cast (T4, §3.3) ------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_four_new_people_cover_every_hour_once() -> None:
    npcs = _schedules()["npcs"]
    for npc_id in NEW_PEOPLE:
        row = npcs[npc_id]
        assert row["name"] and row["role"] and row["home"], npc_id
        hours = sorted(h for slot in row["routine"] for h in slot["hours"])
        assert hours == list(range(24)), npc_id


@pytest.mark.parametrize("npc_id", NEW_PEOPLE)
def test_each_stands_where_the_cast_table_puts_them(flagship, npc_id: str) -> None:
    from engine.world import npc_sim

    state = new_state()
    for place, hours in WHERE[npc_id].items():
        for hour in hours:
            at_hour(state, hour)
            presence = npc_sim.resolve_npc(state, npc_id)
            assert presence.location_id == place, (npc_id, hour)
            assert presence.available is (hour not in ASLEEP[npc_id]), (npc_id, hour)


def test_they_are_not_in_the_procgen_village() -> None:
    """§3.3: the PROCGEN stream draws the template's named list; adding to it would regenerate every seed."""
    template = (STORY / "data" / "procgen_templates" / "edgewood.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert not any(npc_id in template for npc_id in NEW_PEOPLE)


def test_nessa_has_one_surname() -> None:
    for path in STORY.rglob("*"):
        if path.is_file() and path.suffix in (".yaml", ".md", ".json"):
            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            assert "Nessa Weller" not in text and "Weller girl" not in text, path
    assert "Weller" in (STORY / "data" / "quests" / "whisper" / "lost_goat.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_the_vines_bring_ilya_to_the_square(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.world import npc_sim, world_effects

    state = new_state()
    at_hour(state, 10)
    assert npc_sim.resolve_npc(state, "npc_ilya").location_id == "tinker_caravan"
    world_effects.apply_beat(state, "vines_breach_forest")
    assert npc_sim.resolve_npc(state, "npc_ilya").location_id == "edgewood_square"


def test_the_tower_empties_the_fallow_holding_and_brings_sera_in(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.world import npc_sim, world_effects

    state = new_state()
    at_hour(state, 10)
    world_effects.apply_beat(state, "tower_assembles")
    for npc_id, place in (("npc_edrin", "refugee_camp"), ("npc_nessa", "refugee_camp"),
                          ("npc_sera", "edgewood_square")):
        assert npc_sim.resolve_npc(state, npc_id).location_id == place, npc_id


def test_a_pin_still_outranks_a_doom_move(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.world import npc_sim, world_effects

    state = new_state()
    at_hour(state, 10)
    world_effects.apply_beat(state, "vines_breach_forest")
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "flag", "flag": "npc_pin_npc_ilya", "value": True})
    assert npc_sim.resolve_npc(state, "npc_ilya").location_id == "tinker_caravan"


def test_the_codex_lists_the_declared_people_named_once_met(flagship) -> None:
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from engine.scenes import default_api

    state = new_state()
    unmet = default_api.codex_souls(state, SimpleNamespace(relations={}))["souls"]
    met = default_api.codex_souls(state, SimpleNamespace(relations={"npc_hallam": SimpleNamespace(met=True)}))["souls"]
    assert len(unmet) == len(state.procgen.npcs) + len(NEW_PEOPLE)
    assert "Hallam" in [s["name"] for s in met] and "Hallam" not in [s["name"] for s in unmet]
    assert sum(1 for s in met if s["name"] == "Hallam") == 1


def test_the_prompt_names_the_four_and_nothing_else_moved() -> None:
    prompt = (STORY / "prompts" / "storyteller.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for name in ("Hallam", "Nessa Fallow", "Wenna", "Edrin Fallow"):
        assert name in prompt
    for word in ("intensity", "explicit", "safety", "content limit"):
        assert word not in prompt.lower()          # rule 12
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The moves that moved nobody (Plan decision 1).** In `engine/game/clocks.py::_apply_npc_moves`, replace the body from `npc = state.procgen.npc_by_id(npc_id)` to the `applied.append(...)` with:

```python
        npc = state.procgen.npc_by_id(npc_id)
        from engine.world import npc_sim

        scheduled = npc_id in (npc_sim.load_npc_schedules().get("npcs") or {})
        if npc is None and not scheduled:
            logger.warning(
                "[clocks] npc_move names an NPC this save does not have, "
                "skipping (operation=_apply_npc_moves, beat=%s, npc=%s)",
                beat_id,
                npc_id,
            )
            continue
        override = f"{npc_sim.OVERRIDE_FLAG_PREFIX}{npc_id}"
        if state.flags.get(override) == destination and (npc is None or npc.get("location_id") == destination):
            continue
        if npc is not None:
            npc["location_id"] = destination
            # Read by narration and the UI to explain why someone is standing
            # somewhere they have no business being.
            npc["displaced"] = True
        # THE MOVE ITSELF (v0.25.0). The procgen row above is read only for a
        # person with no routine; anyone with a routine slot was placed by the
        # slot, so the move moved nobody. The soft override (npc_sim's rank
        # above a routine, below an event and a pin) is what places them now.
        effects_module.apply_effect(state, {"type": "flag", "flag": override, "value": destination})
        applied.append({"type": "npc_move", "value": f"{npc_id}->{destination}"})
```

**Plan decision 17 (this task):** `edgewood_square>fallow_farm` gets `danger_dc: 6` (§5.3: "its return leg's value"), but the band its return leg uses is `village.yaml`, whose encounters trigger on `to: [edgewood_square]`, so no row would match the outbound leg and `test_every_edge_in_the_graph_with_danger_has_content` would fail. Each `village.yaml` encounter's `to:` gains `fallow_farm`. Only legs arriving at the holding change, and before this release those carried no danger.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Append to `tests/test_clockwork_dark.py`:

```python
# -- the map (T5, §3.4, §5.3) ----------------------------------------------------------------------

LOCATIONS = STORY / "data" / "world" / "locations.yaml"


def test_no_edge_keeps_a_needs_band_note_with_danger() -> None:
    for number, line in enumerate(LOCATIONS.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(), 1):
        if "needs band" in line and "danger_dc:" in line:
            assert "danger_dc: 0" in line, f"locations.yaml:{number} has danger and still needs a band"
    assert "needs band" not in "".join(
        line for line in LOCATIONS.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if "danger_dc:" in line)


BANDED = {
    6: ("forest_clearing>deeper_forest", "deeper_forest>forest_clearing", "forest_clearing>herb_glen",
        "herb_glen>forest_clearing", "deeper_forest>herb_glen", "herb_glen>deeper_forest",
        "deeper_forest>charcoal_burn", "charcoal_burn>deeper_forest", "edgewood_square>charcoal_burn",
        "edgewood_square>fallow_farm"),
    10: ("deeper_forest>old_barrows", "old_barrows>deeper_forest", "old_barrows>tunnel_entrance",
         "tunnel_entrance>old_barrows", "millhaven_gate>toll_bridge", "toll_bridge>burned_farmstead",
         "burned_farmstead>toll_bridge", "refugee_camp>burned_farmstead", "burned_farmstead>refugee_camp",
         "millhaven_gate>refugee_camp"),
    12: ("forest_clearing>the_works", "the_works>forest_clearing"),
    14: ("toll_bridge>heartland_road", "heartland_road>toll_bridge", "heartland_road>the_join",
         "the_join>heartland_road"),
}
DOORS = ("millhaven_gate>millhaven_market", "millhaven_gate>millhaven_barracks",
         "millhaven_market>millhaven_barracks", "millhaven_barracks>millhaven_market")


def test_every_marked_line_is_banded_or_a_door(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.game.locations import LOCATIONS as GRAPH

    for dc, legs in BANDED.items():
        for leg in legs:
            src, dst = leg.split(">")
            assert int(GRAPH[src]["connections"][dst]["danger_dc"]) == dc, leg
    for leg in DOORS:
        src, dst = leg.split(">")
        edge = GRAPH[src]["connections"][dst]
        assert int(edge["danger_dc"]) == 0 and int(edge["hours"]) == 0, leg


@pytest.mark.parametrize("place, condition", [
    ("the_works", "seam"),
    ("the_join", "road"),
])
def test_a_secret_place_is_unknown_until_its_condition(flagship, place: str, condition: str) -> None:
    from engine.game import locations
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.game.quests import QuestProgress

    state = new_state()
    assert not locations.is_known(state, place)
    if condition == "seam":
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "flag", "flag": "seam_opened", "value": True})
    else:
        state.quests["the_heartland_road"] = QuestProgress(quest_id="the_heartland_road", stage_index=1).to_dict()
    assert locations.is_known(state, place)


def test_a_secret_place_is_never_offered_as_a_road_before(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.game import intents

    state = new_state()
    state.location_id = "forest_clearing"
    travel = intents.find_verb(intents.legal_intents(state), "travel")
    assert "the_works" not in (travel.targets if travel else ())


def test_the_works_below_is_found_on_arrival(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine, STATUS_COMPLETED, progress_records

    state = new_state()
    state.arcs_unlocked.append("whisper")          # long open by 0.70 (arcs.yaml); the arc writer is a list append
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "flag", "flag": "seam_opened", "value": True})
    state.location_id = "the_works"
    for _ in range(3):
        QuestEngine.evaluate(state)
    assert progress_records(state)["the_works_below"].status == STATUS_COMPLETED
    assert any(i.id == "brass_shard" for i in state.inventory)


def test_the_join_is_where_the_road_is_walked() -> None:
    quest = yaml.safe_load((STORY / "data" / "quests" / "convergence" / "the_heartland_road.yaml").read_text("utf-8"))
    walk = next(s for s in quest["stages"] if s["id"] == "walk_the_join")
    assert {"at_location": "the_join"} in walk["complete_when"]["all"]


def test_the_seams_riddle_counts_its_own_answer() -> None:
    doc = yaml.safe_load((STORY / "data" / "challenges" / "edgewood.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    seam = next(p for p in doc["set_pieces"] if p["id"] == "sealed_seam")
    answer = str(seam["challenge"]["answer"])
    words = {4: "four", 5: "five", 6: "six"}
    assert f"{words[len(answer)]} letters" in str(seam["challenge"]["prompt"])
```

(`set_pieces`, `challenge`, `prompt` and `answer` are `edgewood.yaml`'s own keys; `engine/game/locations.is_known` is the secret-place reader HUE & CRY's places use.)

```yaml
  # SECRET (v0.25.0 §3.4). What the doom clock has made under the clearing: the
  # `tunnels_open` beat (0.70) opens the sealed seam's riddle at the stump, and
  # SOLVING it (`seam_opened`) is the way in. Not the barrow mouth: the two do not
  # connect. The cold in the one and the oil under the other are the same weather
  # from two directions.
  the_works:
    name: "The Works Below the Stump"
    ring: 0
    evil_multiplier: 1.6

    known_when: { flag: seam_opened }

      further than the stump has roots. It smells of oil and old time, and
      something below the last step keeps a slow, patient count.
    tags: [underground, secret, dark]
    connections:
      forest_clearing: { hours: 1, danger_dc: 12, awareness_delta: 3 }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The five bands.** Each file follows `forest.yaml`'s shape exactly (`band:`, `encounters:` rows with `id`, `tier`, `weight`, `triggers.edges` (the band's legs, both directions where both are banded), optional `triggers.phases`, `intro`, `threat: {name, resolve}`, two or three `approaches` with `skill`/`difficulty`/`text` (one may `requires_item`), and `outcomes` for `crit_success`, `success`, `partial`, `failure`, each with `text` and at most four `effects`). Its header says what roads it takes and that every number is a first value, unmeasured until T10. `works.yaml`, whole:

```yaml
# The Works Below the Stump (v0.25.0 §5.3): the clearing<->works legs (danger_dc 12).
# First values, unmeasured until T10 (rule 10).
band: works

encounters:
  - id: oil_and_old_time
    tier: 2
    weight: 30
    triggers:
      edges: ["forest_clearing>the_works", "the_works>forest_clearing"]
    intro: >-
      The steps are slick with something that is not water. Below, a sound
      like a great many small things being counted, very slowly, by something
      that is in no hurry and has never once lost its place.
    threat: { name: "The count below the stair", resolve: 3 }
    approaches:
      listen:
        skill: lore
        difficulty: standard
        text: "Stand still and learn the count's rhythm"
      steady:
        skill: nerve
        difficulty: standard
        text: "Keep your feet and keep going"
    outcomes:
      crit_success:
        text: >-
          You find the rhythm and walk inside it, and the count does not notice
          you any more than a clock notices dust.
        effects:
          - { type: awareness, delta: 3 }
      success:
        text: >-
          You go down and come up with your feet under you and the count still
          going behind you, unbothered.
        effects:
          - { type: awareness, delta: 2 }
          - { type: stamina, delta: -6 }
      partial:
        text: >-
          You slip twice on the oiled stone and the second time the count
          pauses. Then it picks up exactly where it stopped.
        effects:
          - { type: stamina, delta: -10 }
          - { type: awareness, delta: 2 }
      failure:
        text: >-
          You go down hard on the stair and lie there listening to the count
          go on as if you had not happened.
        effects:
          - { type: hp, delta: -3 }
          - { type: stamina, delta: -12 }

  - id: the_works_engine
    tier: 3
    weight: 20
    art: the_works_engine
    triggers:
      edges: ["forest_clearing>the_works", "the_works>forest_clearing"]
    intro: >-
      A thing of brass and pinned leather is working in the dark, fitting one
      cut stone to the next with the care of a mason who has forgotten what a
      wall is for. It turns its head when you breathe.
    threat: { name: "The works' engine", resolve: 4 }
    approaches:
      still:
        skill: stealth
        difficulty: hard
        text: "Go perfectly still until it goes back to its stone"
      jam:
        skill: craft
        difficulty: hard
        text: "Wedge something into the joint it keeps turning"
      run:
        skill: survival
        difficulty: standard
        text: "Back up the stair the way you came"
    outcomes:
      crit_success:
        text: >-
          It goes back to its stone. You leave with the shape of its joints in
          your head, which is worth more than anything you could have carried.
        effects:
          - { type: awareness, delta: 4 }
          - { type: item, item_id: brass_shard, qty: 1 }
      success:
        text: >-
          It loses interest in you the way weather does, all at once, and you
          are on the stair before it remembers.
        effects:
          - { type: awareness, delta: 3 }
          - { type: stamina, delta: -8 }
      partial:
        text: >-
          A pinned arm catches your sleeve and lets it go, and the cloth comes
          away with a neat row of holes in it, evenly spaced.
        effects:
          - { type: hp, delta: -3 }
          - { type: awareness, delta: 2 }
      failure:
        text: >-
          It does not hurt you so much as move you, the way it moves stone, and
          you come to on the stair with your ribs telling you about it.
        effects:
          - { type: hp, delta: -6 }
          - { type: stamina, delta: -12 }
```

```yaml
id: the_works_below
arc: whisper
name: "The Works Below"
summary: >-

  Whatever is coming has had the room ready for longer than Edgewood has had a name.
```

```python
def test_the_two_secret_places_are_on_the_procedural_tile_until_t15() -> None:
    from engine.media.art import load_subjects
    from engine.media.providers.base import ImageRequest
    from engine.media.providers.shipped import lookup

    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        for place in ("the_works", "the_join"):
            assert place in (load_subjects().get("locations") or {}), place
            assert lookup(ImageRequest(subject_id=place, kind="location")) is None, place
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
```

### Task 6: Doors I: the morning firing, the count kept, the muster roll; the eight gates and teases (§4.1-§4.3) — L

**Files:**
- Create: `games/clockwork-dark/data/scenes/the_morning_firing.yaml`, `the_count_kept.yaml`, `the_muster_roll.yaml`
- Modify: `games/clockwork-dark/game.yaml` (`paths.decks`; the header's "Three doors carry the lock" paragraph)
- Modify: `games/clockwork-dark/data/rules/endings.yaml` (all eight classes: gates, lock reasons, `tease:`; the header's "SHAPE OF THE FINALE")
- Modify: `games/clockwork-dark/data/quests/quiet_life/banking_the_fire.yaml`, `convergence/the_last_quiet_thing.yaml` (id-less locks gone; the last quiet thing's preconditions)
- Modify: `games/clockwork-dark/data/canon/state-dictionary.json` (`ending_gallery_unlocked` if absent)
- Create: `tests/clockwork_doors.py` (the door setups; T7-T10 add to it, T9 registers them in `tests/test_finales.py`)
- Test: `tests/test_clockwork_dark.py` (appended)
- Docs: story `CHANGELOG.md`, `README.md` (the endings and where their doors stand), `docs/DESIGN.md` (story bible: endings)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T3's beat-level `when:` and `npc_here`; T4's Wenna; T1's events (unchanged here).
- Produces: decks `the_morning_firing` (cards `H0_taking_stock`, `H1_the_question` beats `stay`/`stay_anyway`/`not_yet`, `Q0_show_up`, `Q1_tell_her_or_dont` beats `tell_her`/`say_nothing`), `the_count_kept` (`W1_the_list`: `give_it`/`keep_it`), `the_muster_roll` (`M1_strike_the_name`: `strike_it`/`not_yet`); every ending's clause ids, exactly §4.2's: `the_hearth_holds` {`a_place_here`, `larder_kept`, `unknowing`, `village_stands`}, `the_long_witness` {`eyes_open`, `listened`, `counted`, `stayed_three_counts`, `world_still_turning`}, `the_counted_march` {`marched`, `counted`, `served`, `still_on_the_rolls`}, `the_named_wound` {`named`, `decided`, `baked`, `wheel_still_turning`}, `the_price_asked` {`named`, `chose`, `paid`}, `the_pin_and_the_join` {`pin_taken`, `pin_held`, `join_walked`, `wheel_still_turning`}, `the_stopped_wheel` {`wheel_stopped`}.
- Produces: `tests.clockwork_doors` — `fed_until(state, day, hour)`, `stand(state, place, hour)`, `completed(state, quest_id)`, `at_stage(state, quest_id, index)`, `flag(state, name)`, `effect(state, **row)`, and the door setups `hearth_door`, `stay_anyway_door`, `witness_door`, `march_door`, `named_wound_door`.

**Plan decision 18 (this task):** a door setup may complete a quest, or stand it at a stage, through `engine/game/quests.py::_write` (the module's own record writer), because walking every quest on an ending's path stage by stage is the card-only walks' job (T9, §4.4), not each door's. Everything else a setup writes goes through `apply_effect` and `advance_time`, as `tests/endings_driver.py` requires.

- [ ] **Step 1: The door setups.** Create `tests/clockwork_doors.py`:

```python
"""
The Clockwork Dark's door setups (v0.25.0 §4.2, §12.1), shared by
tests/test_clockwork_dark.py (each door as it lands, T6-T8) and
tests/test_finales.py (the registered rows, T9).

A setup puts the run where a door opens. It writes state as play would --
`apply_effect` for flags, items, standings and stats; `advance_time` for time,
fed on the way; `state.location_id` only to stand somewhere -- with one
exception (plan decision 18): a quest the door needs completed, or at a stage,
is written through the quest module's own record writer, `quests._write`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any


def effect(state: Any, **row: Any) -> dict:
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect

    return apply_effect(state, dict(row))


def flag(state: Any, name: str) -> None:
    effect(state, type="flag", flag=name, value=True)


def fed_until(state: Any, day: int, hour: int) -> None:
    """The clock walked to `day`/`hour` through `advance_time`, fed and rested before each step."""
    from engine.game.clock import advance_time

    target = float((day - 1) * 24 + hour)
    while state.world_clock_hours < target:
        if state.hunger > 0:
            effect(state, type="hunger", delta=-state.hunger)
        if state.stats.hp < state.stats.max_hp:
            effect(state, type="hp", delta=state.stats.max_hp - state.stats.hp)
        advance_time(state, min(6.0, target - state.world_clock_hours))


def stand(state: Any, place: str, hour: int) -> None:
    """Stand at `place` at the next `hour` (today's, if it is still ahead)."""
    day = state.world_day if state.world_hour < hour else state.world_day + 1
    fed_until(state, day, hour)
    state.location_id = place


def _record(state: Any, quest_id: str, index: int, status: str) -> None:
    from engine.game.quests import QuestProgress, _write

    _write(state, QuestProgress(quest_id=quest_id, stage_index=index, status=status,
                                started_day=state.world_day, stage_started_day=state.world_day))


def completed(state: Any, quest_id: str) -> None:
    _record(state, quest_id, 99, "completed")


def at_stage(state: Any, quest_id: str, index: int) -> None:
    _record(state, quest_id, index, "active")


# -- the morning firing (H1, Q1) ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def hearth_door(state: Any) -> None:
    """The Hearth Holds: a place here, the larder walked (H0's flag), awareness low; the bakery at five."""
    completed(state, "bakery_apprentice")
    at_stage(state, "banking_the_fire", 1)
    flag(state, "nf_hearth_taking_stock")
    stand(state, "edgewood_bakery", 5)


def stay_anyway_door(state: Any) -> None:
    """The fail-forward's morning door: the larder walked, but nobody here would miss you yet."""
    at_stage(state, "banking_the_fire", 1)
    flag(state, "nf_hearth_taking_stock")
    stand(state, "edgewood_bakery", 5)


def named_wound_door(state: Any) -> None:
    """The Named Wound: named, decided, and at the last quiet morning's third stage; the bakery at five."""
    completed(state, "naming_the_wound")
    completed(state, "what_edgewood_keeps")
    at_stage(state, "the_last_quiet_thing", 2)
    stand(state, "edgewood_bakery", 5)


# -- the count kept (W1) -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def witness_door(state: Any) -> None:
    """The Long Witness: eyes open and listened (awareness 50), counted by the saints, three counts stayed;
    the shrine at six in the evening, Wenna lighting her candles."""
    effect(state, type="awareness", delta=50)
    effect(state, type="reputation", faction="unnamed_saints", delta=30)
    fed_until(state, 27, 12)
    stand(state, "edgewood_shrine", 18)


# -- the muster roll (M1) ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def march_door(state: Any) -> None:
    """The Counted March: the gate visited (the arc), militia at 20, the press seen through; the gate at nine."""
    from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine

    effect(state, type="reputation", faction="militia", delta=20)
    completed(state, "militia_press")
    stand(state, "millhaven_gate", 9)
    QuestEngine.evaluate(state)          # records the visit and opens the march arc, as a turn does
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests.** Append to `tests/test_clockwork_dark.py`:

```python
# -- doors I (T6, §4.2-§4.3) ---------------------------------------------------------------------

from endings_driver import Door, drive_ending  # noqa: E402

from tests import clockwork_doors as doors  # noqa: E402

SCENES = STORY / "data" / "scenes"


def _drive(ending_id: str, door: Door, setup) -> Any:
    state, shown = drive_ending("clockwork-dark", ending_id, door, setup, seed=7)
    return state


@pytest.mark.parametrize("ending_id, door, setup", [
    ("the_hearth_holds", Door.card("the_morning_firing", "H1_the_question", "stay"), doors.hearth_door),
    ("the_dark_keeps_its_hours", Door.card("the_morning_firing", "H1_the_question", "stay_anyway"),
     doors.stay_anyway_door),
    ("the_long_witness", Door.card("the_count_kept", "W1_the_list", "give_it"), doors.witness_door),
    ("the_counted_march", Door.card("the_muster_roll", "M1_strike_the_name", "strike_it"), doors.march_door),
    ("the_named_wound", Door.card("the_morning_firing", "Q1_tell_her_or_dont", "tell_her"), doors.named_wound_door),
    ("the_named_wound", Door.card("the_morning_firing", "Q1_tell_her_or_dont", "say_nothing"),
     doors.named_wound_door),
])
def test_each_door_of_the_first_three_decks_locks_its_ending(ending_id, door, setup) -> None:
    _drive(ending_id, door, setup)


def _deal(state: Any, deck_id: str) -> list[str]:
    from engine.content import director

    receipt = director.begin(state, deck_id)
    assert receipt["ok"], receipt
    return receipt["card_ids"]


def test_h1_offers_stay_only_while_the_hearth_is_open(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.content import director

    open_state, shut_state = new_state(), new_state()
    doors.hearth_door(open_state)
    doors.stay_anyway_door(shut_state)
    for state, offered in ((open_state, ["stay", "not_yet"]), (shut_state, ["stay_anyway", "not_yet"])):
        assert _deal(state, "the_morning_firing") == ["H1_the_question"]
        assert [o["id"] for o in director.options(state)] == offered


def test_h1_is_not_dealt_while_the_named_wound_is_open(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.content import deck as deck_module

    state = new_state()
    doors.named_wound_door(state)
    at_stage = doors.at_stage
    at_stage(state, "banking_the_fire", 1)
    doors.flag(state, "nf_hearth_taking_stock")
    eligible, _ = deck_module.eligible_cards(state, deck_module.load_deck("the_morning_firing"))
    assert [c.id for c in eligible] == ["Q1_tell_her_or_dont"]


def test_a_dealt_door_stays_answerable_after_its_person_leaves(flagship) -> None:
    """Review Focus 1: Maris goes to the square at noon; the card in hand still locks what it says."""
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game import endings
    from engine.game.clock import advance_time

    state = new_state()
    doors.hearth_door(state)
    _deal(state, "the_morning_firing")
    advance_time(state, 8.0)                       # 05:00 -> 13:00
    assert director.resolve(state, chosen="stay")["ok"]
    assert endings.locked(state) == "the_hearth_holds"


def test_not_yet_locks_nothing_and_the_deck_comes_round_again(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game import endings

    state = new_state()
    doors.hearth_door(state)
    _deal(state, "the_morning_firing")
    assert director.resolve(state, chosen="not_yet")["ok"]
    assert endings.locked(state) == endings.NONE_ID
    doors.stand(state, "edgewood_square", 12)
    director.rearm(state)
    doors.stand(state, "edgewood_bakery", 5)
    deck_id, _card, _src = director.due(state)
    assert deck_id == "the_morning_firing"


def test_no_id_less_ending_lock_remains_in_the_story() -> None:
    from engine.games.validation import walk

    for path in (STORY / "data").rglob("*.yaml"):
        for node in walk(yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))):
            if node.get("type") == "ending_lock":
                assert node.get("ending"), f"{path.relative_to(STORY)}: an id-less ending_lock"


def test_every_tease_is_one_sentence_and_names_no_ending() -> None:
    doc = yaml.safe_load((STORY / "data" / "rules" / "endings.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    titles = [str(body["label"]).lower() for body in doc["classes"].values()]
    for ending_id, body in doc["classes"].items():
        tease = str(body.get("tease") or "")
        assert tease.endswith(".") and tease.count(". ") == 0, ending_id
        assert not any(title in tease.lower() for title in titles), ending_id
        assert "clockwork" not in tease.lower(), ending_id    # the reveal word (spoilers.yaml)
```

(`engine.games.validation.walk` is the validator's node walker used by `check_event_references`.)

- [ ] **Step 3: The three decks.** `game.yaml` `paths` gains `decks: "games/clockwork-dark/data/scenes"` (with a comment: fourteen decks, v0.25.0 §4.3; the filename is the deck id). Create `data/scenes/the_morning_firing.yaml`:

```yaml
# The Clockwork Dark -- the morning firing (v0.25.0 §4.3).
# ==========================================================================
# The bakery from four to ten, while Maris is there (`npc_here`, which asks
# npc_sim, so a doom move or a pin moves the door with her). Two doors and the
# two stages before them:
#   H0  banking_the_fire's `taking_stock` (sets nf_hearth_taking_stock)
#   H1  The Hearth Holds (`stay`) or, its gate shut, the fail-forward's
#       morning door (`stay_anyway`); offered by beat-level `when:` (E5)
#   Q0  the_last_quiet_thing's `show_up`, in its own hours (4-7)
#   Q1  The Named Wound (`tell_her` / `say_nothing`)
# H1 is not dealt while The Named Wound is eligible, so H1 and Q1 never
# compete for one arming. REPEATABLE, draw 1: one card a morning, and "not
# yet" is never the last chance (director.rearm deals again on the next
# visit). The deck's `when:` ends in `any:` of its cards' own gates (plan
# decision 3), so it is due only when a card can be dealt. Each lock runs
# before its module; no beat carries more than four effects.
#
# Version: v0.1.0 [2026-10-27]

id: the_morning_firing
repeatable: true
draw: 1

when:
  all:
    - { at_location: edgewood_bakery }
    - { hour_between: [4, 10] }
    - { npc_here: npc_maris }
    - any:
        - all:
            - { quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, status: active } }
            - { quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, stage_at_least: 0 } }
            - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, stage_at_least: 1 } }]
        - all:
            - { quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, status: active } }
            - { quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, stage_at_least: 1 } }
            - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, stage_at_least: 2 } }]
            - { flag: nf_hearth_taking_stock }
            - none: [{ ending: { eligible: the_named_wound } }]
        - all:
            - { quest_state: { quest: the_last_quiet_thing, status: active } }
            - { quest_state: { quest: the_last_quiet_thing, stage_at_least: 0 } }
            - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: the_last_quiet_thing, stage_at_least: 1 } }]
            - { hour_between: [4, 7] }
        - { ending: { eligible: the_named_wound } }

cards:

  - id: H0_taking_stock
    title: "Taking stock"
    when:
      all:
        - { quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, status: active } }
        - { quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, stage_at_least: 0 } }
        - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, stage_at_least: 1 } }]
    tags: [hearth]
    text: >-
      INTENT: The oven ticking as it heats, Maris at the trough, and the
      player walking their life here end to end in their head: the larder,
      the woodpile, the names at every door between the well and the oven.
      CONSTRAINTS: Maris says little. Nothing is decided yet.
    beats:
      - id: take_stock
        text: >-
          Count it up while the oven comes to heat: the stores, the wood, the
          people who would notice if you were gone.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              Laid end to end it is not nothing. Laid end to end it looks a
              great deal like a life.
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_hearth_taking_stock }

  - id: H1_the_question
    title: "The question"
    when:
      all:
        - { quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, status: active } }
        - { quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, stage_at_least: 1 } }
        - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: banking_the_fire, stage_at_least: 2 } }]
        - { flag: nf_hearth_taking_stock }
        - none: [{ ending: { eligible: the_named_wound } }]
    tags: [menu, hearth, door]
    text: >-
      INTENT: The morning firing. Maris hands over the long peel and goes on
      shaping, and the question she stopped asking sits between them.
      CONSTRAINTS: She does not ask it aloud. Saying "I am staying" ends the
      story; letting the morning be a morning ends nothing.
      MENU -- resolve exactly the beat the player chose.
    beats:
      - id: stay
        label: "Tell her you are staying"
        when: { ending: { eligible: the_hearth_holds } }
        text: >-
          Tell Maris you are staying. This ends the story: The Hearth Holds.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              "I know," she says, the way she says the oven is hot.
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_hearth_book_closed }
              - { type: ending_lock, ending: the_hearth_holds }
              - { type: ending_module }
      - id: stay_anyway
        label: "Stay anyway"
        when: { none: [{ ending: { eligible: the_hearth_holds } }] }
        text: >-
          Stay anyway, knowing what you know, or knowing nobody yet. This ends
          the story: The Dark Keeps Its Hours.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              She nods once and hands you the peel, and the morning goes on
              being a morning because somebody has to make it one.
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_hearth_book_closed }
              - { type: ending_lock, ending: the_dark_keeps_its_hours }
              - { type: ending_module }
      - id: not_yet
        label: "Let the morning be a morning"
        text: >-
          Say nothing yet. Work the batch and keep the question.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              The batch comes out. The question stays where it was, warm.

  - id: Q0_show_up
    title: "Showing up"
    when:
      all:
        - { quest_state: { quest: the_last_quiet_thing, status: active } }
        - { quest_state: { quest: the_last_quiet_thing, stage_at_least: 0 } }
        - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: the_last_quiet_thing, stage_at_least: 1 } }]
        - { hour_between: [4, 7] }
    tags: [hearth]
    text: >-
      INTENT: Four in the morning, after everything, and Maris at the oven the
      way she is every morning. CONSTRAINTS: No ceremony. She hands over the
      peel without looking up.
    beats:
      - id: take_the_peel
        text: >-
          Take the long peel from her and stand where you always stand.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              There is no ceremony to it, and that is the mercy.
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_lastquiet_showed_up }

  - id: Q1_tell_her_or_dont
    title: "What you paid"
    when: { ending: { eligible: the_named_wound } }
    tags: [menu, hearth, door]
    text: >-
      INTENT: The last quiet morning's batch is out. Maris is shaping the
      next, and the player could tell her what naming the thing cost, or let
      the morning be a morning. CONSTRAINTS: She says "I know" either way.
      Both choices end the story.
      MENU -- resolve exactly the beat the player chose.
    beats:
      - id: tell_her
        label: "Tell her what you paid"
        text: >-
          Tell Maris what you paid. This ends the story: The Named Wound.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              "I know," she says, and goes on shaping. One turn and a press.
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_lastquiet_told_her }
              - { type: ending_lock, ending: the_named_wound }
              - { type: ending_module }
      - id: say_nothing
        label: "Say nothing, and work"
        text: >-
          Say nothing and work beside her. This ends the story: The Named Wound.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              She does not ask. One turn and a press. One turn and a press.
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_lastquiet_said_nothing }
              - { type: ending_lock, ending: the_named_wound }
              - { type: ending_module }
```

`data/scenes/the_count_kept.yaml` (same header shape: the shrine, while Wenna is there, Plan decision 2's hours):

```yaml
id: the_count_kept
repeatable: true
draw: 1

when:
  all:
    - { at_location: edgewood_shrine }
    - { npc_here: npc_wenna }
    - any:
        - { ending: { eligible: the_long_witness } }

cards:
  - id: W1_the_list
    title: "The list"
    when: { ending: { eligible: the_long_witness } }
    tags: [menu, witness, door]
    text: >-
      INTENT: Old Wenna at the shrine with her two candles, reading names off
      the folded list she keeps in her sleeve. She has seen the player
      writing in the evenings. She holds out her hand for theirs.
      CONSTRAINTS: She does not read it in front of them. Giving it ends the
      story; keeping it ends nothing.
      MENU -- resolve exactly the beat the player chose.
    beats:
      - id: give_it
        label: "Give Wenna your list"
        text: >-
          Give her the list you have been keeping. This ends the story: The
          Long Witness.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              She folds it in with hers without reading it, and lights a third
              candle, which she has never done.
            effects:
              - { type: ledger_fact, kind: event, text: "You gave old Wenna your list of wrong things, and she keeps it with hers." }
              - { type: ending_lock, ending: the_long_witness }
              - { type: ending_module }
      - id: keep_it
        label: "Keep it a while longer"
        text: >-
          Keep the list. There are more wrong things to write down.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              She nods as if that were also an answer, and goes back to her names.
```

The file header's "SHAPE OF THE FINALE" is rewritten: ten doors, each locking its ending by name (§4.2's table, one line each), no id-less lock, `score:` rows now only feeding the gallery's closeness band; the teases ship in the payload at every tier and are written to be safe there (§8.4).

```yaml
    - quest_completed: what_edgewood_keeps
```

(`the_sacrifice` and `the_heartland_road` now end the story at their own doors, so requiring either would make The Named Wound unreachable: §4.3.) Its header's "THE LAST DOOR" paragraph points at `Q1`.

- Modify: `games/clockwork-dark/data/rules/death.yaml` (`terminal.ending`)
- Modify: `games/clockwork-dark/data/world/schedules.yaml` (`the_last_hour`, Plan decision 15)
- Modify: `games/clockwork-dark/data/quests/convergence/the_sacrifice.yaml`, `the_ninth_pin.yaml` (bugs 1 and 2; the four narrative flags dropped, §4.1)
- Modify: `games/clockwork-dark/data/canon/state-dictionary.json` (the three `sacrifice_offered_*`)
- Modify: `tests/clockwork_doors.py` (`price_door_for(item)`, `pin_door`, `last_hour_door`, `second_death_door`)
- Test: `tests/test_clockwork_dark.py` (appended)
- Docs: story `CHANGELOG.md` (bugs 1-2), `docs/DESIGN.md` § Death (the terminal shows its epilogue)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T3 (E3, E5, E6), T5 (`the_join`), T6's decks and gates.
- Produces: decks `the_price` (`S0_understand`, `S1_choose_what` beats `offer_the_name`/`offer_the_pin`/`offer_the_shard`/`not_yet`, `S2_pay_it` beats `pay_the_name`/`pay_the_pin`/`pay_the_shard`/`not_yet`), `the_join` (`R2_walk_the_join`, `J1_the_pin` beats `set_it`/`keep_it`), `the_stopped_wheel` (`Z1_the_last_morning`, sequence); flags `sacrifice_offered_the_written_name`, `sacrifice_offered_ninth_pin`, `sacrifice_offered_brass_shard`; the declared event `the_last_hour`; `death.yaml` `terminal.ending: the_dark_keeps_its_hours`.

- [ ] **Step 1: The setups.** Append to `tests/clockwork_doors.py`:

```python
# -- the price (S1, S2) ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

OFFERED = {"the_written_name": "offer_the_name", "ninth_pin": "offer_the_pin", "brass_shard": "offer_the_shard"}
PAID = {"the_written_name": "pay_the_name", "ninth_pin": "pay_the_pin", "brass_shard": "pay_the_shard"}


def _with_ilya_in_the_square(state: Any) -> None:
    """Spreading (the vines have moved Ilya to the square), naming done; the square at ten."""
    from engine.world import world_effects

    state.evil_progress = 0.55                 # a world this far gone; its beats fired as the clock fires them
    world_effects.apply_pending_beats(state)
    effect(state, type="awareness", delta=60)
    completed(state, "naming_the_wound")
    stand(state, "edgewood_square", 10)


def price_door_for(item_id: str):
    """The Price Asked, paying with `item_id`: offered at S1 (its flag and nf_sacrifice_chosen), held, at pay_it."""
    def setup(state: Any) -> None:
        _with_ilya_in_the_square(state)
        effect(state, type="item", item_id=item_id, qty=1)
        at_stage(state, "the_sacrifice", 2)
        flag(state, "nf_sacrifice_understood")
        flag(state, "nf_sacrifice_chosen")
        flag(state, f"sacrifice_offered_{item_id}")
    setup.__name__ = f"price_door_paying_{item_id}"
    return setup


def at_pay_it_by_the_narrator(state: Any) -> None:
    """A v0.24.0-shaped run at pay_it: chosen through the narrator, nothing offered on a card, two things held."""
    _with_ilya_in_the_square(state)
    effect(state, type="item", item_id="the_written_name", qty=1)
    effect(state, type="item", item_id="brass_shard", qty=1)
    at_stage(state, "the_sacrifice", 2)
    flag(state, "nf_sacrifice_understood")
    flag(state, "nf_sacrifice_chosen")


# -- the join (J1) -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

def pin_door(state: Any) -> None:
    """The Pin and the Join: the pin taken and held, the join walked; standing at the join."""
    completed(state, "naming_the_wound")
    flag(state, "nf_ninth_taken")
    effect(state, type="item", item_id="ninth_pin", name="The ninth pin", qty=1, tags=["named", "unsettling"])
    at_stage(state, "the_heartland_road", 2)
    stand(state, "the_join", 12)


# -- the last hour (Z1) and the second death ----------------------------------------------------------

def last_hour_door(state: Any) -> None:
    """The Stopped Wheel: the reading at 1.0, then a day crossed, which fires `the_last_hour`."""
    state.evil_progress = 1.0
    fed_until(state, state.world_day + 1, 6)


def second_death_door(state: Any) -> None:
    """The fail-forward's terminal: consuming, and marked by a first death there already."""
    from engine.game import encounter

    state.evil_progress = 0.85
    effect(state, type="hp", delta=-int(state.stats.hp))
    first = encounter.check_death(state)
    assert first and not first.get("terminal")
```

(`state.evil_progress` is written directly ONLY to stand a world at a reading, as T0's fixtures do; `world_effects.apply_pending_beats` then fires the beats the clock would have.)

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests.** Append to `tests/test_clockwork_dark.py`:

```python
# -- doors II (T7, §4.2-§4.3, §6.3) ------------------------------------------------------------------

@pytest.mark.parametrize("item_id", ["the_written_name", "ninth_pin", "brass_shard"])
def test_s2_takes_exactly_the_thing_offered_and_the_price_is_asked(item_id: str) -> None:
    """Bug 2: the sacrifice took the written name whatever was chosen."""
    others = [i for i in doors.PAID if i != item_id]

    def setup(state):
        doors.price_door_for(item_id)(state)
        for other in others:
            doors.effect(state, type="item", item_id=other, qty=1)

    state = _drive("the_price_asked", Door.card("the_price", "S2_pay_it", doors.PAID[item_id]), setup)
    held = {i.id for i in state.inventory if i.qty > 0}
    assert item_id not in held and set(others) <= held


def test_s1_offers_one_beat_per_thing_carried(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.content import director

    state = new_state()
    doors.at_pay_it_by_the_narrator(state)        # a v0.24.0-shaped run: re-offered the choice (I1)
    assert _deal(state, "the_price") == ["S1_choose_what"]
    assert [o["id"] for o in director.options(state)] == ["offer_the_name", "offer_the_shard", "not_yet"]


def test_a_thing_offered_and_then_sold_is_offered_again(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.content import deck as deck_module

    state = new_state()
    doors.price_door_for("brass_shard")(state)
    doors.effect(state, type="remove_item", item_id="brass_shard", qty=1)
    doors.effect(state, type="item", item_id="the_written_name", qty=1)
    eligible, _ = deck_module.eligible_cards(state, deck_module.load_deck("the_price"))
    assert [c.id for c in eligible] == ["S1_choose_what"]


def test_the_pin_is_set_at_the_join_and_leaves_the_pack() -> None:
    state = _drive("the_pin_and_the_join", Door.card("the_join", "J1_the_pin", "set_it"), doors.pin_door)
    assert not any(i.id == "ninth_pin" and i.qty > 0 for i in state.inventory)


def test_the_last_hour_deals_the_last_morning() -> None:
    _drive("the_stopped_wheel", Door.card("the_stopped_wheel", "Z1_the_last_morning"), doors.last_hour_door)


def test_the_last_morning_waits_for_an_open_hand_then_is_dealt(flagship) -> None:
    """Review Focus 4: an encounter open at the crossing keeps the turn; the wheel's scene comes after."""
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game import encounter

    state = new_state()
    encounter.begin(state, "lost_child_at_the_treeline")
    doors.last_hour_door(state)
    assert director.ensure_scene(state) == []
    encounter.end(state)
    (receipt,) = director.ensure_scene(state)
    assert receipt["result"]["deck_id"] == "the_stopped_wheel"


def test_the_second_death_in_consuming_ends_in_the_fail_forward() -> None:
    _drive("the_dark_keeps_its_hours", Door.death(), doors.second_death_door)


def test_leaving_the_pin_grants_no_pin(flagship) -> None:
    """Bug 1: `take_it_or_dont`'s on_complete granted the pin either way."""
    from engine.game.quests import STATUS_COMPLETED, QuestEngine, progress_records

    state = new_state()
    state.arcs_unlocked.append("convergence")
    doors.completed(state, "naming_the_wound")
    doors.at_stage(state, "the_ninth_pin", 2)
    doors.flag(state, "nf_ninth_left")
    QuestEngine.evaluate(state)
    assert progress_records(state)["the_ninth_pin"].status == STATUS_COMPLETED
    assert not any(i.id == "ninth_pin" for i in state.inventory)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("quest_id, index, flag_id", [
    ("the_ninth_pin", 2, "nf_ninth_taken"),
    ("the_ninth_pin", 2, "nf_ninth_left"),
    ("the_sacrifice", 1, "nf_sacrifice_chosen"),
    ("the_sacrifice", 2, "nf_sacrifice_paid"),
])
def test_the_narrator_cannot_raise_a_card_carried_flag(flagship, quest_id, index, flag_id) -> None:
    from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine

    state = new_state()
    doors.at_stage(state, quest_id, index)
    assert flag_id not in QuestEngine.allowed_narrative_flags(state)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The three decks.** `data/scenes/the_price.yaml` (header: wherever Ilya is, while the sacrifice is open; S1 re-offers the choice to a run at `pay_it` with nothing offered, and to a player who offered a thing and then sold it, §4.3):

```yaml
id: the_price
repeatable: true
draw: 1

when:
  all:
    - { npc_here: npc_ilya }
    - { quest_state: { quest: the_sacrifice, status: active } }
    - any:
        - all:
            - { quest_state: { quest: the_sacrifice, stage_at_least: 0 } }
            - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: the_sacrifice, stage_at_least: 1 } }]
        - all:
            - { quest_state: { quest: the_sacrifice, stage_at_least: 1 } }
            - none:
                - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_the_written_name }, { has_item: { id: the_written_name, qty: 1 } }]
                - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_ninth_pin }, { has_item: { id: ninth_pin, qty: 1 } }]
                - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_brass_shard }, { has_item: { id: brass_shard, qty: 1 } }]
        - all:
            - { quest_state: { quest: the_sacrifice, stage_at_least: 2 } }
            - any:
                - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_the_written_name }, { has_item: { id: the_written_name, qty: 1 } }]
                - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_ninth_pin }, { has_item: { id: ninth_pin, qty: 1 } }]
                - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_brass_shard }, { has_item: { id: brass_shard, qty: 1 } }]

cards:

  - id: S0_understand
    title: "The price, written out"
    when:
      all:
        - { quest_state: { quest: the_sacrifice, stage_at_least: 0 } }
        - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: the_sacrifice, stage_at_least: 1 } }]
    tags: [price]
    text: >-
      INTENT: Ilya writes the requirement out rather than saying it. Not
      blood: something that was ordered and is now not. CONSTRAINTS: He does
      not soften it.
    beats:
      - id: read_it
        text: >-
          Read what he has written, twice, and do not look away from it.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: >-
              A like for a like. Something with the same shape as what it takes.
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_sacrifice_understood }

  - id: S1_choose_what
    title: "What you put on the table"
    when:
      all:
        - { quest_state: { quest: the_sacrifice, stage_at_least: 1 } }
        - none:
            - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_the_written_name }, { has_item: { id: the_written_name, qty: 1 } }]
            - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_ninth_pin }, { has_item: { id: ninth_pin, qty: 1 } }]
            - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_brass_shard }, { has_item: { id: brass_shard, qty: 1 } }]
    tags: [menu, price]
    text: >-
      INTENT: Ilya's table, cleared. The shapes it accepts are short: a name
      written down, the ninth pin, a shard of the brass. CONSTRAINTS: Only
      what the player is carrying can go on the table. Offering is not paying.
      MENU -- resolve exactly the beat the player chose.
    beats:
      - id: offer_the_name
        label: "Offer the written name"
        when: { has_item: { id: the_written_name, qty: 1 } }
        text: >-
          Put the written name on the table.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "He looks at it a long time without touching it."
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_sacrifice_chosen }
              - { type: flag, flag: sacrifice_offered_the_written_name }
      - id: offer_the_pin
        label: "Offer the ninth pin"
        when: { has_item: { id: ninth_pin, qty: 1 } }
        text: >-
          Put the ninth pin back on his table, which is not where he sold it to you.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "He does not say that he expected it. He did."
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_sacrifice_chosen }
              - { type: flag, flag: sacrifice_offered_ninth_pin }
      - id: offer_the_shard
        label: "Offer the brass shard"
        when: { has_item: { id: brass_shard, qty: 1 } }
        text: >-
          Put the shard of brass on the table.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "It lies there catching light it should not have."
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_sacrifice_chosen }
              - { type: flag, flag: sacrifice_offered_brass_shard }
      - id: not_yet
        label: "Not yet"
        text: >-
          Keep your hands in your lap a while longer.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "He waits, and does not fill the silence."

  - id: S2_pay_it
    title: "Paying it"
    when:
      all:
        - { quest_state: { quest: the_sacrifice, stage_at_least: 2 } }
        - any:
            - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_the_written_name }, { has_item: { id: the_written_name, qty: 1 } }]
            - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_ninth_pin }, { has_item: { id: ninth_pin, qty: 1 } }]
            - all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_brass_shard }, { has_item: { id: brass_shard, qty: 1 } }]
    tags: [menu, price, door]
    text: >-
      INTENT: What the player offered is on Ilya's table. Taking their hand
      off it is the whole of paying. CONSTRAINTS: No light, no sound. Paying
      ends the story; not yet ends nothing.
      MENU -- resolve exactly the beat the player chose.
    beats:
      - id: pay_the_name
        label: "Take your hand off the name"
        when:
          all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_the_written_name }, { has_item: { id: the_written_name, qty: 1 } }]
        text: >-
          Take your hand off the written name. This ends the story: The Price Asked.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "A table with nothing on it, and a valley that goes on being a valley."
            effects:
              - { type: remove_item, item_id: the_written_name, qty: 1 }
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_sacrifice_paid }
              - { type: ending_lock, ending: the_price_asked }
              - { type: ending_module }
      - id: pay_the_pin
        label: "Take your hand off the pin"
        when:
          all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_ninth_pin }, { has_item: { id: ninth_pin, qty: 1 } }]
        text: >-
          Take your hand off the ninth pin. This ends the story: The Price Asked.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "A table with nothing on it, and a valley that goes on being a valley."
            effects:
              - { type: remove_item, item_id: ninth_pin, qty: 1 }
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_sacrifice_paid }
              - { type: ending_lock, ending: the_price_asked }
              - { type: ending_module }
      - id: pay_the_shard
        label: "Take your hand off the shard"
        when:
          all: [{ flag: sacrifice_offered_brass_shard }, { has_item: { id: brass_shard, qty: 1 } }]
        text: >-
          Take your hand off the brass shard. This ends the story: The Price Asked.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "A table with nothing on it, and a valley that goes on being a valley."
            effects:
              - { type: remove_item, item_id: brass_shard, qty: 1 }
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_sacrifice_paid }
              - { type: ending_lock, ending: the_price_asked }
              - { type: ending_module }
      - id: not_yet
        label: "Not yet"
        text: >-
          Leave your hand where it is.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "Everything is negotiable until your hand comes off it."
```

`data/scenes/the_join.yaml`:

```yaml
id: the_join
repeatable: true
draw: 1

when:
  all:
    - { at_location: the_join }
    - any:
        - all:
            - { quest_state: { quest: the_heartland_road, status: active } }
            - { quest_state: { quest: the_heartland_road, stage_at_least: 1 } }
            - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: the_heartland_road, stage_at_least: 2 } }]
            - { hour_between: [10, 15] }
        - { ending: { eligible: the_pin_and_the_join } }

cards:
  - id: R2_walk_the_join
    title: "The join"
    when:
      all:
        - { quest_state: { quest: the_heartland_road, status: active } }
        - { quest_state: { quest: the_heartland_road, stage_at_least: 1 } }
        - none: [{ quest_state: { quest: the_heartland_road, stage_at_least: 2 } }]
        - { hour_between: [10, 15] }
    tags: [road]
    text: >-
      INTENT: Midday on the place where the road's two ends meet. Underfoot it
      is one road. CONSTRAINTS: Nothing moves that the player can see.
    beats:
      - id: walk_it
        text: >-
          Walk the seam where the hedgerows have grown together, end to end.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "Two places that were four miles apart last spring, and the ends match."
            effects:
              - { type: flag, flag: nf_road_walked }

  - id: J1_the_pin
    title: "Where it goes"
    when: { ending: { eligible: the_pin_and_the_join } }
    tags: [menu, road, door]
    text: >-
      INTENT: The join, and the ninth pin in the player's hand: the opposite
      of a ward, and this is where an opposite belongs. CONSTRAINTS: Setting
      it ends the story; keeping it ends nothing.
      MENU -- resolve exactly the beat the player chose.
    beats:
      - id: set_it
        label: "Set the pin into the join"
        text: >-
          Set the ninth pin into the join. This ends the story: The Pin and the Join.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "It goes in the way a nail goes into green wood, reluctant and then all at once."
            effects:
              - { type: ending_lock, ending: the_pin_and_the_join }
              - { type: remove_item, item_id: ninth_pin, qty: 1 }
              - { type: ending_module }
              - { type: ledger_fact, kind: secret, text: "You set the ninth pin into the join, and the road stopped getting shorter." }
      - id: keep_it
        label: "Keep it"
        text: >-
          Keep the pin in your pocket and walk on.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "The hedgerows shiver in no wind at all, and are still."
```
