```python
# -- the critical path, the levy, the bargain (T8, §4.3-§5.1) -------------------------------------

def test_every_deck_is_due_only_when_a_card_can_be_dealt() -> None:
    """Plan decision 3: a deck with a `when:` ends it with `any:` of its cards' own gates, in card order."""
    for path in sorted(SCENES.glob("*.yaml")):
        deck = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert deck.get("draw") == 1, path.name
        if "when" not in deck or path.stem == "the_press":
            continue
        tail = deck["when"]["all"][-1]
        assert list(tail) == ["any"], path.name
        assert tail["any"] == [card["when"] for card in deck["cards"]], path.name


def test_fourteen_decks_and_thirty_four_cards() -> None:
    decks = [yaml.safe_load(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) for p in SCENES.glob("*.yaml")]
    assert len(decks) == 14 and sum(len(d["cards"]) for d in decks) == 34


#: (deck, card, beat or "", quest, stage index, place, hour, extra setup, flag the beat writes)
def _rep(faction: str, n: int):
    return lambda s: doors.effect(s, type="reputation", faction=faction, delta=n)


def _item(item_id: str):
    return lambda s: doors.effect(s, type="item", item_id=item_id, qty=1)


def _phase(progress: float):
    def setup(s):
        from engine.world import world_effects

        s.evil_progress = progress
        world_effects.apply_pending_beats(s)
    return setup


def _pin_ilya(s):
    doors.flag(s, "npc_pin_npc_ilya")


def _pin_sera(s):
    doors.flag(s, "npc_pin_npc_sera")


CARDS = [
    ("the_bakehouse", "B1_ask_for_work", "", "bakery_apprentice", 0, "edgewood_bakery", 9, None, "nf_bakery_asked_for_work"),
    ("nessas_drawings", "N1_see_it", "", "childs_drawing", 0, "edgewood_square", 10, None, "nf_drawing_seen"),
    ("nessas_drawings", "N2_ask_her", "", "childs_drawing", 1, "edgewood_square", 10, None, "nf_drawing_asked"),
    ("nessas_drawings", "N3_keep_one", "", "childs_drawing", 2, "edgewood_bakery", 10, None, "nf_drawing_kept"),
    ("the_naming", "G1_lay_it_out", "", "naming_the_wound", 0, "edgewood_square", 20, None, "nf_naming_laid_it_out"),
    ("the_naming", "G2_say_it_aloud", "", "naming_the_wound", 1, "tinker_caravan", 20, _phase(0.55), "nf_naming_spoke_it"),
    ("the_naming", "G3_write_it_down", "", "naming_the_wound", 2, "edgewood_bakery", 20, None, "nf_naming_wrote_it_down"),
    ("the_village_meeting", "V1_call_the_meeting", "", "what_edgewood_keeps", 0, "edgewood_square", 12, None, "nf_edgewood_called_it"),
    ("the_village_meeting", "V2_the_vote", "split", "what_edgewood_keeps", 1, "edgewood_square", 12, None, "nf_edgewood_split"),
    ("the_village_meeting", "V3_warn_them", "", "conscription_list", 2, "edgewood_square", 12, _item("copied_list"), "nf_list_warned_edgewood"),
    ("the_levy_table", "L1_watch_them_choose", "", "militia_press", 0, "millhaven_gate", 9, None, "nf_press_watched"),
    ("the_levy_table", "L2_the_weller_boy", "speak_up", "militia_press", 1, "millhaven_gate", 9, None, "nf_press_spoke_up"),
    ("the_levy_table", "L3_get_near_it", "", "conscription_list", 0, "millhaven_gate", 9, None, "nf_list_glimpsed"),
    ("the_levy_table", "L5_ask_her_straight", "", "seras_compromise", 0, "millhaven_gate", 9, _pin_sera, "nf_sera_asked"),
    ("the_levy_table", "L6_hear_the_number", "", "seras_compromise", 1, "millhaven_gate", 9, _pin_sera, "nf_sera_heard_out"),
    ("the_levy_table", "L7_answer_her", "refuse", "seras_compromise", 2, "millhaven_gate", 9, _pin_sera, "nf_sera_refused"),
    ("the_ninth_pin", "T1_ask_for_it", "", "the_ninth_pin", 0, "tinker_caravan", 10, _pin_ilya, "nf_ninth_asked"),
    ("the_ninth_pin", "T2_pay_for_it", "", "the_ninth_pin", 1, "tinker_caravan", 10,
     lambda s: (_pin_ilya(s), _rep("tinkers", 10)(s)), "nf_ninth_earned"),
    ("the_ninth_pin", "T3_take_it_or_dont", "leave_it", "the_ninth_pin", 2, "tinker_caravan", 10, _pin_ilya, "nf_ninth_left"),
    ("the_inward_road", "R1_measure_it", "", "the_heartland_road", 0, "millhaven_gate", 12, _item("road_map"), "nf_road_measured"),
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("row", CARDS, ids=[r[1] for r in CARDS])
def test_each_critical_path_card_is_dealt_at_its_stage_and_writes_its_flag(flagship, row) -> None:
    from engine.content import deck as deck_module
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game.quests import evaluate_condition

    deck_id, card_id, beat, quest, index, place, hour, extra, flag_id = row
    state = new_state()
    doors.at_stage(state, quest, index)
    if extra is not None:
        extra(state)
    doors.stand(state, place, hour)
    deck = deck_module.load_deck(deck_id)
    assert evaluate_condition(state, deck.when), f"{deck_id} is not due at {place} at {hour}"
    eligible, rejected = deck_module.eligible_cards(state, deck)
    assert card_id in [c.id for c in eligible], rejected
    assert director.begin(state, deck_id, forced_card=card_id)["ok"]
    out = director.resolve(state, chosen=beat)
    assert out["ok"], out
    assert state.flags.get(flag_id), flag_id


def test_l4_copies_the_list_on_a_pass_and_costs_standing_on_a_fail(flagship, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """A stealth roll (`checks.resolve`, which a beat's skill gate calls), forced both ways."""
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game import checks, reputation

    for passed in (True, False):
        state = new_state()
        doors.at_stage(state, "conscription_list", 1)
        doors.stand(state, "millhaven_gate", 9)
        outcome = SimpleNamespace(skill="stealth", difficulty="standard", dc=12, total=12 if passed else 4,
                                  margin=0, degree="success" if passed else "failure", summary="",
                                  success=passed)
        monkeypatch.setattr(checks, "resolve", lambda *a, outcome=outcome, **k: outcome)
        before = reputation.get(state, "militia")
        assert director.begin(state, "the_levy_table", forced_card="L4_copy_it")["ok"]
        assert director.resolve(state, chosen="")["ok"]
        assert bool(state.flags.get("nf_list_copied")) is passed
        assert reputation.get(state, "militia") == (before if passed else before - 4)


def test_the_pin_is_earned_by_the_tinkers_standing_not_a_disposition(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine, progress_records

    state = new_state()
    state.arcs_unlocked.append("convergence")
    doors.completed(state, "naming_the_wound")
    doors.at_stage(state, "the_ninth_pin", 1)
    doors.flag(state, "nf_ninth_earned")
    doors.stand(state, "tinker_caravan", 10)
    QuestEngine.evaluate(state)
    assert progress_records(state)["the_ninth_pin"].stage_index == 1     # tinkers 0
    doors.effect(state, type="reputation", faction="tinkers", delta=10)
    QuestEngine.evaluate(state)
    assert progress_records(state)["the_ninth_pin"].stage_index == 2


def test_the_levy_fills_on_its_days_and_forces_the_press_in_the_square(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game import clocks
    from engine.world.world_sim import WorldSim

    state = new_state()
    doors.effect(state, type="awareness", delta=25)
    doors.fed_until(state, 3, 9)
    WorldSim.on_tick(state, hours=0.0, force=["militia_press"])
    from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine

    QuestEngine.evaluate(state)                                   # records the press as seen
    doors.fed_until(state, 3 + 9, 9)
    assert clocks.value_of(state, "the_levy") == 4                # +1 seen, then days 3, 6, 9
    doors.fed_until(state, 3 + 15, 7)
    assert clocks.value_of(state, "the_levy") == 6
    doors.stand(state, "edgewood_square", 9)
    deck_id, _card, source = director.due(state)
    assert (deck_id, source) == ("the_press", "forced")


def test_the_press_never_schedules_itself(flagship) -> None:
    """Plan decision 4: in the square in its hours, with no levy owed, it is not due."""
    from engine.content import director

    state = new_state()
    doors.stand(state, "edgewood_square", 9)
    assert director.due(state)[0] != "the_press"


def _strike(state):
    """`strike_bargain`'s own two steps: offer, then seal (engine/skills/builtin/scenes.py)."""
    from engine.game import threads

    assert not threads.strike_refusal(state, "seras_bargain")
    threads.seal(state, threads.offer(state, "seras_bargain"))
    return threads.active(state)[-1]["id"]


def test_seras_bargain_strikes_discharges_on_the_wall_and_breaks_on_its_day(flagship) -> None:
    import json

    from engine.game import threads

    state = new_state()
    doors.at_stage(state, "conscription_list", 0)
    doors.stand(state, "millhaven_gate", 9)
    tid = _strike(state)
    doors.stand(state, "millhaven_gate", 23)
    assert threads.discharge(state, tid)["ok"]
    assert state.flags.get("stood_the_wall")
    broken = new_state()
    doors.at_stage(broken, "conscription_list", 0)
    doors.stand(broken, "millhaven_gate", 9)
    tid = _strike(broken)
    doors.fed_until(broken, broken.world_day + 4, 9)
    assert threads.get(broken, tid)["status"] == threads.STATUS_BROKEN
    assert "night on the wall" in json.dumps(threads.get(broken, tid)) + json.dumps(broken.moved)
```

(`clocks.value_of` is the clock module's reader; a thread's id is its record's `id`.)

```yaml
# The Clockwork Dark -- one progress clock (v0.25.0 §5.1): Sera's levy.
# +1 the first time the press is seen, then +1 at 3, 6, 9, 12 and 15 days after
# it: full at fifteen days, when it forces `the_press` in the square. First
# values, UNMEASURED until T10 (rule 10); the levy's band is written from T10's
# first measurement. A v0.24.0 save whose first press is fifteen or more days
# old fills on its first turn and is dealt the press in the square in its hours
# (accepted: the levy has been due for weeks in that world; pinned by save 8).
#
# Version: v0.1.0 [2026-10-27]

version: 1

clocks:
  the_levy:
    label: "How long Sera's list has been growing"
    advance_when:
      - { id: levy_first_press, when: { event_seen: militia_press }, by: 1 }
      - { id: levy_day_3, when: { days_since_event: { event: militia_press, days: 3 } }, by: 1 }
      - { id: levy_day_6, when: { days_since_event: { event: militia_press, days: 6 } }, by: 1 }
      - { id: levy_day_9, when: { days_since_event: { event: militia_press, days: 9 } }, by: 1 }
      - { id: levy_day_12, when: { days_since_event: { event: militia_press, days: 12 } }, by: 1 }
      - { id: levy_day_15, when: { days_since_event: { event: militia_press, days: 15 } }, by: 1 }
    beats:
      - id: levy_second_page
        at: 3
        text: "Sera's clerk has started a second page."
      - id: levy_full
        at: max
        forces_scene: the_press
        text: "Sera's list has an Edgewood name on it."
```

`data/scenes/the_press.yaml` (not repeatable; Plan decision 4):

```yaml
id: the_press
draw: 1

when:
  all:
    - { forced_scene: the_press }
    - { at_location: edgewood_square }
    - { hour_between: [8, 18] }

cards:
  - id: P1_a_name_on_the_list
    title: "A name on the list"
    tags: [menu, levy]
    text: >-
      INTENT: The square, the levy table, and Sergeant Venn reading a name off
      her list: a boy the player knows, too young for the march. CONSTRAINTS:
      She reads it twice. Nobody else steps forward.
      MENU -- resolve exactly the beat the player chose.
    beats:
      - id: speak_for_him
        label: "Speak for him"
        text: >-
          Speak for the boy, in front of everyone. A persuasion roll, and a hard one.
        gate:
          check: { skill: persuasion, difficulty: hard }
          on_pass:
            text: "Sera crosses him off, slowly, and writes something else in the margin."
            effects:
              - { type: reputation, faction: militia, delta: -4 }
              - { type: reputation, faction: edgewood, delta: 6 }
          on_fail:
            text: "She hears you out and does not cross him off."
            effects:
              - { type: reputation, faction: militia, delta: -6 }
      - id: hide_him
        label: "Get him out of sight"
        text: >-
          Get the boy out of the square before his name is called again. A stealth roll.
        gate:
          check: { skill: stealth, difficulty: standard }
          on_pass:
            text: "By the time the name is read a third time there is nobody there to answer it."
            effects:
              - { type: reputation, faction: edgewood, delta: 4 }
              - { type: ledger_fact, kind: event, text: "You hid a boy from Sera's levy, and the square saw you do it." }
          on_fail:
            text: "A corporal has him by the collar before you reach the lane."
            effects:
              - { type: reputation, faction: militia, delta: -10 }
      - id: let_it_be
        label: "Let it be"
        text: >-
          Let the list be read. It is the levy, and it is the law.
        gate:
          on_pass:
            text: "He goes. His mother does not look at you, which is worse than if she had."
            effects:
              - { type: reputation, faction: edgewood, delta: -6 }
              - { type: reputation, faction: militia, delta: 4 }
```

`data/rules/threads.yaml`:

```yaml
# The Clockwork Dark -- one thread (v0.25.0 §5.1): Sergeant Venn's bargain. A
# night on the wall for a name off her list. Struck at the gate in the morning
# while the conscription list is open; discharged at the gate at night; broken
# on its day if you never came. First values, unmeasured until T10.
#
# Version: v0.1.0 [2026-10-27]

version: 1

tags:
  - Contract

templates:
  seras_bargain:
    title: "Sergeant Venn's bargain"
    source: npc_sera
    tags: [Contract]
    terms: >-
      A night on the wall, any night inside three days, and one Edgewood name
      comes off the list. She does not say which. She does not need to.
    due_in_days: 3
    requires:
      all:
        - { quest_state: { quest: conscription_list, status: active } }
        - { at_location: millhaven_gate }
        - { hour_between: [8, 11] }
    on_seal:
      - { type: ledger_fact, kind: event, text: "Sergeant Venn has your name down for a night on the wall, and an Edgewood name off her list for it." }
```

```python
#: Clauses that cannot fail while their ending's `requires` holds (each row comment says why).
UNFAILABLE = {"hue-and-cry": {("cleared", "no_wrong_name_standing")}, "clockwork-dark": set()}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("row", UNEARNED_DOORS,
                         ids=[f"{r.slug}:{r.ending_id}:{r.door.label()}:{r.setup.__name__.strip('_')}"
                              for r in UNEARNED_DOORS])
def test_an_unearned_ending_cannot_lock_at_its_door(row: UnearnedDoor) -> None:
    from endings_driver import drive_ending

    _activate(row.slug)
    try:
        from engine.game import endings
        from engine.game.procgen import new_game_state

        state = new_game_state(seed=row.seed, location_id=row.entry)
        with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match=row.match):
            drive_ending(None, row.ending_id, row.door, row.setup, state=state)
        assert endings.locked(state) == endings.NONE_ID
        report = endings.eligible(state)
        assert row.ending_id not in report.eligible
        body = endings.declared()[row.ending_id]
        kinds = dict(_gate_clause_ids(body))
        expected = []
        for clause in _clauses(row):
            reason = str(body["lock_reasons"][clause])
            expected.append(f"cannot complete: {reason}" if kinds[clause] else reason)
        assert report.locked[row.ending_id] == expected, (row.clause, report.locked[row.ending_id])
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("slug", sorted(COMPLETE_DOORS))
def test_every_gate_clause_has_an_unearned_row(slug: str) -> None:
    """Every clause of every earned ending has a row, but the ones `UNFAILABLE` names."""
    _activate(slug)
    try:
        from engine.game import endings

        wanted = {(ending_id, clause)
                  for ending_id, body in endings.declared().items()
                  if ending_id != endings.fail_forward_id()
                  for clause, _ in _gate_clause_ids(body)}
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
    covered = {(r.ending_id, c) for r in UNEARNED_DOORS if r.slug == slug for c in _clauses(r)}
    assert wanted - covered == UNFAILABLE[slug], sorted(wanted - covered)
    assert covered <= wanted, sorted(covered - wanted)
```

```python
# -- unearned (v0.25.0 §12.1): each walks a real door with ONE clause unmet --------------------------

def hearth_unplaced(state: Any) -> None:            # a_place_here: no quiet-life quest done
    stay_anyway_door(state)


def hearth_larder_unwalked(state: Any) -> None:     # larder_kept: H0 never answered
    completed(state, "bakery_apprentice")
    at_stage(state, "banking_the_fire", 1)
    stand(state, "edgewood_bakery", 5)


def hearth_knowing(state: Any) -> None:              # unknowing: awareness past 40
    hearth_door(state)
    effect(state, type="awareness", delta=41)


def hearth_consumed(state: Any) -> None:             # village_stands: the world consuming
    hearth_door(state)
    state.evil_progress = 0.85


def _witness_but(state: Any, *, awareness: int = 50, saints: int = 30, day: int = 27,
                 drawing: bool = False, doom: float = 0.0) -> None:
    effect(state, type="awareness", delta=awareness)
    effect(state, type="reputation", faction="unnamed_saints", delta=saints)
    if drawing:
        completed(state, "childs_drawing")
    fed_until(state, day, 12)
    stand(state, "edgewood_shrine", 18)
    if doom:
        state.evil_progress = doom


def witness_blind(state: Any) -> None:               # eyes_open: awareness 20 (listened by the drawing)
    _witness_but(state, awareness=20, drawing=True)


def witness_deaf(state: Any) -> None:                # listened: awareness 40, no whisper quest
    _witness_but(state, awareness=40)


def witness_uncounted(state: Any) -> None:           # counted
    _witness_but(state, saints=0)


def witness_early(state: Any) -> None:               # stayed_three_counts: day 20
    _witness_but(state, day=20)


def witness_too_late(state: Any) -> None:            # world_still_turning
    _witness_but(state, doom=1.0)


def _march_but(state: Any, *, arc: bool = True, militia: int = 20, served: bool = True,
               marked: bool = False) -> None:
    from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine

    effect(state, type="reputation", faction="militia", delta=militia)
    if served:
        completed(state, "militia_press")
    if marked:
        flag(state, "saints_marked_you")
    if arc:
        stand(state, "millhaven_gate", 9)
        QuestEngine.evaluate(state)
    else:
        stand(state, "edgewood_square", 18)          # Sera's notice-board hours; the gate never visited


def march_unmarched(state: Any) -> None:
    _march_but(state, arc=False)


def march_uncounted(state: Any) -> None:
    _march_but(state, militia=10)


def march_unserved(state: Any) -> None:
    _march_but(state, served=False)


def march_marked(state: Any) -> None:
    _march_but(state, marked=True)


def _named_but(state: Any, *, named: bool = True, decided: bool = True, stage: int = 2, doom: float = 0.0) -> None:
    if named:
        completed(state, "naming_the_wound")
    if decided:
        completed(state, "what_edgewood_keeps")
    at_stage(state, "the_last_quiet_thing", stage)
    stand(state, "edgewood_bakery", 5)
    if doom:
        state.evil_progress = doom


def named_unnamed(state: Any) -> None:
    _named_but(state, named=False)


def named_undecided(state: Any) -> None:
    _named_but(state, decided=False)


def named_unbaked(state: Any) -> None:
    _named_but(state, stage=1)


def named_too_late(state: Any) -> None:
    _named_but(state, doom=1.0)


def price_nothing_open(state: Any) -> None:          # named, chose, paid: no sacrifice, nothing named
    _with_ilya_in_the_square(state)
    from engine.game.quests import progress_records

    state.quests.pop("naming_the_wound", None)
    assert "naming_the_wound" not in progress_records(state)


def price_nothing_offered(state: Any) -> None:       # chose, paid: at choose_what, the name held, not offered
    _with_ilya_in_the_square(state)
    effect(state, type="item", item_id="the_written_name", qty=1)
    at_stage(state, "the_sacrifice", 1)
    flag(state, "nf_sacrifice_understood")


def _pin_but(state: Any, *, taken: bool = True, held: bool = True, stage: int = 2, doom: float = 0.0) -> None:
    completed(state, "naming_the_wound")
    if taken:
        flag(state, "nf_ninth_taken")
    if held:
        effect(state, type="item", item_id="ninth_pin", name="The ninth pin", qty=1, tags=["named", "unsettling"])
    at_stage(state, "the_heartland_road", stage)
    stand(state, "the_join", 12)
    if doom:
        state.evil_progress = doom


def pin_untaken(state: Any) -> None:
    _pin_but(state, taken=False)


def pin_spent(state: Any) -> None:
    _pin_but(state, held=False)


def pin_unwalked(state: Any) -> None:                # join_walked: R2 is dealt, J1 is not
    _pin_but(state, stage=1)


def pin_too_late(state: Any) -> None:
    _pin_but(state, doom=1.0)


def wheel_still_turning(state: Any) -> None:         # wheel_stopped: 0.9, no last hour
    state.evil_progress = 0.9
```

A row whose setup moves the hour or the doom so that the person has left, or another clause fails as well, is fixed by its SETUP (stand at the person's hour; meet every other clause), never by widening `clause`. The `hearth_larder_unwalked` row: with `nf_hearth_taking_stock` unset at stage 1, no card of `the_morning_firing` is eligible, so its `when:` fails (Plan decision 3).

- [ ] **Step 4: The card-only walks (§4.4, §12.2).** Create `tests/clockwork_walks.py`:

```python
"""
The Clockwork Dark's card-only walks (v0.25.0 §4.4, §12.2): for each ending, a
run from a new game to its lock in which no narrative flag is raised by the
narrator (`QuestEngine.set_narrative_flag` patched to fail by the test) and no
disposition is written. Every stage completes by a dealt card, a place, an
hour, an item, a day, a stat or an engine-written standing.

A WALK is the turn's own order around the narration -- quests, the deal, each
dealt card answered, quests again -- an hour at a time, the player fed through
`apply_effect` (survival is not what is being walked). A stat or a standing the
spec's path earns by work or play (awareness from what the player looks at,
the saints' standing from the shrine shift, the tinkers' from Ilya's) is
written as the effect that work writes. Time moves only through
`advance_time`; an event the path needs that the calendar would bring (the
press) is forced through the world tick's own force hook.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

#: Every door card's NON-locking answer: a walk never locks by an unlisted card.
DEFAULT_ANSWERS = {
    "H1_the_question": "not_yet", "W1_the_list": "keep_it", "M1_strike_the_name": "not_yet",
    "S1_choose_what": "not_yet", "S2_pay_it": "not_yet", "J1_the_pin": "keep_it",
    "L2_the_weller_boy": "speak_up", "V2_the_vote": "stay", "L7_answer_her": "refuse",
    "T3_take_it_or_dont": "leave_it", "P1_a_name_on_the_list": "let_it_be",
}

#: Door decks with a person (§6.6), for the presence sweep.
DOOR_DECKS = {
    "the_morning_firing": "npc_maris", "the_count_kept": "npc_wenna",
    "the_muster_roll": "npc_sera", "the_price": "npc_ilya",
}


class Walk:
    """One run from a new game, played by cards, places, hours, items, days and engine standings only."""

    def __init__(self, seed: int = 7, answers: Optional[dict[str, str]] = None) -> None:
        from engine.game.procgen import new_game_state
        from engine.memory.ledger import StoryLedger

        self.state = new_game_state(seed=seed)
        self.ledger = StoryLedger()
        self.answers = {**DEFAULT_ANSWERS, **(answers or {})}
        self.dealt: list[str] = []

    def give(self, **row: Any) -> None:
        from engine.game.effects import apply_effect

        apply_effect(self.state, dict(row))

    def _fed(self) -> None:
        if self.state.hunger > 0:
            self.give(type="hunger", delta=-self.state.hunger)
        if self.state.stats.hp < self.state.stats.max_hp:
            self.give(type="hp", delta=self.state.stats.max_hp - self.state.stats.hp)
        if self.state.stats.stamina < self.state.stats.max_stamina // 2:
            self.give(type="stamina", delta=self.state.stats.max_stamina)

    def turn(self) -> None:
        from engine.content import director
        from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine

        QuestEngine.evaluate(self.state, self.ledger)
        director.ensure_scene(self.state, ledger=self.ledger)
        while director.active(self.state):
            card = director.current_card(self.state)
            offered = [o["id"] for o in director.options(self.state)]
            chosen = self.answers.get(card.id, "")
            if chosen not in offered:
                chosen = offered[-1] if card.id in DEFAULT_ANSWERS else ""
            out = director.resolve(self.state, chosen=chosen, ledger=self.ledger)
            assert out.get("ok"), out
            self.dealt.append(card.id)
        QuestEngine.evaluate(self.state, self.ledger)

    def until(self, condition: Any, *, at: str, hours: tuple[int, int] = (0, 24), max_days: int = 40) -> None:
        """Stand at `at`, a turn an hour inside `hours`, until `condition` holds."""
        from engine.game.clock import advance_time
        from engine.game.quests import evaluate_condition

        deadline = self.state.world_day + max_days
        while not evaluate_condition(self.state, condition, ledger=self.ledger):
            assert self.state.world_day <= deadline, f"never held in {max_days} days: {condition} at {at}"
            self._fed()
            start, end = hours
            hour = self.state.world_hour
            inside = start <= hour < end if start <= end else (hour >= start or hour < end)
            if inside:
                self.state.location_id = at
                self.turn()
                advance_time(self.state, 1.0)
            else:
                advance_time(self.state, float((start - hour) % 24))


def _stage(quest: str, index: int) -> dict:
    return {"quest_state": {"quest": quest, "stage_at_least": index}}


def _done(quest: str) -> dict:
    return {"quest_completed": quest}


def _locked(ending: str) -> dict:
    return {"ending": {"locked": ending}}


def hearth(w: Walk) -> None:
    w.until(_stage("bakery_apprentice", 1), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(7, 11))
    w.until(_stage("bakery_apprentice", 2), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 7))
    w.until(_done("bakery_apprentice"), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(8, 11))
    w.give(type="item", item_id="winter_ration", qty=1)                       # 22 gold, economy.yaml
    w.until({"quest_state": {"quest": "banking_the_fire", "status": "active"}}, at="edgewood_bakery")
    w.until(_stage("banking_the_fire", 1), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 10))
    w.until(_locked("the_hearth_holds"), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 10))


def stay_anyway(w: Walk) -> None:
    w.give(type="awareness", delta=41)                                        # the Hearth's gate shut
    w.give(type="item", item_id="winter_ration", qty=1)
    w.until({"quest_state": {"quest": "banking_the_fire", "status": "active"}}, at="edgewood_square")
    w.until(_stage("banking_the_fire", 1), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 10))
    w.until(_locked("the_dark_keeps_its_hours"), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 10))


def witness(w: Walk) -> None:
    w.give(type="awareness", delta=30)
    w.until({"arc_unlocked": "whisper"}, at="edgewood_square", max_days=30)
    w.until(_stage("childs_drawing", 1), at="edgewood_square", hours=(9, 17))
    w.until(_stage("childs_drawing", 2), at="edgewood_square", hours=(9, 17))
    w.until(_done("childs_drawing"), at="edgewood_square", hours=(9, 17))
    w.give(type="reputation", faction="unnamed_saints", delta=30)            # the shrine's shifts (+3 each)
    w.until({"min_day": 27}, at="edgewood_square", max_days=30)
    w.until(_locked("the_long_witness"), at="edgewood_shrine", hours=(18, 22))


def march(w: Walk) -> None:
    from engine.world.world_sim import WorldSim

    w.give(type="awareness", delta=20)
    w.until({"arc_unlocked": "march"}, at="millhaven_gate", hours=(8, 12))
    WorldSim.on_tick(w.state, hours=0.0, force=["militia_press"])            # the press, by the force hook
    w.until(_stage("militia_press", 1), at="edgewood_square", hours=(9, 12))
    w.until(_stage("militia_press", 2), at="edgewood_square", hours=(9, 12))
    w.until(_done("militia_press"), at="edgewood_square", hours=(9, 12))
    w.give(type="reputation", faction="militia", delta=20)                   # the gate's shifts
    w.until(_locked("the_counted_march"), at="millhaven_gate", hours=(8, 12))


def _naming(w: Walk) -> None:
    w.give(type="awareness", delta=52)
    w.give(type="item", item_id="brass_shard", qty=1)                        # the works' find
    w.until(_stage("naming_the_wound", 1), at="edgewood_square", hours=(19, 23))
    w.until({"min_phase": "spreading"}, at="edgewood_bakery", max_days=60)
    w.until(_stage("naming_the_wound", 2), at="edgewood_square", hours=(19, 23))
    w.until(_done("naming_the_wound"), at="edgewood_square", hours=(19, 23))


def named_wound(w: Walk) -> None:
    _naming(w)
    w.give(type="awareness", delta=10)                                        # 62
    w.until(_stage("what_edgewood_keeps", 1), at="edgewood_square", hours=(10, 16))
    w.until(_stage("what_edgewood_keeps", 2), at="edgewood_square", hours=(10, 16))
    w.until(_done("what_edgewood_keeps"), at="edgewood_square", hours=(10, 16))
    w.until(_stage("the_last_quiet_thing", 1), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 7))
    w.give(type="craft", delta=1)                                             # 11
    w.until(_stage("the_last_quiet_thing", 2), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(8, 12))
    w.until(_locked("the_named_wound"), at="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 10))


def price(w: Walk) -> None:
    _naming(w)
    w.give(type="awareness", delta=6)                                         # 58
    w.until(_stage("the_sacrifice", 1), at="edgewood_square", hours=(10, 15))
    w.until(_stage("the_sacrifice", 2), at="edgewood_square", hours=(10, 15))
    w.until(_locked("the_price_asked"), at="edgewood_square", hours=(10, 15))


def pin(w: Walk) -> None:
    _naming(w)
    w.give(type="awareness", delta=8)                                         # 60
    w.until(_stage("the_ninth_pin", 1), at="tinker_caravan", hours=(9, 15))
    w.give(type="reputation", faction="tinkers", delta=10)                   # Ilya's shifts and trade
    w.until(_stage("the_ninth_pin", 2), at="tinker_caravan", hours=(9, 15))
    w.until(_done("the_ninth_pin"), at="tinker_caravan", hours=(9, 15))
    w.give(type="item", item_id="road_map", qty=1)                           # 15 gold, economy.yaml
    w.until(_stage("the_heartland_road", 1), at="millhaven_gate", hours=(10, 15))
    w.until(_stage("the_heartland_road", 2), at="the_join", hours=(10, 15))
    w.until(_locked("the_pin_and_the_join"), at="the_join", hours=(10, 15))


def stopped_wheel(w: Walk) -> None:
    w.until(_locked("the_stopped_wheel"), at="edgewood_bakery", max_days=120)


#: ending id -> (walk, its answers).
WALKS: dict[str, tuple[Callable[[Walk], None], dict[str, str]]] = {
    "the_hearth_holds": (hearth, {"H1_the_question": "stay"}),
    "the_dark_keeps_its_hours": (stay_anyway, {"H1_the_question": "stay_anyway"}),
    "the_long_witness": (witness, {"W1_the_list": "give_it"}),
    "the_counted_march": (march, {"M1_strike_the_name": "strike_it"}),
    "the_named_wound": (named_wound, {"Q1_tell_her_or_dont": "tell_her"}),
    "the_price_asked": (price, {"S1_choose_what": "offer_the_shard", "S2_pay_it": "pay_the_shard"}),
    "the_pin_and_the_join": (pin, {"T3_take_it_or_dont": "take_it", "J1_the_pin": "set_it"}),
    "the_stopped_wheel": (stopped_wheel, {}),
}
```

```python
# -- card-only walks, presence, the static door check (T9, §12.2) -------------------------------------

from tests import clockwork_walks  # noqa: E402


@pytest.mark.parametrize("ending_id", sorted(clockwork_walks.WALKS))
def test_each_ending_is_reached_by_cards_alone(flagship, monkeypatch, ending_id: str) -> None:
    from engine.game import endings, epilogue
    from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine
    from engine.memory.ledger import StoryLedger

    def no_narrator(*_a, **_k):
        raise AssertionError("a narrative flag was raised by the narrator")

    def no_disposition(*_a, **_k):
        raise AssertionError("a disposition was written")

    monkeypatch.setattr(QuestEngine, "set_narrative_flag", staticmethod(no_narrator))
    monkeypatch.setattr(StoryLedger, "adjust_disposition", no_disposition)
    walk, answers = clockwork_walks.WALKS[ending_id]
    w = clockwork_walks.Walk(seed=7, answers=answers)
    walk(w)
    assert endings.locked(w.state) == ending_id
    assert epilogue.for_state(w.state).ending_id == ending_id


def test_every_door_deck_with_a_person_reads_npc_here() -> None:
    for deck_id, npc_id in clockwork_walks.DOOR_DECKS.items():
        deck = yaml.safe_load((SCENES / f"{deck_id}.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert {"npc_here": npc_id} in deck["when"]["all"], deck_id


DOOR_PLACES = {
    "the_morning_firing": ["edgewood_bakery"], "the_count_kept": ["edgewood_shrine"],
    "the_muster_roll": ["millhaven_gate", "edgewood_square"],
    "the_price": ["edgewood_square", "tinker_caravan", "millhaven_gate", "edgewood_bakery"],
}


@pytest.mark.slow
@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(10))
def test_a_door_deck_is_due_only_where_its_person_is(flagship, seed: int) -> None:
    """§12.2: every hour of days 1-60, with the tinker's camp, the press, the pin and the 0.50 and 0.88
    moves each driven: the deck's person/place gate holds only where npc_sim places that person."""
    from engine.content import deck as deck_module
    from engine.game.clock import advance_time
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.game.quests import evaluate_condition
    from engine.world import npc_sim, world_effects
    from engine.world.world_sim import WorldSim

    state = new_state(seed)
    gates = {d: {"all": deck_module.load_deck(d).when["all"][:-1]} for d in clockwork_walks.DOOR_DECKS}
    # `the_price` also reads the sacrifice's status, which nothing here opens: hold its person clause alone.
    gates["the_price"] = {"all": [{"npc_here": "npc_ilya"}]}
    for _ in range(60 * 24):
        day = state.world_day
        if day == 10 and state.world_hour == 0:
            WorldSim.on_tick(state, hours=0.0, force=["tinker_camp"])
        if day == 15 and state.world_hour == 0:
            WorldSim.on_tick(state, hours=0.0, force=["militia_press"])
        if day == 20 and state.world_hour == 0:
            apply_effect(state, {"type": "flag", "flag": "npc_pin_npc_ilya", "value": True})
        if day == 30 and state.world_hour == 0:
            apply_effect(state, {"type": "flag", "flag": "npc_pin_npc_ilya", "value": False})
            state.evil_progress = max(state.evil_progress, 0.5)
            world_effects.apply_pending_beats(state)
        if day == 45 and state.world_hour == 0:
            state.evil_progress = max(state.evil_progress, 0.88)
            world_effects.apply_pending_beats(state)
        for deck_id, npc_id in clockwork_walks.DOOR_DECKS.items():
            for place in DOOR_PLACES[deck_id]:
                state.location_id = place
                if evaluate_condition(state, gates[deck_id]):
                    presence = npc_sim.resolve_npc(state, npc_id)
                    assert presence.location_id == place and presence.available, (deck_id, day, state.world_hour)
        if state.hunger > 0:
            apply_effect(state, {"type": "hunger", "delta": -state.hunger})
        advance_time(state, 1.0)
```

```python
# -- v0.25.0 assertions (plan T9) ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _play(state, place: str = "", hour: int = -1) -> None:
    """Stand somewhere (at an hour), then what a turn does around the narration."""
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine
    from tests.clockwork_doors import stand

    if place:
        stand(state, place, hour) if hour >= 0 else setattr(state, "location_id", place)
    QuestEngine.evaluate(state)
    director.ensure_scene(state)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", NAMES)
def test_each_save_plays_a_turn_saves_and_reloads(flagship, name: str, tmp_path) -> None:
    from engine.game.state import GameState
    from engine.persistence.saves import SaveStore

    state = load_fixture(name)
    _play(state)
    store = SaveStore(root=tmp_path / "saves")
    save_id = store.save(state)
    again, _memory = store.load(save_id)
    assert isinstance(again, GameState) and again.world_day == state.world_day


def test_save_2_keeps_its_caravan_and_odran(flagship) -> None:
    """Review Focus 5: a legacy-shaped caravan (goods, npc_id): Odran still in the square, no second caravan."""
    from engine.game.clock import advance_time
    from engine.world import npc_sim

    state = load_fixture("2_caravan_active")
    first = [e for e in state.world_events if e["event_id"] == "caravan_arrival"]
    assert first and "goods" in first[0]["payload"]
    state.location_id = "edgewood_square"
    assert npc_sim.resolve_npc(state, "npc_odran").location_id == "edgewood_square"
    expires = int(first[0]["expires_day"])
    advance_time(state, 24.0 * (expires - state.world_day) - 1.0)
    assert [e["day"] for e in state.world_events if e["event_id"] == "caravan_arrival"] == [first[0]["day"]]


def test_save_3_knows_the_works_and_save_4_is_offered_the_seam(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.game import locations

    assert locations.is_known(load_fixture("3_seam_solved"), "the_works")
    unsolved = load_fixture("4_seam_unsolved")
    assert not locations.is_known(unsolved, "the_works") and unsolved.flags.get("tunnels_open")


def test_save_5_is_dealt_h1_at_five_with_maris(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.content import director

    state = load_fixture("5_at_the_question")
    _play(state, "edgewood_bakery", 5)
    assert director.current_card(state).id == "H1_the_question"


def test_save_7_is_re_offered_the_choice_then_pays(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game import endings

    state = load_fixture("7_at_pay_it_by_narrator")
    # A v0.24.0 world past 0.50 fired the vines before a doom move placed anyone (plan decision 1):
    # Ilya keeps his routine, the caravan at ten.
    _play(state, "tinker_caravan", 10)
    assert director.current_card(state).id == "S1_choose_what"
    assert director.resolve(state, chosen="offer_the_shard")["ok"]
    _play(state, "edgewood_bakery", 9)                     # walk out: the deck re-arms
    _play(state, "tinker_caravan", 10)
    assert director.current_card(state).id == "S2_pay_it"
    assert director.resolve(state, chosen="pay_the_shard")["ok"]
    assert endings.locked(state) == "the_price_asked"


def test_save_8_fills_the_levy_and_is_dealt_the_press(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.content import director
    from engine.game import clocks
    from engine.game.clock import advance_time

    state = load_fixture("8_old_press")
    advance_time(state, 1.0)                                # the first turn's clock pass
    assert clocks.value_of(state, "the_levy") == 6
    _play(state, "edgewood_square", 9)
    assert director.current_card(state).id == "P1_a_name_on_the_list"


def test_save_9_keeps_its_ending(flagship) -> None:
    from engine.game import endings

    state = load_fixture("9_locked_hearth")
    _play(state)
    assert endings.locked(state) == "the_hearth_holds"
```

- [ ] **Step 1: The harness.** Create `scripts/simulate_doors.py`:

```python
"""
The doors harness (v0.25.0 §10.1; rule 10).

One policy per door, the same seeds, the flagship's real code. A policy
ANSWERS DEALT CARDS through `director.resolve` (the `card` verb's own path,
never `set_narrative_flag` for a card-carried stage), raises any OTHER
pending narrative flag as `scripts/simulate.py` does (the narrator's stand-in
for the side quests), works, eats, rests and travels as `cautious` does, and
stops at a lock or the cap. The deck walker (`simulate_decks.py`) is not asked:
it never travels, and every flagship deck is gated by a place.

Per policy it reports: the share of runs reaching its OWN ending, ANOTHER, or
NONE by the cap; turns, in-game days and evil_progress at the lock (median);
deaths and starvation deaths; and the cards dealt and answered. CARD COVERAGE
is the union over every policy of the cards dealt and answered: every one of
the 34 (§4.3) at least once.

    python scripts/simulate_doors.py --seeds 40
    python scripts/simulate_doors.py --policy namer --seeds 3 --json
    python scripts/simulate.py --policy doors --seeds 40

Version: v0.1.0 [2026-10-27]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import statistics
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

_SCRIPTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(_SCRIPTS) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_SCRIPTS))

import simulate as sim  # noqa: E402

from engine import yamlio  # noqa: E402
from engine.content import director  # noqa: E402
from engine.game import economy as economy_module  # noqa: E402
from engine.game import endings  # noqa: E402
from engine.game import trade as trade_module  # noqa: E402
from engine.game.quests import QuestEngine, evaluate_condition  # noqa: E402
from engine.games.validation import flags_written  # noqa: E402
from engine.world import npc_sim  # noqa: E402

FLAGSHIP = "clockwork-dark"
CAP_TURNS = 1000
ROOT = _SCRIPTS.parent
SCENES = ROOT / "games" / FLAGSHIP / "data" / "scenes"
#: Where an item a goal buys is sold (economy.yaml).
BUY_FROM = {"winter_ration": "npc_maris", "road_map": "npc_odran"}
#: Every door card's NON-locking answer (a policy locks only by its own door).
SAFE = {
    "H1_the_question": "not_yet", "W1_the_list": "keep_it", "M1_strike_the_name": "not_yet",
    "S1_choose_what": "not_yet", "S2_pay_it": "not_yet", "J1_the_pin": "keep_it",
    "T3_take_it_or_dont": "leave_it", "L2_the_weller_boy": "speak_up", "V2_the_vote": "stay",
    "L7_answer_her": "refuse", "P1_a_name_on_the_list": "let_it_be",
}


def card_flags() -> frozenset[str]:
    """Every flag a flagship deck beat writes: never raised as a 'pending narrative flag'."""
    out: set[str] = set()
    for path in sorted(SCENES.glob("*.yaml")):
        out |= flags_written(yamlio.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {})
    return frozenset(out)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Goal:
    """Pursue until `done` holds: be at `place` (or wherever `npc` is) inside `hours`; there,
    work (`work=True`) or wait; or, with `buy`, buy that item from its vendor."""

    done: Any
    place: str = ""
    npc: str = ""
    hours: tuple[int, int] = (0, 24)
    work: bool = False
    buy: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DoorPolicy:
    ending: str
    goals: tuple[Goal, ...]
    answers: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    #: Arcs whose pending narrative flags this policy raises ("" = all).
    flag_arcs: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    #: `simulate.py`'s upkeep style; "reckless" walks the dangerous road and takes no goal.
    style: str = "cautious"


def _stage(quest: str, index: int) -> dict:
    return {"quest_state": {"quest": quest, "stage_at_least": index}}


def _done(quest: str) -> dict:
    return {"quest_completed": quest}


def _locked(ending: str) -> dict:
    return {"ending": {"locked": ending}}


NAMING = (
    Goal(_stage("naming_the_wound", 1), place="edgewood_square", hours=(19, 23)),
    Goal({"min_phase": "spreading"}, place="edgewood_bakery", work=True),
    Goal(_stage("naming_the_wound", 2), place="edgewood_square", hours=(19, 23)),
    Goal(_done("naming_the_wound"), place="edgewood_square", hours=(19, 23)),
)

POLICIES: dict[str, DoorPolicy] = {
    "baker": DoorPolicy("the_hearth_holds", (
        Goal(_stage("bakery_apprentice", 1), place="edgewood_bakery", hours=(7, 11)),
        Goal(_stage("bakery_apprentice", 2), place="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 7)),
        Goal(_done("bakery_apprentice"), place="edgewood_bakery", hours=(8, 11), work=True),
        Goal({"has_item": {"id": "winter_ration", "qty": 1}}, buy="winter_ration"),
        Goal({"quest_state": {"quest": "banking_the_fire", "status": "active"}}, place="edgewood_bakery", work=True),
        Goal(_stage("banking_the_fire", 1), place="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 10)),
        Goal(_locked("the_hearth_holds"), place="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 10)),
    ), {"H1_the_question": "stay"}, flag_arcs=("quiet_life",)),
    "settler": DoorPolicy("the_dark_keeps_its_hours", (
        Goal({"min_awareness": 41}, place="edgewood_square", work=True),
        Goal({"has_item": {"id": "winter_ration", "qty": 1}}, buy="winter_ration"),
        Goal({"quest_state": {"quest": "banking_the_fire", "status": "active"}}, place="edgewood_square", work=True),
        Goal(_stage("banking_the_fire", 1), place="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 10)),
        Goal(_locked("the_dark_keeps_its_hours"), place="edgewood_bakery", hours=(4, 10)),
    ), {"H1_the_question": "stay_anyway"}),
    "witness": DoorPolicy("the_long_witness", (
        Goal({"arc_unlocked": "whisper"}, place="edgewood_square", work=True),
        Goal(_done("childs_drawing"), place="edgewood_square", hours=(9, 17)),
        Goal({"reputation": {"faction": "unnamed_saints", "min": 30}}, place="edgewood_shrine", work=True),

    ), {"W1_the_list": "give_it"}),

    ), {"S1_choose_what": "offer_the_name", "S2_pay_it": "pay_the_name"}),

class DoorRun(sim.Simulation):
    """One policy, one seed, to a lock or the cap."""

    def __init__(self, policy: str, seed: int, cap: int = CAP_TURNS) -> None:
        self.door = POLICIES[policy]
        super().__init__(policy="reckless" if self.door.style == "reckless" else "cautious",
                         seed=seed, turns=cap)
        self.policy = self._decide
        self.door_name = policy
        self.answers = {**SAFE, **self.door.answers}
        self.card_flags = card_flags()
        self.dealt: set[str] = set()
        self.answered: set[str] = set()
        self.lock: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None

    # -- the turn ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def run(self) -> sim.RunResult:
        original_resolve, original_death = self._install_probes()
        try:
            for _ in range(self.turns):
                self._one_turn()
                self._deal_and_answer()
                if self._locked_now() or self.state.ended:
                    break
        finally:
            sim.checks_module.resolve = original_resolve
            sim.encounter_module.check_death = original_death
        return self.result

    def _deal_and_answer(self) -> None:
        director.ensure_scene(self.state, ledger=self.ledger)
        while director.active(self.state):
            card = director.current_card(self.state)
            self.dealt.add(card.id)
            offered = [o["id"] for o in director.options(self.state)]
            chosen = self.answers.get(card.id, "")
            if chosen not in offered:
                chosen = offered[-1] if card.id in SAFE else ""
            out = director.resolve(self.state, chosen=chosen, ledger=self.ledger)
            if out.get("ok"):
                self.answered.add(card.id)
            else:
                self.result.errors.append(f"card {card.id} refused {chosen!r}: {out.get('error')}")
                director.end(self.state)
        QuestEngine.evaluate(self.state, self.ledger)

    def _locked_now(self) -> bool:
        locked = endings.locked(self.state)
        if locked == endings.NONE_ID:
            return False
        if self.lock is None:
            self.lock = {"ending": locked, "turn": self.state.turn_number, "day": self.state.world_day,
                         "evil_progress": round(self.state.evil_progress, 4)}
        return True

    # -- the decision --------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _decide(self, state: Any) -> sim.Action:
        upkeep = sim._common_upkeep(state, self.door.style)
        if upkeep:
            return upkeep
        if state.stats.stamina < 50:
            return ("rest", {"kind": "sleep_bed"})
        flag_id = self._pending_flag(state)
        if flag_id:
            return ("flag", {"flag_id": flag_id})
        if self.door.style == "reckless":
            return sim.policy_reckless(state)
        goal = next((g for g in self.door.goals
                     if not evaluate_condition(state, g.done, ledger=self.ledger)), None)
        if goal is None:
            return ("wait", {"hours": 1.0})
        if goal.buy:
            return self._buying(state, goal.buy)
        if state.stats.gold < 4:
            earn = self._earn(state)
            if earn:
                return earn
        place = goal.place or self._where(goal.npc)
        if place and state.location_id != place:
            hops = sim.route(state.location_id, place)
            if hops:
                return ("travel", {"to": hops[0]})
        start, end = goal.hours
        hour = state.world_hour
        inside = start <= hour < end if start <= end else (hour >= start or hour < end)
        if inside and goal.work:
            job = self._job_here(state)
            if job:
                return ("work", {"job_id": job})
        return ("wait", {"hours": 1.0})

    def _pending_flag(self, state: Any) -> str:
        allowed = QuestEngine.allowed_narrative_flags(state)
        for definition, record in QuestEngine.active_quests(state):
            if self.door.flag_arcs and str(definition.get("arc")) not in self.door.flag_arcs:
                continue
            stage = QuestEngine.current_stage(definition, record) or {}
            for flag_id in [*(definition.get("narrative_flags") or []), *(stage.get("narrative_flags") or [])]:
                if flag_id in allowed and flag_id not in self.card_flags and not state.flags.get(flag_id):
                    return str(flag_id)
        return ""

    def _where(self, npc_id: str) -> str:
        presence = npc_sim.resolve_npc(self.state, npc_id) if npc_id else None
        return presence.location_id if presence is not None else ""

    def _job_here(self, state: Any) -> str:
        rows = economy_module.available(state, state.location_id)
        return str(rows[0]["id"]) if rows else ""

    def _earn(self, state: Any) -> Optional[sim.Action]:
        job = self._job_here(state)
        if job:
            return ("work", {"job_id": job})
        hops = sim.route(state.location_id, "edgewood_bakery")
        return ("travel", {"to": hops[0]}) if hops else None

    def _buying(self, state: Any, item_id: str) -> sim.Action:
        vendor = BUY_FROM[item_id]
        listing = trade_module.browse(state, vendor)
        row = next((r for r in (listing.get("sells") or []) if r.get("item_id") == item_id), None)
        if row is None or not row.get("affordable"):
            return self._earn(state) or ("wait", {"hours": 1.0})
        place = self._where(vendor)
        if place and state.location_id != place:
            hops = sim.route(state.location_id, place)
            if hops:
                return ("travel", {"to": hops[0]})
        return ("buy", {"npc_id": vendor, "item_id": item_id, "qty": 1})


def run_policy(name: str, seeds: list[int], cap: int = CAP_TURNS) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """One policy over the seeds: its row of the table."""
    own = other = none = deaths = starved = 0
    turns: list[int] = []
    days: list[int] = []
    evil: list[float] = []
    dealt: set[str] = set()
    answered: set[str] = set()
    errors: list[str] = []
    ending = POLICIES[name].ending
    for seed in seeds:
        run = DoorRun(name, seed, cap)
        run.run()
        deaths += run.result.deaths
        starved += run.result.starvation_deaths
        dealt |= run.dealt
        answered |= run.answered
        errors += run.result.errors[:3]
        if run.lock is None:
            none += 1
            continue
        if run.lock["ending"] == ending:
            own += 1
            turns.append(run.lock["turn"])
            days.append(run.lock["day"])
            evil.append(run.lock["evil_progress"])
        else:
            other += 1
    n = len(seeds)
    return {
        "policy": name, "ending": ending, "runs": n,
        "own": round(own / n, 3), "other": round(other / n, 3), "none": round(none / n, 3),
        "median_turns": statistics.median(turns) if turns else None,
        "median_day": statistics.median(days) if days else None,
        "median_evil": statistics.median(evil) if evil else None,
        "deaths": deaths, "starvation_deaths": starved,
        "dealt": sorted(dealt), "answered": sorted(answered), "errors": errors[:10],
    }


def main(argv: Optional[list[str]] = None) -> int:
    from engine.games import registry

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="The Clockwork Dark's doors harness (v0.25.0 §10.1)")
    parser.add_argument("--policy", default="all", help=f"{', '.join(POLICIES)} or all")
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, default=40)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0, help="the first seed")
    parser.add_argument("--cap", type=int, default=CAP_TURNS)
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    names = list(POLICIES) if args.policy == "all" else [args.policy]
    if any(n not in POLICIES for n in names):
        parser.error(f"--policy: one of {', '.join(POLICIES)} or all")
    seeds = list(range(args.seed, args.seed + args.seeds))
    registry.activate(FLAGSHIP)
    try:
        rows = [run_policy(name, seeds, args.cap) for name in names]
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
    all_cards = sorted(c["id"] for p in sorted(SCENES.glob("*.yaml"))
                       for c in (yamlio.safe_load(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}).get("cards", []))
    covered = sorted(set().union(*(set(r["answered"]) for r in rows)))
    report = {"seeds": [seeds[0], seeds[-1]], "cap": args.cap, "policies": rows,
              "coverage": {"cards": len(all_cards), "answered": len(covered),
                           "missing": sorted(set(all_cards) - set(covered))}}
    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    else:
        for r in rows:
            print(f"{r['policy']:9} own {r['own']:.0%} other {r['other']:.0%} none {r['none']:.0%} "
                  f"turns {r['median_turns']} day {r['median_day']} evil {r['median_evil']} "
                  f"deaths {r['deaths']} (starved {r['starvation_deaths']})")
        print(f"coverage: {len(covered)}/{len(all_cards)}; missing {report['coverage']['missing']}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
def measure_policy_table(policies: list[str], seeds: list[int], turns: int, tick_hours: float) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """DESIGN.md § EvilTicker's table over seeds: per policy, the medians of each seed's report."""
    out: dict[str, Any] = {"seeds": [seeds[0], seeds[-1]], "turns": turns, "policies": {}}
    for name in policies:
        reports = [simulate(policy=name, seed=s, turns=turns, tick_hours=tick_hours) for s in seeds]
        hours = [float(r["summary"]["in_game_hours_per_turn"]) for r in reports]
        evil = [float(r["summary"]["final_evil_progress"]) for r in reports]
        out["policies"][name] = {
            "hours_per_turn": round(statistics.median(hours), 2),
            "evil_progress": round(statistics.median(evil), 3),
            "deaths": sum(int(r["summary"]["deaths"]) for r in reports),
            "starvation_deaths": sum(int(r["summary"]["starvation_deaths"]) for r in reports),
        }
    return out
```

```python
"""The doors harness (v0.25.0 §12.6): three seeds per policy reach their own ending. `slow`: balance."""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def _doors():
    import sys

    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "scripts"))
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("simulate_doors", ROOT / "scripts" / "simulate_doors.py")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


@pytest.mark.slow
@pytest.mark.parametrize("policy", ["baker", "settler", "witness", "marcher", "namer", "payer", "pinner", "drifter"])
def test_three_seeds_reach_their_own_ending(policy: str) -> None:
    from engine.games import registry

    doors = _doors()
    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        row = doors.run_policy(policy, [0, 1, 2])
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
    assert row["own"] == 1.0 and row["other"] == 0.0, row


@pytest.mark.slow
def test_the_reckless_die_into_the_fail_forward_and_see_its_card() -> None:
    from engine.games import registry

    doors = _doors()
    registry.activate("clockwork-dark")
    try:
        row = doors.run_policy("reckless", [0, 1, 2])
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
    assert row["own"] >= 1 / 3 and row["other"] == 0.0, row
```

```js
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { routeOverlayAct } from "../src/core/overlayAct.js";

describe("an overlay's act", () => {
  it("sends a string as typed text (NEON CITY's and the Garden's overlays)", () => {
    const sendText = vi.fn();
    const sendIntent = vi.fn();
    routeOverlayAct("I look at the paper", { sendText, sendIntent });
    expect(sendText).toHaveBeenCalledWith("I look at the paper");
    expect(sendIntent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("sends an intent object as an overlay action", () => {
    const sendText = vi.fn();
    const sendIntent = vi.fn();
    routeOverlayAct({ action: "eat", target: "loaf", label: "Eat: Loaf" }, { sendText, sendIntent });
    expect(sendIntent).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ action: "eat", target: "loaf", label: "Eat: Loaf" });
    expect(sendText).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("drops anything else", () => {
    const sendText = vi.fn();
    const sendIntent = vi.fn();
    routeOverlayAct({ target: "loaf" }, { sendText, sendIntent });
    routeOverlayAct(null, { sendText, sendIntent });
    expect(sendText).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(sendIntent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```jsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
/**
 * The flagship's overlays press intents (v0.25.0 §7.3, §8.1): each button is an `{action, target}` the
 * server lists (`/api/intents/overlay`); a pair not listed is drawn and not pressable; while a scene owns
 * the turn nothing is pressable; Examine stays typed text; Offer and Drop are gone.
 */
import React from "react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { button, click, mount } from "./helpers/dom.js";
import Inventory from "../src/stories/clockwork-dark/parts/Inventory.jsx";
import Notices from "../src/stories/clockwork-dark/screens/Notices.jsx";
import Trade from "../src/stories/clockwork-dark/screens/Trade.jsx";

const ITEMS = {
  items: [
    { id: "loaf", name: "Loaf", tags: ["food"], carried: 2, description: "", weight: 0.5, value: 2 },
    { id: "wool_cloak", name: "Wool cloak", tags: ["apparel"], carried: 1, description: "", weight: 2, value: 8 },
    { id: "poultice", name: "Poultice", tags: ["tool"], carried: 1, description: "", weight: 0.1, value: 3 },
  ],
};
const OFFER = {
  scene_owns_turn: false,
  actions: {
    eat: [{ target: "loaf", label: "Loaf" }],
    equip: [{ target: "wool_cloak", label: "Wool cloak", slot: "back" }],
    unequip: [], use: [], buy: [{ target: "npc_maris/loaf", label: "Loaf from Maris, 2c" }],
    sell: [], craft: [], work: [{ target: "bakery_morning", label: "Morning at the ovens" }],
  },
};
const TRADE = { vendors: [{ npc_id: "npc_maris", name: "Maris", sells: [
  { id: "loaf", item_id: "loaf", name: "Loaf", price: 2, qty: 1 },
  { id: "flour", item_id: "flour", name: "Flour", price: 9, qty: 1 }], buys: [] }] };
const NOTICES = { here: [{ id: "bakery_morning", name: "Morning at the ovens", expected_wage: 3, hours: 4 }],
                  elsewhere: [{ id: "gate_watch", name: "Gate watch", location_id: "millhaven_gate", hours: 6 }] };

function serve(offer = OFFER) {
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(async (url) => {
    const path = String(url).split("?")[0];
    const body = { "/api/intents/overlay": offer, "/api/items": ITEMS, "/api/recipes": { recipes: [] },
                   "/api/trade": TRADE, "/api/notices": NOTICES, "/api/codex/places": { places: [] } }[path] || {};
    return { ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => body };
  });
}

});
afterEach(() => view.unmount());

});
```

```js
/** What an overlay may press now (v0.25.0 §6.4): `{scene_owns_turn, actions: {action: [{target, label, slot?}]}}`. */
export const fetchOverlayIntents = (sessionId) => getJSON("/api/intents/overlay", { session_id: sessionId });
```

`ui/src/core/overlayAct.js`:

```js
/**
 * An overlay's act (v0.25.0 §7.4): a string is the player's typed words (NEON CITY's paper and the
 * Garden's contracts overlay still send those); an `{action, target}` is an intent the server checks
 * against its overlay list under the turn lock. Anything else is dropped.
 */
export function routeOverlayAct(act, { sendText, sendIntent }) {
  if (typeof act === "string") {
    if (act.trim()) sendText(act);
    return;
  }
  if (act && typeof act.action === "string" && typeof act.target === "string") {
    sendIntent({ action: act.action, target: act.target, label: act.label || "" });
  }
}
```

`ui/src/core/hooks/useOverlayIntents.js`:

```js
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { fetchOverlayIntents } from "../api.js";

const NONE = { scene_owns_turn: true, actions: {} };

/**
 * What an overlay may press now, fetched when it opens and again whenever `turnKey` changes (a turn
 * arrived, a refusal came back, a turn started or ended). Until the first answer nothing is pressable.
 */
export function useOverlayIntents(sessionId, turnKey) {
  const [offer, setOffer] = useState(NONE);
  const [nudge, setNudge] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    if (!sessionId) return undefined;
    fetchOverlayIntents(sessionId)
      .then((next) => live && next && setOffer({ scene_owns_turn: Boolean(next.scene_owns_turn), actions: next.actions || {} }))
      .catch(() => live && setOffer(NONE));
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [sessionId, turnKey, nudge]);
  const refresh = useCallback(() => setNudge((n) => n + 1), []);
  return { offer, refresh };
}

/** A story overlay's turn key from the play state: a new turn, a busy flip, a new error. */
export const overlayTurnKey = (state) =>
  `${state?.world?.turn_number ?? ""}|${state?.busy ? 1 : 0}|${state?.error || ""}`;

/** The offer's row for a pair, or null. */
export const offerRow = (offer, action, target) =>
  (offer?.actions?.[action] || []).find((row) => row.target === target) || null;
```

```js
  // An overlay's intent (v0.25.0 §7.1): emitted as `overlay_action`; the server re-checks it against its
  // overlay list under the turn lock, and a pair no longer on offer comes back as a stale refusal.
  const sendOverlay = useCallback(
    ({ action, target, label }) => {
      if (!controlsLive(state) || !state.sessionId) return;
      const emit = (spoken) => {
        turnSent();
        phoneUpRefusal.current = false;
        dispatch({ type: "SUBMIT", text: spoken });
        socketRef.current?.emit("overlay_action", {
          session_id: sessionRef.current || state.sessionId,
          intent: { action, target },
          label: label || "",
        });
      };
      again.current = () => emit("");
      emit(label || "");
    },
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [state.link, state.busy, state.sessionId]
  );
```

```jsx
/**
 * One overlay action (v0.25.0 §8.1): pressable only when the server lists the pair and no scene owns the
 * turn. `aria-disabled`, never `disabled` (focus stays), while a turn runs or the pair is not on offer.
 */
import React from "react";
import { offerRow } from "@core/hooks/useOverlayIntents.js";

export default function ActionButton({ offer, action, target, busy, onAct, why = "", children }) {
  const row = offerRow(offer, action, target);
  const live = Boolean(row) && !offer?.scene_owns_turn && !busy;
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className="btn btn--sm"
      aria-disabled={live ? undefined : "true"}
      title={!row && why ? why : undefined}
      onClick={() => {
        if (live) onAct({ action, target, label: row.label });
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

/** The line every overlay shows while a scene owns the turn. */
export const SceneOwnsTurn = ({ offer }) =>
  offer?.scene_owns_turn ? <p className="overlay__hold">Answer what is in front of you first.</p> : null;
```

```jsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
/** The flagship's encounter (v0.25.0 §8.2): digits bind only when the choice row is empty, never to an unlit plate. */
import React from "react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { key, mount } from "./helpers/dom.js";
import EncounterPanel from "../src/stories/clockwork-dark/parts/EncounterPanel.jsx";

const ENCOUNTER = {
  id: "the_toll_men", round: 0, resolved: false,
  intro: "Two men and a chain across the road.",
  threat: { name: "The toll men", resolve: 3, resolve_max: 3 },
  approaches: [
    { id: "pay", text: "Pay the toll", skill: "persuasion", difficulty: "easy", auto: false, cost_gold: 2 },
    { id: "talk", text: "Talk past them", skill: "persuasion", difficulty: "standard", auto: false, cost_gold: 0 },
    { id: "around", text: "Go round", skill: "stealth", difficulty: "standard", auto: false, cost_gold: 0 },
  ],
};
const offered = (ids) => new Map(ids.map((id) => [id, { id: `c-${id}`, intent: { action: "encounter", target: id } }]));

let view;
beforeEach(() => {
  view = mount();
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: false, status: 404, json: async () => ({}) }));
});
afterEach(() => view.unmount());

const render = (props) => view.render(
  <EncounterPanel encounter={ENCOUNTER} world={{}} phase="stirring" sceneImage="" busy={false} {...props} />);

describe("the encounter's keys and look", () => {
  it("binds 1-9 to the pressable approaches in order when the choice row is empty", () => {
    const onTake = vi.fn();
    render({ offered: offered(["pay", "around"]), onTake, choiceRowEmpty: true });
    key("2");
    expect(onTake).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ENCOUNTER.approaches[2]);
    expect(view.host.querySelectorAll(".approach__key").length).toBe(2);
  });
  it("binds nothing while other choices are on screen", () => {
    const onTake = vi.fn();
    render({ offered: offered(["pay", "around"]), onTake, choiceRowEmpty: false });
    key("1");
    expect(onTake).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("draws an unoffered approach unlit, unnumbered and not pressable", () => {
    const onTake = vi.fn();
    render({ offered: offered(["pay"]), onTake, choiceRowEmpty: true });
    const unlit = view.host.querySelectorAll(".approach.is-unlit");
    expect(unlit.length).toBe(2);
    unlit.forEach((plate) => {
      expect(plate.getAttribute("aria-disabled")).toBe("true");
      expect(plate.querySelector(".approach__key")).toBeNull();
      expect(plate.textContent).toContain("not offered this turn");
    });
  });
  it("binds nothing while a turn runs", () => {
    const onTake = vi.fn();
    render({ offered: offered(["pay"]), onTake, choiceRowEmpty: true, busy: true });
    key("1");
    expect(onTake).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```jsx
...
  const pressable = encounter.approaches.filter((a) => offered.has(a.id));
  useDigitKeys(pressable, (approach) => onTake(approach), Boolean(choiceRowEmpty) && !busy);
```

`Approach` takes `index` (its position in `pressable`, or -1) and renders, when offered and `choiceRowEmpty`, `<span className="approach__key" aria-hidden="true">{index + 1}</span>` first; an unoffered one gets `className="approach is-unlit"`, `aria-disabled="true"` and its tag "not offered this turn" (today's words), no key. `theme/phases.css` defines, per phase, `--approach-unlit-border` and `--approach-unlit-ink` (the muted ink), and `clockwork-dark.css`:

```css
.approach.is-unlit { border-style: dashed; border-color: var(--approach-unlit-border); color: var(--approach-unlit-ink); }
.approach.is-unlit:hover { transform: none; box-shadow: none; }
.approach__key { display: inline-grid; place-items: center; min-width: 1.4em; margin-right: 0.5em;
  border: 1px solid currentColor; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 0.8em; }
@media (max-width: 899px) { .encounter__art { aspect-ratio: 4 / 1; } .encounter { flex-direction: column; } }
@media (max-height: 420px) { .encounter__log > :nth-last-child(n + 3) { display: none; } }
```

(the class names are the panel's own: read `EncounterPanel.jsx` and use them). `ui/tests/contrast.test.js` gains, in its existing pattern, the pairs `--approach-unlit-border` against the card at 3:1 and `--approach-unlit-ink` against the card at 4.5:1 for each of the four phases.

- [ ] **Step 3: The sheet (§8.3).** `Sheet.jsx`'s Standing rows: `const [factions, setFactions] = useState([])`; an effect keyed on `sessionId` and `JSON.stringify(world.reputations || {})` calls `fetchFactions(sessionId).then((rows) => setFactions(Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []))`; each row renders `row.name` and `row.band.label` with the score as today; with no answer yet (or a failed fetch) the rows fall back to today's ids and numbers.

- [ ] **Step 4: "The ways it ends" (§8.4).** `screens/Ends.jsx`:

```jsx
/**
 * "The ways it ends" (v0.25.0 §8.4): only the doors open now -- tier `unlocked` -- with the title, the
 * tease (which names a place and a person) and the closeness band; once a run is locked, that ending.
 * Never a locked or silhouette row, and the fail-forward (unlocked from turn one, no `requires`) only
 * once it is the locked ending. THE HIDING IS COSMETIC: the payload's endings block ships every tier
 * (engine/game/endings.py::to_client; docs/GOVERNANCE.md NOT WIRED), and the flagship's teases are
 * written to be safe there.
 */
import React from "react";
import Modal from "@core/parts/Modal.jsx";

export function openDoors(endings) {
  const rows = endings?.gallery || [];
  const locked = endings?.locked && endings.locked !== "none" ? endings.locked : "";
  if (locked) return rows.filter((row) => row.id === locked);
  return rows.filter((row) => row.tier === "unlocked" && row.id !== endings?.fail_forward);
}

export default function Ends({ state, onClose }) {
  const rows = openDoors(state?.world?.endings);
  return (
    <Modal title="The ways it ends" onClose={onClose}>
      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="overlay__empty">Nothing here is decided yet.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="ends">
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <li key={row.id} className="ends__row">
              <h3 className="ends__title">{row.title}</h3>
              {row.tease && <p className="ends__tease">{row.tease}</p>}
              <span className="pill">{row.closeness}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

(`world.endings` is where the turn payload carries `endings.to_client`; if the reducer stores it elsewhere, read it from there.) `index.jsx`'s `overlays` gains `{ id: "ends", key: "e", label: "The ways it ends", Icon: JournalIcon, Component: Ends }`.

- [ ] **Step 5: The people strip (§8.5) and the ending's plate (§8.6).** `game.yaml` `ui:` gains `panels: [{id: people, region: ledger}]`. Core's `Ending.jsx` takes `story.EndingArt`:

```jsx
    case "gallery_unlock":
      if (story?.EndingArt) return <story.EndingArt ending={ending} />;
      return ending.gallery_key ? (
        <p className="finale__gallery">
          Added to the gallery: <code>{ending.gallery_key}</code>
        </p>
      ) : null;
```

(`story.js`'s contract gains the line "`EndingArt({ending})`: draws the ending's gallery plate in place of core's gallery line (optional; v0.25.0 §8.6)", and `ui/tests/plugin-contract.test.js` its optional-field row.) `parts/EndingPlate.jsx`:

```jsx
/** The ending's plate (v0.25.0 §8.6): the `gallery` art kind (E7), keyed by the epilogue's gallery_key. */
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import PaintFrame from "@core/parts/PaintFrame.jsx";
import { fetchArtUrl } from "@core/api.js";

export default function EndingPlate({ ending }) {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    if (ending?.gallery_key) fetchArtUrl(ending.gallery_key, "gallery").then((u) => live && setUrl(u || "")).catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [ending?.gallery_key]);
  return (
    <PaintFrame size="tile" className="finale__plate">
      {url && <img className="paint__img is-loaded" src={url} alt="" />}
    </PaintFrame>
  );
}
```

(until T15 the frame draws its own empty plate, as every missing picture does; `fetchArtUrl` resolves to the `url` string, `""` when nothing resolves). `index.jsx` exports `EndingArt: EndingPlate`. If the layout check finds the plugin's Ledger slot and the people panel colliding, the Sheet renders core's `PeopleStrip` itself instead (§8.5) and the manifest line is dropped.

- [ ] **Step 6: The layout check (§8).** `scripts/screenshot_runs.py`'s flagship runs gain `"barter": (("intent", "travel", "edgewood_square"),)` (the square holds vendors). `ui/tools/layout-check.mjs` gains:

```js
/** Overlays opened and probed per story run (v0.25.0 §8): each by its key, gated like a dialog. */
export const OVERLAY_PROBES = { "clockwork-dark": ["i", "b", "n", "e"] };

async function overlayScreens(page, opts, tag, size) {
  const failures = [];
  for (const k of OVERLAY_PROBES[opts.story] || []) {
    await page.keyboard.press(k);
    const dialog = await page.waitForSelector("[role=dialog]", { timeout: 5000 }).catch(() => null);
    if (!dialog) {
      failures.push(`overlay ${k}: no dialog opened`);
      continue;
    }
    const clipped = await page.evaluate(clippedControlsIn, "[role=dialog]");
    if (clipped.length) failures.push(`overlay ${k}: ${clipped.length} control(s) cut off: ${clipped.join(", ")}`);
    await page.screenshot({ path: join(opts.out, `${opts.story}-${tag}-${size.name}-overlay-${k}${opts.suffix}.png`) });
    await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
  }
  return failures;
}
```

called from `probe` after the main probe for every run, its failures added to that probe's (`clippedControlsIn` is `layout-probe.js`'s existing export; pass it the selector its signature takes). Start the server as a background task: `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\screenshot_runs.py --serve clockwork-dark --root <SCRATCH>\layout --exit-with-parent` with `CLOCKWORK_DATA_DIR=<SCRATCH>\layout\data`; then `npm run layout-check --prefix ui -- --story clockwork-dark --url http://127.0.0.1:<port> --runs <SCRATCH>\layout\runs.json --out <SCRATCH>\layout\out`; stop the server with `TaskStop`. Expected: exit 0 at all four sizes for `play`, `encounter` and `barter`.

```python
def test_every_flagship_place_has_its_own_base_and_every_person_a_portrait() -> None:
    from engine.game.locations import LOCATIONS
    from engine.media.providers.base import ImageRequest
    from engine.media.providers.shipped import load_manifest, lookup

        for npc_id in npc_sim.load_npc_schedules()["npcs"]:

                    "empty_table", "eight_shadows", "cold_square", "long_dusk"):
            assert lookup(ImageRequest(subject_id=key, kind="gallery")), key
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
```
